```rs
            self.ptr_modules = list.clone();
            self.ptr_modules_loaded = !list.is_empty();
            list.iter().map(|m| (m.name.clone(), m.base)).collect()
        } else {
            self.ptr_modules
                .iter()
                .map(|m| (m.name.clone(), m.base))
                .collect()
        };
        let Some(table) = self.ct_table.clone() else {
            self.toast("还没有打开任何表格。", ToastKind::Warn);
            return;
        };
        let is64 = handle.is_64bit;
        let mut imported = 0usize;
        let mut failed = 0usize;
        for entry in &table.entries {
            let reader = |address: u64| handle.read_pointer(address);
            match ctable::resolve_address(&entry.address, &modules, is64, &reader) {
                Ok(address) => {
                    if self.entries.iter().any(|e| e.address == address) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    let id = self.next_entry_id;
                    self.next_entry_id += 1;
                    let desc = if entry.description.is_empty() {
                        format!("CT {:X}", address)
                    } else {
                        entry.description.clone()
                    };
                    self.entries.push(Entry {
                        id,
                        desc,
                        address,
                        vtype: entry.vtype,
                        active: false,
                        value_text: entry.value.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| "-".to_string()),
                        current: None,
                        previous: None,
                    });
                    imported += 1;
                }
                Err(_) => failed += 1,
            }
        }
        self.ct_status = format!("导入 {imported} 条，{failed} 条解析失败");
        self.toast(
            format!("从表格导入 {imported} 条地址"),
            if imported > 0 {
                ToastKind::Success
            } else {
                ToastKind::Warn
            },
        );
        if imported > 0 {
            self.tab = Tab::List;
        }
    }

    /// Export the address list as a CT table in memory.
    pub fn export_entries_to_ct(&mut self) {
        let entries: Vec<ctable::CtEntry> = self
            .entries
            .iter()
            .map(|e| ctable::CtEntry {
                description: e.desc.clone(),
                vtype: e.vtype,
                address: format!("{:X}", e.address),
                value: if e.value_text == "-" {
                    None
                } else {
                    Some(e.value_text.clone())
                },
                active: e.active,
            })
            .collect();
        let count = entries.len();
        self.ct_table = Some(ctable::CtTable {
            entries,
            raw: String::new(),
        });
        self.ct_status = format!("已从地址列表生成 {count} 条");
        self.toast(format!("已生成 {count} 条表格条目"), ToastKind::Success);
    }

    pub fn assemble_source(&mut self) {
        let origin = if self.asm_origin.trim().is_empty() {
            0u64
        } else {
            match parse_address(&self.asm_origin) {
                Some(value) => value,
                None => {
                    self.asm_error = Some("起始地址格式不对。".to_string());
                    return;
                }
            }
        };
        match assembler::assemble(&self.asm_source, origin) {
            Ok(result) => {
                let count = result.bytes.len();
                let labels = result.labels.len();
                self.asm_error = None;
                self.asm_result = Some(result);
                self.toast(
                    format!("汇编完成：{count} 字节，{labels} 个标签"),
                    ToastKind::Success,
                );
            }
            Err(e) => {
                self.asm_result = None;
                self.asm_error = Some(e.clone());
                self.toast(e, ToastKind::Error);
            }
        }
    }

    /// Send the assembled bytes to the patch page, ready to apply.
    pub fn send_asm_to_patch(&mut self) {
        let Some(result) = self.asm_result.clone() else {
            self.toast("还没有汇编结果。", ToastKind::Warn);
            return;
        };
        let bytes = result
            .bytes
            .iter()
            .map(|b| format!("{b:02X}"))
            .collect::<Vec<_>>()
            .join(" ");
        self.inj_addr = format!("{:X}", result.origin);
        self.inj_bytes = bytes;
        self.tab = Tab::Inject;
        self.toast("已填入代码补丁页，确认后可写入。", ToastKind::Info);
    }

    /// Parse a "48 8B 05" style byte string into raw bytes.
    fn parse_patch_bytes(text: &str) -> Result<Vec<u8>, String> {
        let mut out = Vec::new();
        for token in text.split_whitespace() {
            let t = token.trim_start_matches("0x").trim_start_matches("0X");
            if t.len() != 2 || !t.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_hexdigit()) {
                return Err(format!("「{token}」不是有效字节，请用两位十六进制。"));
            }
            out.push(u8::from_str_radix(t, 16).unwrap_or(0));
        }
        if out.is_empty() {
            return Err("请先输入要写入的字节。".to_string());
        }
        Ok(out)
    }

    pub fn allocate_cave(&mut self) {
        let Some(handle) = self.process.clone() else {
            self.inj_error = Some("先连接一个进程。".to_string());
            return;
        };
        let size = self
            .inj_cave_size
            .trim()
            .parse::<usize>()
            .unwrap_or(4096)
            .clamp(0x1000, 0x100_0000);
        match inj::create_cave(&handle, size) {
            Ok(cave) => {
                self.toast(
                    format!("已分配代码洞 {:X}（{} 字节）", cave.address, cave.size),
                    ToastKind::Success,
                );
                self.inj_caves.push(cave);
                self.inj_selected_cave = self.inj_caves.len() - 1;
                self.inj_error = None;
            }
            Err(e) => {
                self.inj_error = Some(e.clone());
                self.toast(e, ToastKind::Error);
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn free_selected_cave(&mut self) {
        let Some(handle) = self.process.clone() else {
            return;
        };
        let Some(cave) = self.inj_caves.get(self.inj_selected_cave).cloned() else {
            return;
        };
        match inj::destroy_cave(&handle, &cave) {
            Ok(()) => {
                self.inj_caves.remove(self.inj_selected_cave);
                if self.inj_selected_cave >= self.inj_caves.len() && self.inj_selected_cave > 0 {
                    self.inj_selected_cave -= 1;
                }
                self.toast(format!("已释放代码洞 {:X}", cave.address), ToastKind::Info);
            }
            Err(e) => self.toast(e, ToastKind::Error),
        }
    }

    pub fn apply_patch(&mut self) {
        let Some(handle) = self.process.clone() else {
            self.inj_error = Some("先连接一个进程。".to_string());
            return;
        };
        let address = match parse_address(&self.inj_addr) {
            Some(value) => value,
            None => {
                self.inj_error = Some("目标地址格式不对。".to_string());
                return;
            }
        };
        let bytes = match Self::parse_patch_bytes(&self.inj_bytes) {
            Ok(value) => value,
            Err(e) => {
                self.inj_error = Some(e);
                return;
            }
        };
        let label = format!("{:X}", address);
        match inj::apply(&handle, address, &bytes, &label) {
            Ok(patch) => {
                self.toast(
                    format!("已写入 {} 字节到 {:X}", patch.size(), address),
                    ToastKind::Success,
                );
                self.inj_patches.push(patch);
                self.inj_error = None;
                self.save_workspace();
            }
            Err(e) => {
                self.inj_error = Some(e.clone());
                self.toast(e, ToastKind::Error);
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn revert_patch(&mut self, index: usize) {
        let Some(handle) = self.process.clone() else {
            return;
        };
        let Some(patch) = self.inj_patches.get_mut(index) else {
            return;
        };
        match inj::revert(&handle, patch) {
            Ok(()) => {
                let address = patch.address;
                self.toast(format!("已还原 {:X} 处的原始字节", address), ToastKind::Info);
                self.save_workspace();
            }
            Err(e) => self.toast(e, ToastKind::Error),
        }
    }

    pub fn revert_all_patches(&mut self) {
        let Some(handle) = self.process.clone() else {
            return;
        };
        let mut done = 0usize;
        let mut failed = 0usize;
        for patch in self.inj_patches.iter_mut() {
            if !patch.active {
                continue;
            }
            match inj::revert(&handle, patch) {
                Ok(()) => done += 1,
                Err(_) => failed += 1,
            }
        }
        if failed == 0 {
            self.toast(format!("已还原 {done} 个补丁"), ToastKind::Success);
        } else {
            self.toast(
                format!("还原 {done} 个，{failed} 个失败"),
                ToastKind::Error,
            );
        }
    }

    /// Read a code window and decode it into a listing.
    pub fn load_disassembly(&mut self) {
        let Some(handle) = self.process.clone() else {
            self.dis_error = Some("先连接一个进程。".to_string());
            return;
        };
        let address = match parse_address(&self.dis_addr) {
            Some(value) => value,
            None => {
                self.dis_error = Some("地址格式不对，试试 0x 开头或纯十六进制。".to_string());
                return;
            }
        };
        let mut buffer = vec![0u8; 512];
        if !handle.read(address, &mut buffer) {
            self.dis_error = Some(format!("无法读取 {address:X} 处的内存。"));
            self.dis_loaded = false;
            return;
        }
        self.dis_insns = disasm::disassemble(&buffer, address, handle.is_64bit, 96);
        self.dis_base = address;
        self.dis_bytes = buffer;
        self.dis_loaded = true;
        self.dis_error = None;
        self.toast(
            format!("已反汇编 {} 条指令", self.dis_insns.len()),
            ToastKind::Success,
        );
    }

    pub fn jump_to_disasm(&mut self, address: u64) {
        self.dis_addr = format!("{address:X}");
        self.tab = Tab::Disasm;
        self.load_disassembly();
    }

    pub fn open_memory_at(&mut self, address: u64) {
        self.mem_addr = format!("{:X}", address);
        self.mem_base = address;
        self.mem_loaded = false;
        self.mem_error = None;
        self.tab = Tab::Memory;
    }

}

/// Where the workspace lives: next to the executable, so a portable copy of
/// Zero carries its own state.
fn workspace_path() -> String {
    std::env::current_exe()
        .ok()
        .and_then(|exe| exe.parent().map(|dir| dir.join("zero-workspace.json")))
        .map(|path| path.to_string_lossy().to_string())
        .unwrap_or_else(|| "zero-workspace.json".to_string())
}

fn vtype_tag(vtype: VType) -> &'static str {
    match vtype {
        VType::Auto => "auto",
        VType::I8 => "i8",
        VType::I16 => "i16",
        VType::I32 => "i32",
        VType::I64 => "i64",
        VType::F32 => "f32",
        VType::F64 => "f64",
        VType::Str => "str",
    }
}

fn tab_tag(tab: Tab) -> &'static str {
    match tab {
        Tab::Scan => "scan",
        Tab::List => "list",
        Tab::Memory => "memory",
        Tab::Assistant => "assistant",
        Tab::Practice => "practice",
        Tab::PtrScan => "ptr",
        Tab::Aob => "aob",
        Tab::Symbols => "symbols",
        Tab::Disasm => "disasm",
        Tab::Inject => "inject",
        Tab::Asm => "asm",
        Tab::Table => "table",
        Tab::Speed => "speed",
        Tab::Script => "script",
    }
}

fn scan_config(app: &ZeroApp) -> ScanConfig {
    ScanConfig {
        vtype: app.vtype,
        scan_type: app.scan_type,
        value1: app.scan_value.clone(),
        value2: app.scan_value2.clone(),
        fast: app.fast,
        max_results: app.max_results,
    }
}

pub fn read_value(handle: &ProcessHandle, address: u64, vtype: VType) -> Option<Value> {
    if vtype == VType::Str {
        let mut buf = vec![0u8; 512];
        if !handle.read(address, &mut buf) {
            return None;
        }
        let end = buf.iter().position(|b| *b == 0).unwrap_or(buf.len());
        return Some(Value::Text(String::from_utf8_lossy(&buf[..end]).to_string()));
    }
    let mut buf = vec![0u8; vtype.size()];
    if !handle.read(address, &mut buf) {
        return None;
    }
    vtype.decode(&buf)
}

fn start_freeze_thread() -> Arc<Mutex<FreezeState>> {
    let state = Arc::new(Mutex::new(FreezeState {
        handle: None,
        specs: Vec::new(),
    }));
    let thread_state = state.clone();
    std::thread::Builder::new()
        .name("zero-freeze".to_string())
        .spawn(move || loop {
            std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(60));
            let guard = match thread_state.lock() {
                Ok(g) => g,
                Err(_) => continue,
            };
            if let Some(handle) = &guard.handle {
                for spec in &guard.specs {
                    let _ = handle.write(spec.address, &spec.bytes);
                }
            }
        })
        .ok();
    state
}

fn save_png(path: &std::path::Path, image: &egui::ColorImage) -> Result<(), String> {
    if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {
        std::fs::create_dir_all(parent).map_err(|e| format!("无法创建目录：{e}"))?;
    }
    let [w, h] = image.size;
    let mut rgba = Vec::with_capacity(w * h * 4);
    for pixel in &image.pixels {
        rgba.extend_from_slice(&pixel.to_array());
    }
    image::save_buffer(
        path,
        &rgba,
        w as u32,
        h as u32,
        image::ColorType::Rgba8,
    )
    .map_err(|e| format!("无法保存 PNG：{e}"))
}

impl eframe::App for ZeroApp {
    fn update(&mut self, ctx: &egui::Context, _frame: &mut eframe::Frame) {        self.handle_screenshot(ctx);
        self.poll_scan(ctx);
        self.poll_ptr_scan(ctx);
        self.poll_aob_scan(ctx);
        self.refresh_entries(ctx);
        self.tick_practice(ctx);
        self.tick_speed();
        self.tick_watch(ctx);

        self.handle_hotkey(ctx);

        if ctx.input(|i| i.key_pressed(egui::Key::Escape)) {
            self.picker_open = false;
            self.show_add = false;
            self.show_settings = false;
            self.editing = None;
        }

        self.ui_topbar(ctx);
        self.ui_sidebar(ctx);
        self.ui_statusbar(ctx);
        egui::CentralPanel::default()
            .frame(egui::Frame::none().fill(theme::BG))
            .show(ctx, |ui| {
                theme::draw_background(ui);
                let scroll_page = matches!(
                    self.tab,
                    Tab::Memory
                        | Tab::Assistant
                        | Tab::Practice
                        | Tab::PtrScan
                        | Tab::Aob
                        | Tab::Symbols
                        | Tab::Disasm
                        | Tab::Inject
                        | Tab::Asm
                        | Tab::Table
                        | Tab::Speed
                        | Tab::Script
                );
                if scroll_page {
                    egui::ScrollArea::vertical()
                        .auto_shrink([false, false])
                        .show(ui, |ui| {
                            egui::Frame::none()
                                .inner_margin(egui::Margin {
                                    left: 18.0,
                                    right: 20.0,
                                    top: 16.0,
                                    bottom: 22.0,
                                })
                                .show(ui, |ui| {
                                    ui.set_width(ui.available_width());
                                    self.ui_page(ui);
                                });
                        });
                } else {
                    egui::Frame::none()
                        .inner_margin(egui::Margin {
                            left: 18.0,
                            right: 20.0,
                            top: 12.0,
                            bottom: 8.0,
                        })
                        .show(ui, |ui| {
                            ui.set_width(ui.available_width());
                            self.ui_page(ui);
                        });
                }
            });

        self.ui_hotkeys(ctx);
        self.e2e_panel(ctx);
        self.ui_toasts(ctx);
        self.ui_modals(ctx);

        if !self.toasts.is_empty() {
            ctx.request_repaint_after(Duration::from_millis(120));
        }
        if self.practice_pending.is_some() {
            ctx.request_repaint_after(Duration::from_millis(250));
        }
    }

    fn on_exit(&mut self, _gl: Option<&eframe::glow::Context>) {
        // Persist before anything else: the address list and the record of
        // applied patches are the two things a user cannot reconstruct.
        self.save_workspace();
        if let Some(mut child) = self.practice_child.take() {
            let _ = child.kill();
        }
    }
}

```

`app/src/assembler.rs`:

```rs
//! A small x86-64 assembler.
//!
//! This is the reverse of disasm.rs: it turns assembly text into machine code.
//! It covers the instruction forms that show up in hand-written Cheat Engine
//! style patches and code-cave stubs, and supports labels so a jump can name its
//! destination instead of hard-coding an offset.
//!
//! Unsupported syntax is reported as an error naming the line, never emitted as
//! something else: a silently wrong byte would corrupt the target.

/// Result of assembling a source string.
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct AsmResult {
    pub bytes: Vec<u8>,
    pub origin: u64,
    /// Labels and the address each one resolved to, in declaration order.
    pub labels: Vec<(String, u64)>,
}

impl AsmResult {
    pub fn hex(&self) -> String {
        let mut text = self
            .bytes
            .iter()
            .map(|b| format!("{b:02X}"))
            .collect::<Vec<_>>()
            .join(" ");
        text
    }

    pub fn label_address(&self, name: &str) -> Option<u64> {
        self.labels
            .iter()
            .find(|(n, _)| n.eq_ignore_ascii_case(name))
            .map(|(_, a)| *a)
    }
}

const REG8: [&str; 16] = [
    "al", "cl", "dl", "bl", "spl", "bpl", "sil", "dil", "r8b", "r9b", "r10b", "r11b",
    "r12b", "r13b", "r14b", "r15b",
];
const REG16: [&str; 16] = [
    "ax", "cx", "dx", "bx", "sp", "bp", "si", "di", "r8w", "r9w", "r10w", "r11w",
    "r12w", "r13w", "r14w", "r15w",
];
const REG32: [&str; 16] = [
    "eax", "ecx", "edx", "ebx", "esp", "ebp", "esi", "edi", "r8d", "r9d", "r10d",
    "r11d", "r12d", "r13d", "r14d", "r15d",
];
const REG64: [&str; 16] = [
    "rax", "rcx", "rdx", "rbx", "rsp", "rbp", "rsi", "rdi", "r8", "r9", "r10", "r11",
    "r12", "r13", "r14", "r15",
];

/// Look a register name up, returning its encoding index and operand width.
fn parse_register(name: &str) -> Option<(u8, u8)> {
    let lower = name.trim().to_ascii_lowercase();
    for (index, reg) in REG8.iter().enumerate() {
        if *reg == lower {
            return Some((index as u8, 1));
        }
    }
    for (index, reg) in REG16.iter().enumerate() {
        if *reg == lower {
            return Some((index as u8, 2));
        }
    }
    for (index, reg) in REG32.iter().enumerate() {
        if *reg == lower {
            return Some((index as u8, 4));
        }
    }
    for (index, reg) in REG64.iter().enumerate() {
        if *reg == lower {
            return Some((index as u8, 8));
        }
    }
    None
}

/// A memory operand: [base + index*scale + disp].
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default)]
pub struct Mem {
    pub base: Option<u8>,
    pub index: Option<(u8, u8)>,
    pub disp: i64,
    /// True when the base was written as rip.
    pub rip: bool,
    /// True when the operand was a bare [0x1234] absolute address.
    pub absolute: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub enum Operand {
    Reg(u8, u8),
    Imm(i64),
    Mem(Mem, Option<u8>),
    Label(String),
}

/// Parse a number written as decimal, 0x-hex, or with a leading minus.
fn parse_number(text: &str) -> Option<i64> {
    let t = text.trim();
    let negative = t.starts_with('-');
    let t = t.trim_start_matches('-').trim_start_matches('+');
    let lower = t.to_ascii_lowercase();
    let value = if let Some(hex) = lower.strip_prefix("0x") {
        i64::from_str_radix(hex, 16).ok()?
    } else if let Some(bin) = lower.strip_prefix("0b") {
        i64::from_str_radix(bin, 2).ok()?
    } else {
        t.parse::<i64>().ok()?
    };
    Some(if negative { -value } else { value })
}

/// Parse [base + index*scale + disp] and the optional size prefix before it.
fn parse_memory(text: &str) -> Result<(Mem, Option<u8>), String> {
    let trimmed = text.trim();
    let lower = trimmed.to_ascii_lowercase();
    let mut size: Option<u8> = None;
    let mut rest = lower.as_str();
    for (prefix, width) in [
        ("byte ptr ", 1u8),
        ("word ptr ", 2),
        ("dword ptr ", 4),
        ("qword ptr ", 8),
        ("xmmword ptr ", 16),
    ] {
        if let Some(tail) = rest.strip_prefix(prefix) {
            size = Some(width);
            rest = tail;
            break;
        }
    }
    let rest = rest.trim();
    let Some(inner) = rest.strip_prefix('[').and_then(|s| s.strip_suffix(']')) else {
        return Err(format!("{trimmed} 不是有效的内存操作数（需要方括号）。"));
    };
    let mut mem = Mem::default();
    let normalised = inner.replace('-', "+- ");
    for raw in normalised.split('+') {
        let term = raw.trim();
        if term.is_empty() {
            continue;
        }
        let negative = term.starts_with('-');
        let term_body = term.trim_start_matches('-').trim();
        if let Some((reg_text, scale_text)) = term_body.split_once('*') {
            let Some((index, _)) = parse_register(reg_text.trim()) else {
                return Err(format!("索引寄存器「{reg_text}」不认识。"));
            };
            let scale = parse_number(scale_text.trim()).unwrap_or(1);
            if !matches!(scale, 1 | 2 | 4 | 8) {
                return Err(format!("缩放只能是 1/2/4/8，收到 {scale}。"));
            }
            mem.index = Some((index, scale as u8));
            continue;
        }
        if let Some((reg, _width)) = parse_register(term_body) {
            if negative {
                return Err("基址寄存器前不能有负号。".to_string());
            }
            if term_body.trim() == "rip" {
                mem.rip = true;
            } else {
                mem.base = Some(reg);
            }
            continue;
        }
        if let Some(value) = parse_number(term_body) {
            mem.disp += if negative { -value } else { value };
            continue;
        }
        if term_body.trim() == "rip" {
            mem.rip = true;
            continue;
        }
        return Err(format!("内存操作数里的「{term_body}」无法解析。"));
    }
    if mem.base.is_none() && mem.index.is_none() && !mem.rip {
        mem.absolute = true;
    }
    Ok((mem, size))
}

fn parse_operand(text: &str) -> Result<Operand, String> {
    let trimmed = text.trim();
    if trimmed.is_empty() {
        return Err("空的逗号：操作数之间多写了一个逗号。".to_string());
    }
    let lower = trimmed.to_ascii_lowercase();
    if lower.contains('[') || lower.ends_with("ptr") {
        let (mem, size) = parse_memory(trimmed)?;
        return Ok(Operand::Mem(mem, size));
    }
    if let Some((index, width)) = parse_register(&lower) {
        return Ok(Operand::Reg(index, width));
    }
    if let Some(value) = parse_number(&lower) {
        return Ok(Operand::Imm(value));
    }
    // Anything else must be a label reference.
    if lower.chars().next().map(|c| c.is_alphabetic() || c == '_').unwrap_or(false) {
        return Ok(Operand::Label(trimmed.to_string()));
    }
    Err(format!("操作数「{trimmed}」无法解析。"))
}

/// An encoded r/m operand: the REX bits it needs plus the ModRM/SIB/displacement
/// tail. The caller emits the REX prefix before the opcode, then this tail.
struct RmEncoding {
    rex: u8,
    tail: Vec<u8>,
}

/// Encode the r/m side of an instruction. reg_field goes in ModRM.reg.
fn encode_rm(operand: &Operand, reg_field: u8, size: u8) -> Result<RmEncoding, String> {
    let mut rex = 0x40 | (if reg_field >= 8 { 0x04 } else { 0 });
    let mut tail: Vec<u8> = Vec::new();
    match operand {
        Operand::Reg(index, _) => {
            if *index >= 8 {
                rex |= 0x01;
            }
            if size == 8 {
                rex |= 0x08;
            }
            tail.push(0xC0 | ((reg_field & 7) << 3) | (index & 7));
        }
        Operand::Mem(mem, _) => {
            let base = mem.base.map(|b| (b & 7, b >= 8));
            let index = mem.index.map(|(i, scale)| (i & 7, i >= 8, scale));
            // RIP-relative and bare absolute addresses both use mod=00 rm=101.
            let rip = mem.rip || (mem.base.is_none() && mem.index.is_none());
            let (modrm, sib, disp_bytes) = if rip {
                (
                    ((reg_field & 7) << 3) | 0x05,
                    None,
                    (mem.disp as i32).to_le_bytes().to_vec(),
                )
            } else {
                let (base_low, base_high) = base.unwrap_or((5, false));
                if base_high {
                    rex |= 0x01;
                }
                let disp = mem.disp;
                // ModRM mod field: 0 = no displacement, 1 = disp8, 2 = disp32.
                // (0b00/0b01/0b10; the shift into place happens once, below.)
                let mode: u8 = if disp == 0 && base_low != 5 {
                    0
                } else if (-128..=127).contains(&disp) {
                    1
                } else {
                    2
                };
                let mut disp_bytes: Vec<u8> = Vec::new();
                match mode {
                    1 => disp_bytes.push(disp as i8 as u8),
                    2 => disp_bytes.extend_from_slice(&(disp as i32).to_le_bytes()),
                    _ => {}
                }
                let mut sib: Option<u8> = None;
                let modrm_rm = if let Some((index_low, index_high, scale)) = index {
                    if index_high {
                        rex |= 0x02;
                    }
                    let scale_bits = match scale {
                        2 => 1u8,
                        4 => 2,
                        8 => 3,
                        _ => 0,
                    };
                    sib = Some((scale_bits << 6) | (index_low << 3) | base_low);
                    0x04
                } else if base_low == 4 {
                    // rsp/r12 as base always needs a SIB byte with no index.
                    sib = Some(base_low);
                    0x04
                } else {
                    base_low
                };
                (
                    (mode << 6) | ((reg_field & 7) << 3) | modrm_rm,
                    sib,
                    disp_bytes,
                )
            };
            if size == 8 {
                rex |= 0x08;
            }
            tail.push(modrm);
            if let Some(sib_byte) = sib {
                tail.push(sib_byte);
            }
            tail.extend_from_slice(&disp_bytes);
        }
        _ => {
            return Err("这里需要寄存器或内存操作数，不能是立即数或标签。".to_string());
        }
    }
    Ok(RmEncoding { rex, tail })
}

/// Emit a REX prefix only when one of its bits is actually used.
fn push_rex(out: &mut Vec<u8>, rex: u8) {
    if rex != 0x40 {
        out.push(rex);
    }
}

fn push_imm(out: &mut Vec<u8>, value: i64, width: usize) {
    match width {
        1 => out.push(value as i8 as u8),
        2 => out.extend_from_slice(&(value as i16).to_le_bytes()),
        8 => out.extend_from_slice(&value.to_le_bytes()),
        _ => out.extend_from_slice(&(value as i32).to_le_bytes()),
    }
}

/// Immediate width for a full-size operand (imm32 unless 16-bit).
fn imm_width(size: u8) -> usize {
    if size == 2 {
        2
    } else {
        4
    }
}

/// How an instruction's operands must look, so errors are caught before encoding.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
enum Form {
    /// No operands.
    None,
    /// A single register or memory operand.
    OneRm,
    /// r/m destination, register source.
    RmReg,
    /// register destination, r/m source.
    RegRm,
    /// register/memory, immediate.
    RmImm,
    /// register, immediate.
    RegImm,
    /// register, register.
    RegReg,
    /// a single immediate.
    Imm,
    /// a relative target (label or absolute address).
    Rel,
}

/// One supported mnemonic and the opcode bytes that start its 8/32-bit forms.
struct Spec {
    name: &'static str,
    form: Form,
    /// Opcode when the source/destination is a byte (None: not available).
    op8: Option<u8>,
    /// Opcode for the full-width form.
    op: u8,
    /// 0x0F-prefixed opcode (the value is the second byte).
    twobyte: bool,
}

const fn spec(name: &'static str, form: Form, op8: Option<u8>, op: u8) -> Spec {
    Spec { name, form, op8, op, twobyte: false }
}

const fn spec2(name: &'static str, form: Form, op: u8) -> Spec {
    Spec { name, form, op8: None, op, twobyte: true }
}

static SPECS: &[Spec] = &[
    spec("mov", Form::RmReg, Some(0x88), 0x89),
    spec("mov", Form::RegRm, Some(0x8A), 0x8B),
    spec("mov", Form::RegImm, Some(0xB0), 0xB8),
    spec("mov", Form::RmImm, Some(0xC6), 0xC7),
    spec("add", Form::RmReg, Some(0x00), 0x01),
    spec("add", Form::RegRm, Some(0x02), 0x03),
    spec("add", Form::RmImm, Some(0x80), 0x81),
    spec("or", Form::RmReg, Some(0x08), 0x09),
    spec("or", Form::RegRm, Some(0x0A), 0x0B),
    spec("and", Form::RmReg, Some(0x20), 0x21),
    spec("and", Form::RegRm, Some(0x22), 0x23),
    spec("and", Form::RmImm, Some(0x80), 0x81),
    spec("sub", Form::RmReg, Some(0x28), 0x29),
    spec("sub", Form::RegRm, Some(0x2A), 0x2B),
    spec("sub", Form::RmImm, Some(0x80), 0x81),
    spec("xor", Form::RmReg, Some(0x30), 0x31),
    spec("xor", Form::RegRm, Some(0x32), 0x33),
    spec("xor", Form::RmImm, Some(0x80), 0x81),
    spec("cmp", Form::RmReg, Some(0x38), 0x39),
    spec("cmp", Form::RegRm, Some(0x3A), 0x3B),
    spec("cmp", Form::RmImm, Some(0x80), 0x81),
    spec("test", Form::RmReg, Some(0x84), 0x85),
    spec("test", Form::RmImm, Some(0xF6), 0xF7),
    spec("xchg", Form::RmReg, Some(0x86), 0x87),
    spec("lea", Form::RegRm, None, 0x8D),
    spec("movzx", Form::RegRm, None, 0xB6),
    spec("movsx", Form::RegRm, None, 0xBF),
    spec("imul", Form::RegRm, None, 0xAF),
    spec("not", Form::OneRm, Some(0xF6), 0xF7),
    spec("neg", Form::OneRm, Some(0xF6), 0xF7),
    spec("mul", Form::OneRm, Some(0xF6), 0xF7),
    spec("div", Form::OneRm, Some(0xF6), 0xF7),
    spec("inc", Form::OneRm, Some(0xFE), 0xFF),
    spec("dec", Form::OneRm, Some(0xFE), 0xFF),
    spec("push", Form::OneRm, None, 0xFF),
    spec("call", Form::OneRm, None, 0xFF),
    spec("jmp", Form::OneRm, None, 0xFF),
];

/// The /digit value in ModRM.reg for each group-encoded mnemonic.
fn group_digit(name: &str) -> u8 {
    match name {
        "test" => 0,
        "not" => 2,
        "neg" => 3,
        "mul" => 4,
        "div" => 6,
        "inc" => 0,
        "dec" => 1,
        "call" => 2,
        "jmp" => 4,
        "push" => 6,
        _ => 0,
    }
}

/// Conditional jumps, indexed by the low nibble of the 0x70 opcode.
const JCC: [(&str, u8); 16] = [
    ("jo", 0x0), ("jno", 0x1), ("jb", 0x2), ("jae", 0x3),
    ("je", 0x4), ("jne", 0x5), ("jbe", 0x6), ("ja", 0x7),
    ("js", 0x8), ("jns", 0x9), ("jp", 0xA), ("jnp", 0xB),
    ("jl", 0xC), ("jge", 0xD), ("jle", 0xE), ("jg", 0xF),
];

const SHIFTS: [(&str, u8); 8] = [
    ("rol", 0), ("ror", 1), ("rcl", 2), ("rcr", 3),
    ("shl", 4), ("shr", 5), ("sal", 4), ("sar", 7),
];

/// One parsed source line.
enum Line {
    Empty,
    Label(String),
    Insn { mnemonic: String, operands: Vec<String>, raw: String },
}

fn parse_line(raw: &str) -> Result<Line, String> {
    let mut text = raw.trim();
    if text.is_empty() || text.starts_with(';') || text.starts_with("//") {
        return Ok(Line::Empty);
    }
    // Strip a trailing comment, but only when it is not inside brackets.
    if let Some(pos) = text.find("//") {
        text = text[..pos].trim();
    } else if let Some(pos) = text.find(';') {
        text = text[..pos].trim();
    }
    if text.is_empty() {
        return Ok(Line::Empty);
    }
    if let Some(colon) = text.find(':') {
        let candidate = text[..colon].trim();
        let valid = !candidate.is_empty()
            && candidate
                .chars()
                .all(|c| c.is_alphanumeric() || c == '_' || c == '.');
        if valid && !candidate.contains(' ') {
            let rest = text[colon + 1..].trim();
            if rest.is_empty() {
                return Ok(Line::Label(candidate.to_string()));
            }
            // A label followed by an instruction on the same line.
            let (mnemonic, operands) = split_insn(rest)?;
            return Ok(Line::Insn {
                mnemonic,
                operands,
                raw: format!("{candidate}: {rest}"),
            });
        }
    }
    let (mnemonic, operands) = split_insn(text)?;
    Ok(Line::Insn {
        mnemonic,
        operands,
        raw: text.to_string(),
    })
}

fn split_insn(text: &str) -> Result<(String, Vec<String>), String> {
    let mut parts = text.splitn(2, char::is_whitespace);
    let mnemonic = parts.next().unwrap_or("").trim().to_ascii_lowercase();
    let rest = parts.next().unwrap_or("").trim();
    if mnemonic.is_empty() {
        return Err("空指令。".to_string());
    }
    let mut operands = Vec::new();
    if !rest.is_empty() {
        let mut depth = 0i32;
        let mut current = String::new();
        for ch in rest.chars() {
            match ch {
                '[' => {
                    depth += 1;
                    current.push(ch);
                }
                ']' => {
                    depth -= 1;
                    current.push(ch);
                }
                ',' if depth == 0 => {
                    operands.push(current.trim().to_string());
                    current = String::new();
                }
                _ => current.push(ch),
            }
        }
        operands.push(current.trim().to_string());
        if operands.iter().any(|o| o.is_empty()) {
            return Err(format!("「{text}」里有空的逗号。"));
        }
    }
    Ok((mnemonic, operands))
}

/// Assemble source into bytes starting at `origin`.
///
/// Two passes: the first sizes every instruction so labels get real addresses,
/// the second emits bytes with the jump displacements resolved.
pub fn assemble(source: &str, origin: u64) -> Result<AsmResult, String> {
    let mut lines: Vec<Line> = Vec::new();
    for (index, raw) in source.lines().enumerate() {
        match parse_line(raw) {
            Ok(line) => lines.push(line),
            Err(e) => return Err(format!("第 {} 行：{e}", index + 1)),
        }
    }
    // Pass 1: assign addresses to labels and size every instruction.
    let mut labels: Vec<(String, u64)> = Vec::new();
    let mut cursor = origin;
    let mut sizes: Vec<Option<usize>> = Vec::with_capacity(lines.len());
    for line in &lines {
        match line {
            Line::Empty => sizes.push(None),
            Line::Label(name) => {
                if labels.iter().any(|(n, _)| n.eq_ignore_ascii_case(name)) {
                    return Err(format!("标签「{name}」重复定义了。"));
                }
                labels.push((name.clone(), cursor));
                sizes.push(None);
            }
            Line::Insn { mnemonic, operands, raw } => {
                let size = encode_line(mnemonic, operands, cursor, &labels, true, raw)?.len();
                cursor += size as u64;
                sizes.push(Some(size));
            }
        }
    }
    // Pass 2: emit the real bytes now that every label has an address.
    let mut bytes: Vec<u8> = Vec::new();
    let mut cursor = origin;
    for (index, line) in lines.iter().enumerate() {
        if let Line::Insn { mnemonic, operands, raw } = line {
            let encoded = encode_line(mnemonic, operands, cursor, &labels, false, raw)?;
            if let Some(expected) = sizes[index] {
                if encoded.len() != expected {
                    return Err(format!("内部不一致：同一条指令两次长度不同（{raw}）。"));
                }
            }
            cursor += encoded.len() as u64;
            bytes.extend_from_slice(&encoded);
        }
    }
    Ok(AsmResult {
        bytes,
        origin,
        labels,
    })
}

/// Encode one instruction. `sizing` is true during pass 1, where a label's
/// address may not exist yet and a placeholder zero displacement is used.
fn encode_line(
    mnemonic: &str,
    operand_texts: &[String],
    address: u64,
    labels: &[(String, u64)],
    sizing: bool,
    raw: &str,
) -> Result<Vec<u8>, String> {
    let mut out: Vec<u8> = Vec::new();
    let name = mnemonic.trim().to_ascii_lowercase();

    // Zero-operand instructions.
    let simple: Option<&[u8]> = match name.as_str() {
        "nop" => Some(&[0x90]),
        "ret" => Some(&[0xC3]),
        "int3" => Some(&[0xCC]),
        "leave" => Some(&[0xC9]),
        "pushfq" => Some(&[0x9C]),
        "popfq" => Some(&[0x9D]),
        "cdqe" => Some(&[0x48, 0x98]),
        "cqo" => Some(&[0x48, 0x99]),
        "cld" => Some(&[0xFC]),
        "std" => Some(&[0xFD]),
        "clc" => Some(&[0xF8]),
        "stc" => Some(&[0xF9]),
        "cli" => Some(&[0xFA]),
        "sti" => Some(&[0xFB]),
        "cpuid" => Some(&[0x0F, 0xA2]),
        "rdtsc" => Some(&[0x0F, 0x31]),
        "syscall" => Some(&[0x0F, 0x05]),
        "ud2" => Some(&[0x0F, 0x0B]),
        _ => None,
    };
    if let Some(bytes) = simple {
        if !operand_texts.is_empty() {
            return Err(format!("{name} 不接受操作数。"));
        }
        return Ok(bytes.to_vec());
    }

    let operands: Vec<Operand> = operand_texts
        .iter()
        .map(|t| parse_operand(t))
        .collect::<Result<_, _>>()?;

    // Relative branches.
    if let Some(opcode) = rel_opcode(&name) {
        if operands.len() != 1 {
            return Err(format!("{name} 需要一个跳转目标。"));
        }
        let target = resolve_target(&operands[0], labels, sizing)?;
        let mut bytes = vec![opcode];
        if opcode == 0xE8 || opcode == 0xE9 {
            let displacement = target as i128 - (address as i128 + 5);
            if displacement < i32::MIN as i128 || displacement > i32::MAX as i128 {
                return Err(format!("{raw}：跳转距离超出 rel32 范围。"));
            }
            bytes.extend_from_slice(&(displacement as i32).to_le_bytes());
        } else {
            let displacement = target as i128 - (address as i128 + 2);
            if displacement < i8::MIN as i128 || displacement > i8::MAX as i128 {
                return Err(format!("{raw}：短跳转超出 rel8 范围，请用 jmp 远的写法或调整布局。"));
            }
            bytes.push(displacement as i8 as u8);
        }
        return Ok(bytes);
    }

    // Shifts: shl/shr/sar/rol/ror with an 8-bit count.
    if let Some(digit) = SHIFTS.iter().find(|(n, _)| *n == name).map(|(_, d)| *d) {
        if operands.len() != 2 {
            return Err(format!("{name} 需要两个操作数（目标, 位数）。"));
        }
        let size = operand_size(&operands[0]).ok_or_else(|| format!("{raw}：无法确定操作数宽度。"))?;
        let opcode = if size == 1 { 0xC0 } else { 0xC1 };
        let count = match &operands[1] {
            Operand::Imm(v) => *v,
            _ => return Err(format!("{name} 的第二操作数必须是立即数（用 cl 的写法暂不支持）。")),
        };
        let rm = encode_rm(&operands[0], digit, if size == 2 { 4 } else { size })?;
        if size == 2 {
            out.push(0x66);
        }
        push_rex(&mut out, rm.rex);
        out.push(opcode);
        out.extend_from_slice(&rm.tail);
        push_imm(&mut out, count, 1);
        return Ok(out);
    }

    let specs: Vec<&Spec> = SPECS.iter().filter(|s| s.name == name).collect();
    if specs.is_empty() {
        return Err(format!("不支持的指令「{name}」。"));
    }
    for spec in &specs {
        if let Some(bytes) = try_encode(spec, &operands, address, labels, sizing) {
            return bytes;
        }
    }
    Err(format!("{raw}：操作数组合不支持。"))
}

fn rel_opcode(name: &str) -> Option<u8> {
    match name {
        "jmp" => Some(0xEB),
        "call" => Some(0xE8),
        _ => JCC
            .iter()
            .find(|(n, _)| *n == name)
            .map(|(_, nibble)| 0x70 | *nibble),
    }
}

fn resolve_target(
    operand: &Operand,
    labels: &[(String, u64)],
    sizing: bool,
) -> Result<u64, String> {
    match operand {
        Operand::Imm(value) => Ok(*value as u64),
        Operand::Label(name) => {
            if let Some((_, address)) = labels.iter().find(|(n, _)| n.eq_ignore_ascii_case(name)) {
                Ok(*address)
            } else if sizing {
                // Not yet known in pass 1; the target address does not change
                // the instruction length, so a placeholder is fine.
                Ok(0)
            } else {
                Err(format!("标签「{name}」没有定义。"))
            }
        }
        _ => Err("跳转目标必须是标签或地址。".to_string()),
    }
}

fn operand_size(operand: &Operand) -> Option<u8> {
    match operand {
        Operand::Reg(_, width) => Some(*width),
        Operand::Mem(_, hint) => *hint,
        _ => None,
    }
}

fn try_encode(
    spec: &Spec,
    operands: &[Operand],
    _address: u64,
    labels: &[(String, u64)],
    sizing: bool,
) -> Option<Result<Vec<u8>, String>> {
    let mut out: Vec<u8> = Vec::new();
    match spec.form {
        Form::RegImm => {
            let [Operand::Reg(index, width), Operand::Imm(value)] = operands else {
                return None;
            };
            let opcode = if *width == 1 {
                spec.op8? 
            } else {
                spec.op
            };
            let mut rex = 0x40;
            if *index >= 8 {
                rex |= 0x01;
            }
            if *width == 8 {
                rex |= 0x08;
            }
            if *width == 2 {
                out.push(0x66);
            }
            push_rex(&mut out, rex);
            out.push(opcode + (index & 7));
            let imm_width = if *width == 1 { 1 } else { imm_width(*width) };
            if *width == 8 && name_is_mov_imm(spec.name) {
                push_imm(&mut out, *value, 8);
            } else {
                push_imm(&mut out, *value, imm_width);
            }
            Some(Ok(out))
        }
        Form::RmReg => {
            // For "mov r/m, reg" the first operand is the destination (r/m) and
            // the second is the source register.
            let [dst, Operand::Reg(reg, width)] = operands else {
                return None;
            };
            let opcode = if *width == 1 { spec.op8? } else { spec.op };
            let rm = encode_rm(dst, *reg, *width).ok()?;
            if *width == 2 {
                out.push(0x66);
            }
            push_rex(&mut out, rm.rex);
            out.push(opcode);
            out.extend_from_slice(&rm.tail);
            Some(Ok(out))
        }
        Form::RegRm => {
            let [Operand::Reg(reg, width), src] = operands else {
                return None;
            };
            let opcode = if *width == 1 {
                spec.op8.unwrap_or(0x8A)
            } else {
                spec.op
            };
            let effective = if *width == 0 { 4 } else { *width };
            let rm = encode_rm(src, *reg, effective).ok()?;
            if effective == 2 {
                out.push(0x66);
            }
            push_rex(&mut out, rm.rex);
            if spec.twobyte {
                out.push(0x0F);
            }
            out.push(opcode);
            out.extend_from_slice(&rm.tail);
            Some(Ok(out))
        }
        Form::OneRm => {
            let [target] = operands else {
                return None;
            };
            let size = operand_size(target).unwrap_or(4);
            let opcode = if size == 1 { spec.op8? } else { spec.op };
            let digit = group_digit(spec.name);
            let rm = encode_rm(target, digit, if size == 2 { 4 } else { size }).ok()?;
            if size == 2 {
                out.push(0x66);
            }
            push_rex(&mut out, rm.rex);
            out.push(opcode);
            out.extend_from_slice(&rm.tail);
            Some(Ok(out))
        }
        Form::RmImm => {
            let [dst, Operand::Imm(value)] = operands else {
                return None;
            };
            let size = operand_size(dst)?;
            let opcode = if size == 1 {
                spec.op8? 
            } else {
                spec.op
            };
            let digit = group_digit(spec.name);
            // For test/not/neg/mul/div the mnemonic is selected by ModRM.reg.
            let reg_field = if matches!(
                spec.name,
                "test" | "not" | "neg" | "mul" | "div"
            ) {
                digit
            } else {
                0
            };
            let rm = encode_rm(dst, reg_field, if size == 2 { 4 } else { size }).ok()?;
            if size == 2 {
                out.push(0x66);
            }
            push_rex(&mut out, rm.rex);
            out.push(opcode);
            out.extend_from_slice(&rm.tail);
            push_imm(&mut out, *value, if size == 1 { 1 } else { imm_width(size) });
            Some(Ok(out))
        }
        Form::Rel => {
            let [target] = operands else {
                return None;
            };
            let address = resolve_target(target, labels, sizing).ok()?;
            out.push(spec.op);
            out.extend_from_slice(&(address as i32).to_le_bytes());
            Some(Ok(out))
        }
        _ => None,
    }
}

fn name_is_mov_imm(name: &str) -> bool {
    name == "mov"
}
```

`app/src/ctable.rs`:

```rs
//! Cheat Table (.ct) support.
//!
//! A .ct file is XML. The parts that matter for Zero are the cheat entries:
//! each one carries a description, a variable type, an address expression, and
//! optionally a frozen value. This module reads and writes those, keeping the
//! original document so that unrecognised sections are not destroyed on save.

use crate::model::VType;

/// One cheat entry from a table.
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct CtEntry {
    pub description: String,
    pub vtype: VType,
    /// Address as written in the file: a hex offset, a module expression, or
    /// a pointer chain such as [[game.exe+1A2B]+18]+30.
    pub address: String,
    /// The frozen value, when the entry has one.
    pub value: Option<String>,
    /// True when the entry was active in the file (the checkbox was ticked).
    pub active: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default)]
pub struct CtTable {
    pub entries: Vec<CtEntry>,
    /// The raw document, so saving can preserve unknown sections.
    pub raw: String,
}

impl CtTable {
    pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool {
        self.entries.is_empty()
    }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// A deliberate small XML reader. It only needs to walk well-formed CE tables,
// so it tracks elements and attributes and ignores everything else; it never
// tries to resolve namespaces or entities beyond the basic five.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct Element {
    pub name: String,
    pub attributes: Vec<(String, String)>,
    pub children: Vec<Element>,
    pub text: String,
}

impl Element {
    pub fn attr(&self, key: &str) -> Option<&str> {
        self.attributes
            .iter()
            .find(|(k, _)| k.eq_ignore_ascii_case(key))
            .map(|(_, v)| v.as_str())
    }

    pub fn child(&self, name: &str) -> Option<&Element> {
        self.children
            .iter()
            .find(|c| c.name.eq_ignore_ascii_case(name))
    }

    pub fn children_named<'a>(&'a self, name: &'a str) -> impl Iterator<Item = &'a Element> {
        self.children
            .iter()
            .filter(move |c| c.name.eq_ignore_ascii_case(name))
    }

    /// Trimmed text content of this element.
    pub fn text_trimmed(&self) -> String {
        self.text.trim().to_string()
    }
}

fn unescape(text: &str) -> String {
    let mut out = String::with_capacity(text.len());
    let mut rest = text;
    while let Some(pos) = rest.find('&') {
        out.push_str(&rest[..pos]);
        let tail = &rest[pos..];
        let Some(semi) = tail.find(';') else {
            out.push_str(tail);
            return out;
        };
        let entity = &tail[1..semi];
        let decoded = match entity {
            "lt" => Some('<'),
            "gt" => Some('>'),
            "amp" => Some('&'),
            "quot" => Some('"'),
            "apos" => Some('\''),
            _ => None,
        };
        match decoded {
            Some(ch) => out.push(ch),
            None => {
                if let Some(hex) = entity.strip_prefix("#x").or_else(|| entity.strip_prefix("#X")) {
                    if let Ok(code) = u32::from_str_radix(hex, 16) {
                        if let Some(ch) = char::from_u32(code) {
                            out.push(ch);
                        }
                    }
                } else if let Some(dec) = entity.strip_prefix('#') {
                    if let Ok(code) = dec.parse::<u32>() {
                        if let Some(ch) = char::from_u32(code) {
                            out.push(ch);
                        }
                    }
                } else {
                    out.push_str(&tail[..=semi]);
                }
            }
        }
        rest = &tail[semi + 1..];
    }
    out.push_str(rest);
    out
}

fn escape(text: &str) -> String {
    text.replace('&', "&amp;")
        .replace('<', "&lt;")
        .replace('>', "&gt;")
        .replace('"', "&quot;")
}

/// Parse an XML document into a tree. Comments, the declaration, and doctypes
/// are skipped; malformed markup returns an error naming the offset.
pub fn parse_xml(source: &str) -> Result<Element, String> {
    // Work on bytes throughout: the document is UTF-8 and markup is ASCII, so
    // every structural character sits on a byte boundary. Slicing by char index
    // here would cut multi-byte text (CJK descriptions) in half.
    let bytes = source.as_bytes();
    let mut pos = 0usize;
    let mut stack: Vec<Element> = vec![Element {
        name: "#document".to_string(),
        attributes: Vec::new(),
        children: Vec::new(),
        text: String::new(),
    }];
    while pos < bytes.len() {
        let ch = bytes[pos];
        if ch == b'<' {
            // Comments, declarations, processing instructions, doctypes.
            if source[pos..].starts_with("<!--") {
                match source[pos + 4..].find("-->") {
                    Some(end) => pos = pos + 4 + end + 3,
                    None => return Err("注释没有闭合。".to_string()),
                }
                continue;
            }
            if source[pos..].starts_with("<?") {
                match source[pos + 2..].find("?>") {
                    Some(end) => pos = pos + 2 + end + 2,
                    None => return Err("处理指令没有闭合。".to_string()),
                }
                continue;
            }
            if source[pos..].starts_with("<!") {
                match source[pos..].find('>') {
                    Some(end) => pos = pos + end + 1,
                    None => return Err("DOCTYPE 没有闭合。".to_string()),
                }
                continue;
            }
            if source[pos..].starts_with("</") {
                let Some(end) = source[pos..].find('>') else {
                    return Err("闭合标签没有结束。".to_string());
                };
                let name = source[pos + 2..pos + end].trim().to_string();
                if stack.len() < 2 {
                    return Err(format!("多余的闭合标签 </{name}>。"));
                }
                let element = stack.pop().unwrap();
                if !element.name.eq_ignore_ascii_case(&name) {
                    return Err(format!("标签不匹配：<{}> 与 </{name}>。", element.name));
                }
                stack.last_mut().unwrap().children.push(element);
                pos = pos + end + 1;
                continue;
            }
            // An opening tag.
            let Some(end) = source[pos..].find('>') else {
                return Err("开始标签没有结束。".to_string());
            };
            let inner = &source[pos + 1..pos + end];
            let self_closing = inner.ends_with('/');
            let inner = inner.trim_end_matches('/').trim();
            let mut parts = inner.splitn(2, char::is_whitespace);
            let name = parts.next().unwrap_or("").to_string();
            let mut attributes = Vec::new();
            if let Some(rest) = parts.next() {
                attributes = parse_attributes(rest);
            }
            let element = Element {
                name,
                attributes,
                children: Vec::new(),
                text: String::new(),
            };
            if self_closing {
                stack.last_mut().unwrap().children.push(element);
            } else {
                stack.push(element);
            }
            pos = pos + end + 1;
            continue;
        }
        // Character data.
        let start = pos;
        while pos < bytes.len() && bytes[pos] != b'<' {
            pos += 1;
        }
        let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&bytes[start..pos]).to_string();
        if let Some(current) = stack.last_mut() {
            current.text.push_str(&unescape(&text));
        }
    }
    if stack.len() != 1 {
        let open = stack.last().map(|e| e.name.clone()).unwrap_or_default();
        return Err(format!("标签没有闭合：<{open}>。"));
    }
    Ok(stack.pop().unwrap())
}


/// Map a CE VariableType name onto our value types.
fn vtype_from_ct(name: &str) -> VType {
    match name.trim().to_ascii_lowercase().as_str() {
        "4 bytes" | "dword" => VType::I32,
        "8 bytes" | "qword" => VType::I64,
        "2 bytes" | "word" => VType::I16,
        "byte" => VType::I8,
        "float" => VType::F32,
        "double" => VType::F64,
        "string" => VType::Str,
        _ => VType::I32,
    }
}

/// The name CE writes for each of our value types.
pub fn vtype_to_ct(vtype: VType) -> &'static str {
    match vtype {
        VType::I8 => "Byte",
        VType::I16 => "2 Bytes",
        VType::I32 | VType::Auto => "4 Bytes",
        VType::I64 => "8 Bytes",
        VType::F32 => "Float",
        VType::F64 => "Double",
        VType::Str => "String",
    }
}

/// Parse a .ct document.
pub fn parse(source: &str) -> Result<CtTable, String> {
    let root = parse_xml(source)?;
    let cheat_table = root
        .child("CheatTable")
        .ok_or_else(|| "这不是 Cheat Table：缺少 CheatTable 根元素。".to_string())?;
    let mut entries = Vec::new();
    collect_entries(cheat_table, &mut entries);
    Ok(CtTable {
        entries,
        raw: source.to_string(),
    })
}

/// Cheat entries may nest inside groups, so walk the whole tree.
fn collect_entries(element: &Element, out: &mut Vec<CtEntry>) {
    for child in &element.children {
        if child.name.eq_ignore_ascii_case("CheatEntry") {
            if let Some(entry) = entry_from_element(child) {
                out.push(entry);
            }
        }
        collect_entries(child, out);
    }
}

fn entry_from_element(element: &Element) -> Option<CtEntry> {
    // CE wraps descriptions in double quotes; strip exactly one pair so a
    // write/read round-trip does not accumulate them.
    let description = element
        .child("Description")
        .map(|e| {
            let text = e.text_trimmed();
            let inner = text
                .strip_prefix('"')
                .and_then(|t| t.strip_suffix('"'))
                .unwrap_or(&text);
            inner.to_string()
        })
        .unwrap_or_default();
    let vtype_name = element
        .child("VariableType")
        .map(|e| e.text_trimmed())
        .unwrap_or_default();
    let vtype = vtype_from_ct(&vtype_name);
    let address = element
        .child("Address")
        .map(|e| e.text_trimmed())
        .unwrap_or_default();
    let value = element
        .child("Value")
        .map(|e| e.text_trimmed())
        .filter(|v| !v.is_empty());
    // An entry is "active" when it has a frozen value.
    let active = value.is_some();
    if description.is_empty() && address.is_empty() {
        return None;
    }
    Some(CtEntry {
        description,
        vtype,
        address,
        value,
        active,
    })
}

/// Resolve a CE address expression against a module list.
///
/// Supported forms:
///   deadbeef                 absolute hex address
///   game.exe+1A2B            module base + offset
///   [game.exe+1A2B]+18       one dereference
///   [[game.exe+1A2B]+18]+30  nested pointer chain
///
/// `read` performs one pointer read and returns None when it fails, so an
/// unreadable chain reports a clear error instead of a bogus address.
pub fn resolve_address(
    expression: &str,
    modules: &[(String, u64)],
    is_64bit: bool,
    read_pointer: &dyn Fn(u64) -> Option<u64>,
) -> Result<u64, String> {
    let expression = expression.trim();
    if expression.is_empty() {
        return Err("地址为空。".to_string());
    }
    let mut depth = 0usize;
    if expression.starts_with('[') {
        // Find the bracket that closes the outermost one.
        let mut close = None;
        for (index, ch) in expression.char_indices() {
            match ch {
                '[' => depth += 1,
                ']' => {
                    depth -= 1;
                    if depth == 0 {
                        close = Some(index);
                        break;
                    }
                }
                _ => {}
            }
        }
        let Some(close) = close else {
            return Err(format!("方括号没有闭合：{expression}"));
        };
        // Inside may itself be a chain, so resolve it recursively first.
        let inner = &expression[1..close];
        let tail = &expression[close + 1..];
        let base = resolve_address(inner, modules, is_64bit, read_pointer)?;
        let Some(pointer) = read_pointer(base) else {
            return Err(format!("无法读取 {base:X} 处的指针。"));
        };
        if pointer == 0 {
            return Err(format!(
                "{base:X} 处的指针是空的（进程可能还没到那一步）。"
            ));
        }
        if !is_64bit && pointer > 0xFFFF_FFFF {
            return Err(format!("32 位进程里读到了越界的指针 {pointer:X}。"));
        }
        return apply_offsets(pointer, tail);
    }
    resolve_base(expression, modules)
}

/// A trailing "+18-4" run of signed offsets.
fn apply_offsets(mut address: u64, tail: &str) -> Result<u64, String> {
    let normalised = tail.trim().replace('-', "+-");
    for term in normalised.split('+') {
        let term = term.trim();
        if term.is_empty() {
            continue;
        }
        let negative = term.starts_with('-');
        let body = term.trim_start_matches('-').trim();
        if body.is_empty() {
            continue;
        }
        let value = parse_hex_or_dec(body).ok_or_else(|| format!("偏移「{body}」无法解析。"))?;
        address = if negative {
            address.wrapping_sub(value)
        } else {
            address.wrapping_add(value)
        };
    }
    Ok(address)
}

/// Resolve "module.exe+OFFSET" or a bare hex address.
fn resolve_base(expression: &str, modules: &[(String, u64)]) -> Result<u64, String> {
    let text = expression.trim();
    // A module name may itself contain dots; match it as a prefix.
    for (name, base) in modules {
        if text.len() < name.len() {
            continue;
        }
        let (head, tail) = text.split_at(name.len());
        if !head.eq_ignore_ascii_case(name) {
            continue;
        }
        if tail.is_empty() {
            return Ok(*base);
        }
        if tail.starts_with('+') || tail.starts_with('-') {
            return apply_offsets(*base, tail);
        }
    }
    if let Some(value) = parse_hex_or_dec(text) {
        return Ok(value);
    }
    // A bare number followed by offsets, e.g. 7FF6A1B2+18.
    if let Some(pos) = text[1..].find(['+', '-']).map(|p| p + 1) {
        let (head, tail) = text.split_at(pos);
        if let Some(value) = parse_hex_or_dec(head) {
            return apply_offsets(value, tail);
        }
    }
    Err(format!("地址「{text}」无法解析。"))
}

/// Parse a number from a CE address expression.
///
/// Everything in a CE address is hexadecimal, including a bare run of digits.
/// "18" in "[game.exe+8C]+18" means 0x18; treating it as decimal silently
/// produced an address six bytes off.
fn parse_hex_or_dec(text: &str) -> Option<u64> {
    let t = text.trim();
    let lower = t.to_ascii_lowercase();
    let digits = lower.strip_prefix("0x").unwrap_or(&lower);
    if digits.is_empty() || !digits.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_hexdigit()) {
        return None;
    }
    u64::from_str_radix(digits, 16).ok()
}

/// Serialise a table back to .ct XML.
pub fn to_xml(table: &CtTable) -> String {
    let mut xml = String::new();
    xml.push_str("<?xml version=\"1.0\" encoding=\"utf-8\"?>\n");
    xml.push_str("<CheatTable>\n");
    xml.push_str("  <CheatEntries>\n");
    for entry in &table.entries {
        xml.push_str("    <CheatEntry>\n");
        xml.push_str(&format!(
            "      <Description>\"{}\"</Description>\n",
            escape(&entry.description)
        ));
        xml.push_str(&format!(
            "      <VariableType>{}</VariableType>\n",
            vtype_to_ct(entry.vtype)
        ));
        xml.push_str(&format!(
            "      <Address>{}</Address>\n",
            escape(&entry.address)
        ));
        if let Some(value) = &entry.value {
            xml.push_str(&format!("      <Value>{}</Value>\n", escape(value)));
        }
        xml.push_str("    </CheatEntry>\n");
    }
    xml.push_str("  </CheatEntries>\n");
    xml.push_str("</CheatTable>\n");
    xml
}
fn parse_attributes(text: &str) -> Vec<(String, String)> {
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    let chars: Vec<char> = text.chars().collect();
    let mut i = 0usize;
    while i < chars.len() {
        while i < chars.len() && chars[i].is_whitespace() {
            i += 1;
        }
        let name_start = i;
        while i < chars.len() && chars[i] != '=' && !chars[i].is_whitespace() {
            i += 1;
        }
        if name_start == i {
            break;
        }
        let name: String = chars[name_start..i].iter().collect();
        while i < chars.len() && chars[i] != '=' {
            i += 1;
        }
        if i >= chars.len() {
            break;
        }
        i += 1;
        while i < chars.len() && chars[i].is_whitespace() {
            i += 1;
        }
        if i >= chars.len() {
            break;
        }
        let quote = chars[i];
        if quote != '"' && quote != '\'' {
            continue;
        }
        i += 1;
        let value_start = i;
        while i < chars.len() && chars[i] != quote {
            i += 1;
        }
        let value: String = chars[value_start..i].iter().collect();
        i += 1;
        out.push((name, unescape(&value)));
    }
    out
}
```

`app/src/disasm.rs`:

```rs
//! A small x86-64 disassembler.
//!
//! Cheat Engine shows code as instructions. This module decodes the integer and
//! SSE subset that shows up in real game code well enough to give a readable
//! listing with correct instruction lengths, which is what an address view and an
//! eventual assembler need.
//!
//! Unknown encodings are reported as data bytes rather than guessed at, so the
//! listing never lies about how much space an instruction occupies.

/// What an instruction does to the instruction pointer.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Flow {
    None,
    /// A call to this absolute address.
    Call(u64),
    /// An unconditional jump to this absolute address.
    Jump(u64),
    /// A conditional jump; the mnemonic already says the condition.
    CondJump(u64),
    /// ret / syscall: leaves this block.
    Return,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct Insn {
    pub address: u64,
    pub bytes: Vec<u8>,
    pub length: usize,
    pub text: String,
    pub flow: Flow,
}

impl Insn {
    pub fn hex(&self) -> String {
        self.bytes
            .iter()
            .map(|b| format!("{b:02X}"))
            .collect::<Vec<_>>()
            .join(" ")
    }
}

const REG8_REX: [&str; 16] = [
    "al", "cl", "dl", "bl", "spl", "bpl", "sil", "dil", "r8b", "r9b", "r10b", "r11b", "r12b",
    "r13b", "r14b", "r15b",
];
const REG8_LEGACY: [&str; 8] = ["al", "cl", "dl", "bl", "ah", "ch", "dh", "bh"];
const REG16: [&str; 16] = [
    "ax", "cx", "dx", "bx", "sp", "bp", "si", "di", "r8w", "r9w", "r10w", "r11w", "r12w", "r13w",
    "r14w", "r15w",
];
const REG32: [&str; 16] = [
    "eax", "ecx", "edx", "ebx", "esp", "ebp", "esi", "edi", "r8d", "r9d", "r10d", "r11d", "r12d",
    "r13d", "r14d", "r15d",
];
const REG64: [&str; 16] = [
    "rax", "rcx", "rdx", "rbx", "rsp", "rbp", "rsi", "rdi", "r8", "r9", "r10", "r11", "r12", "r13",
    "r14", "r15",
];
const SREG: [&str; 8] = ["es", "cs", "ss", "ds", "fs", "gs", "?", "?"];

fn reg_name(index: u8, size: u8, has_rex: bool) -> String {
    let index = (index & 0x0F) as usize;
    match size {
        1 => {
            if has_rex || index >= 8 {
                REG8_REX[index].to_string()
            } else {
                REG8_LEGACY[index].to_string()
            }
        }
        2 => REG16[index].to_string(),
        4 => REG32[index].to_string(),
        8 => REG64[index].to_string(),
        16 => format!("xmm{index}"),
        _ => format!("r{index}"),
    }
}

struct Decoder<'a> {
    buf: &'a [u8],
    pos: usize,
    address: u64,
    is64: bool,
    rex: u8,
    opsize16: bool,
    addr32: bool,
    rep: Option<&'static str>,
    seg: Option<&'static str>,
}

impl<'a> Decoder<'a> {
    fn new(buf: &'a [u8], address: u64, is64: bool) -> Self {
        Self {
            buf,
            pos: 0,
            address,
            is64,
            rex: 0,
            opsize16: false,
            addr32: false,
            rep: None,
            seg: None,
        }
    }

    fn taken(&self) -> usize {
        self.pos
    }

    fn has_rex(&self) -> bool {
        self.rex != 0
    }

    fn peek(&self, offset: usize) -> Option<u8> {
        self.buf.get(self.pos + offset).copied()
    }

    fn byte(&mut self) -> Option<u8> {
        let value = *self.buf.get(self.pos)?;
        self.pos += 1;
        Some(value)
    }

    fn word(&mut self) -> Option<u64> {
        let low = self.byte()? as u64;
        let high = self.byte()? as u64;
        Some(low | (high << 8))
    }

    fn dword(&mut self) -> Option<u64> {
        let mut value = 0u64;
        for shift in 0..4 {
            value |= (self.byte()? as u64) << (shift * 8);
        }
        Some(value)
    }

    fn qword(&mut self) -> Option<u64> {
        let low = self.dword()?;
        let high = self.dword()?;
        Some(low | (high << 32))
    }

    /// An immediate of the given byte size, sign-extended.
    fn immediate(&mut self, size: usize) -> Option<i64> {
        match size {
            1 => Some(self.byte()? as i8 as i64),
            2 => Some(self.word()? as u16 as i16 as i64),
            4 => Some(self.dword()? as u32 as i32 as i64),
            8 => Some(self.qword()? as i64),
            _ => None,
        }
    }

    /// Size of a full-size operand, honoring REX.W and the 0x66 prefix.
    fn opsize(&self) -> u8 {
        if self.is64 && self.rex & 0x08 != 0 {
            8
        } else if self.opsize16 {
            2
        } else {
            4
        }
    }

    fn addr_size(&self) -> u8 {
        if self.is64 && !self.addr32 {
            8
        } else {
            4
        }
    }

    fn imm_size(&self, size: u8) -> usize {
        if size == 2 {
            2
        } else {
            4
        }
    }

    /// Decode ModRM plus any SIB and displacement. Returns the r/m operand text
    /// and the register index from the ModRM.reg field.
    fn modrm(&mut self, size: u8) -> Option<(String, u8)> {
        let modrm = self.byte()?;
        let mode = modrm >> 6;
        let reg = ((modrm >> 3) & 7) | ((self.rex & 0x04) << 1);
        let rm_low = modrm & 7;
        let mut rm = rm_low | ((self.rex & 0x01) << 3);
        if mode == 3 {
            // Register-direct form: the r/m field is itself a register.
            return Some((reg_name(rm, size, self.has_rex()), reg));
        }

        let mut base_name: Option<String> = None;
        let mut index_name: Option<String> = None;
        let mut scale = 1u64;
        let mut disp: i64 = 0;
        let mut have_disp = false;

        if rm_low == 4 {
            let sib = self.byte()?;
            scale = 1u64 << (sib >> 6);
            let index_low = (sib >> 3) & 7;
            let base_low = sib & 7;
            let index = index_low | ((self.rex & 0x02) << 2);
            if index_low != 4 || (self.rex & 0x02) != 0 {
                index_name = Some(reg_name(index, self.addr_size(), self.has_rex()));
            }
            if base_low == 5 && mode == 0 {
                disp = self.dword()? as u32 as i32 as i64;
                have_disp = true;
            } else {
                rm = base_low | ((self.rex & 0x01) << 3);
                base_name = Some(reg_name(rm, self.addr_size(), self.has_rex()));
            }
        } else if mode == 0 && rm_low == 5 && self.is64 && !self.addr32 {
            // RIP-relative: the displacement is resolved against the next
            // instruction, which is not known until the whole insn is decoded.
            disp = self.dword()? as u32 as i32 as i64;
            have_disp = true;
            base_name = Some("rip".to_string());
        } else {
            base_name = Some(reg_name(rm, self.addr_size(), self.has_rex()));
        }

        match mode {
            1 => {
                disp = self.byte()? as i8 as i64;
                have_disp = true;
            }
            2 => {
                disp = self.dword()? as u32 as i32 as i64;
                have_disp = true;
            }
            _ => {}
        }

        let mut parts: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
        if let Some(base) = base_name {
            parts.push(base);
        }
        if let Some(index) = index_name {
            if scale > 1 {
                parts.push(format!("{index}*{scale}"));
            } else {
                parts.push(index);
            }
        }
        if have_disp {
            if disp < 0 {
                parts.push(format!("-0x{:X}", -(disp as i128) as i64));
            } else {
                parts.push(format!("0x{disp:X}"));
            }
        }
        let mut text = String::new();
        if let Some(seg) = self.seg {
            text.push_str(seg);
            text.push(':');
        }
        text.push('[');
        text.push_str(&parts.join("+"));
        text.push(']');
        let text = match size {
            1 => format!("byte ptr {text}"),
            2 => format!("word ptr {text}"),
            8 => format!("qword ptr {text}"),
            16 => format!("xmmword ptr {text}"),
            _ => text,
        };
        Some((text, reg))
    }
}

/// Relative-branch target: the displacement is relative to the next instruction.
fn branch_target(address: u64, taken: usize, displacement: i64) -> u64 {
    (address as i128 + taken as i128 + displacement as i128) as u64
}

const ALU: [&str; 8] = ["add", "or", "adc", "sbb", "and", "sub", "xor", "cmp"];
const SHIFTS: [&str; 8] = ["rol", "ror", "rcl", "rcr", "shl", "shr", "sal", "sar"];
const CONDITIONS: [&str; 16] = [
    "o", "no", "b", "ae", "e", "ne", "be", "a", "s", "ns", "p", "np", "l", "ge", "le", "g",
];

/// Decode one instruction at the given address.
pub fn decode(buf: &[u8], address: u64, is64: bool) -> Option<Insn> {
    let mut d = Decoder::new(buf, address, is64);

    // Prefixes. REX must come last, so stop scanning once it is seen.
    loop {
        let next = d.peek(0)?;
        match next {
            0x66 => {
                d.opsize16 = true;
                d.pos += 1;
            }
            0x67 => {
                d.addr32 = true;
                d.pos += 1;
            }
            0xF0 => d.pos += 1,
            0xF2 => {
                d.rep = Some("repne ");
                d.pos += 1;
            }
            0xF3 => {
                d.rep = Some("rep ");
                d.pos += 1;
            }
            0x26 => {
                d.seg = Some("es");
                d.pos += 1;
            }
            0x2E => {
                d.seg = Some("cs");
                d.pos += 1;
            }
            0x36 => {
                d.seg = Some("ss");
                d.pos += 1;
            }
            0x3E => {
                d.seg = Some("ds");
                d.pos += 1;
            }
            0x64 => {
                d.seg = Some("fs");
                d.pos += 1;
            }
            0x65 => {
                d.seg = Some("gs");
                d.pos += 1;
            }
            0x40..=0x4F if is64 => {
                d.rex = next;
                d.pos += 1;
            }
            _ => break,
        }
    }

    let opcode = d.byte()?;
    let mut mnemonic = String::new();
    let mut operands: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
    let mut flow = Flow::None;

    match opcode {
        0x00..=0x3B if opcode & 7 <= 3 => {
            let alu = (opcode >> 3) as usize;
            mnemonic = ALU[alu].to_string();
            let byte = opcode & 1 == 0;
            let size = if byte { 1 } else { d.opsize() };
            let (rm, reg) = d.modrm(size)?;
            if opcode & 2 == 0 {
                operands.push(rm);
                operands.push(reg_name(reg, size, d.has_rex()));
            } else {
                operands.push(reg_name(reg, size, d.has_rex()));
                operands.push(rm);
            }
        }
        0x04 | 0x05 | 0x0C | 0x0D | 0x14 | 0x15 | 0x1C | 0x1D | 0x24 | 0x25 | 0x2C | 0x2D
        | 0x34 | 0x35 | 0x3C | 0x3D => {
            let alu = (opcode >> 3) as usize;
            mnemonic = ALU[alu].to_string();
            let byte = opcode & 1 == 0;
            let size = if byte { 1 } else { d.opsize() };
            operands.push(reg_name(0, size, d.has_rex()));
            let imm = if byte { 1 } else { d.imm_size(size) };
            operands.push(format!("0x{:X}", d.immediate(imm)?));
        }
        0x50..=0x57 => {
            mnemonic = "push".to_string();
            let reg = (opcode & 7) | ((d.rex & 0x01) << 3);
            operands.push(reg_name(reg, if is64 { 8 } else { d.opsize() }, d.has_rex()));
        }
        0x58..=0x5F => {
            mnemonic = "pop".to_string();
            let reg = (opcode & 7) | ((d.rex & 0x01) << 3);
            operands.push(reg_name(reg, if is64 { 8 } else { d.opsize() }, d.has_rex()));
        }
        0x63 => {
            mnemonic = "movsxd".to_string();
            let size = d.opsize();
            let (rm, reg) = d.modrm(4)?;
            operands.push(reg_name(reg, if size == 8 { 8 } else { 4 }, d.has_rex()));
            operands.push(rm);
        }
        0x68 => {
            mnemonic = "push".to_string();
            operands.push(format!("0x{:X}", d.immediate(d.imm_size(d.opsize()))?));
        }
        0x6A => {
            mnemonic = "push".to_string();
            operands.push(format!("0x{:X}", d.immediate(1)?));
        }
        0x69 | 0x6B => {
            mnemonic = "imul".to_string();
            let size = d.opsize();
            let (rm, reg) = d.modrm(size)?;
            operands.push(reg_name(reg, size, d.has_rex()));
            operands.push(rm);
            let imm = if opcode == 0x6B { 1 } else { d.imm_size(size) };
            operands.push(format!("0x{:X}", d.immediate(imm)?));
        }
        0x70..=0x7F => {
            mnemonic = format!("j{}", CONDITIONS[(opcode & 0x0F) as usize]);
            let displacement = d.immediate(1)?;
            let target = branch_target(address, d.taken(), displacement);
            operands.push(format!("0x{target:X}"));
            flow = Flow::CondJump(target);
        }
        0x80 | 0x81 | 0x83 => {
            let size = if opcode == 0x80 { 1 } else { d.opsize() };
            let (rm, reg) = d.modrm(size)?;
            mnemonic = ALU[(reg & 7) as usize].to_string();
            operands.push(rm);
            let imm = if opcode == 0x83 { 1 } else { d.imm_size(size) };
            operands.push(format!("0x{:X}", d.immediate(imm)?));
        }
        0x84 | 0x85 => {
            mnemonic = "test".to_string();
            let size = if opcode == 0x84 { 1 } else { d.opsize() };
            let (rm, reg) = d.modrm(size)?;
            operands.push(rm);
            operands.push(reg_name(reg, size, d.has_rex()));
        }
        0x86 | 0x87 => {
            mnemonic = "xchg".to_string();
            let size = if opcode == 0x86 { 1 } else { d.opsize() };
            let (rm, reg) = d.modrm(size)?;
            operands.push(rm);
            operands.push(reg_name(reg, size, d.has_rex()));
        }
        0x88 | 0x89 => {
            mnemonic = "mov".to_string();
            let size = if opcode == 0x88 { 1 } else { d.opsize() };
            let (rm, reg) = d.modrm(size)?;
            operands.push(rm);
            operands.push(reg_name(reg, size, d.has_rex()));
        }
        0x8A | 0x8B => {
            mnemonic = "mov".to_string();
            let size = if opcode == 0x8A { 1 } else { d.opsize() };
            let (rm, reg) = d.modrm(size)?;
            operands.push(reg_name(reg, size, d.has_rex()));
            operands.push(rm);
        }
        0x8D => {
            mnemonic = "lea".to_string();
            let size = d.opsize();
            let (rm, reg) = d.modrm(size)?;
            operands.push(reg_name(reg, size, d.has_rex()));
            operands.push(rm);
        }
        0x8E => {
            mnemonic = "mov".to_string();
            let (rm, reg) = d.modrm(2)?;
            operands.push(SREG[(reg & 7) as usize].to_string());
            operands.push(rm);
        }
        0x8F => {
            mnemonic = "pop".to_string();
            let (rm, _) = d.modrm(if is64 { 8 } else { 2 })?;
            operands.push(rm);
        }
        0x90 => mnemonic = "nop".to_string(),
        0x91..=0x97 => {
            mnemonic = "xchg".to_string();
            let size = d.opsize();
            let reg = (opcode & 7) | ((d.rex & 0x01) << 3);
            operands.push(reg_name(reg, size, d.has_rex()));
            operands.push(reg_name(0, size, d.has_rex()));
        }
        0x98 => {
            mnemonic = match d.opsize() {
                8 => "cdqe",
                2 => "cbw",
                _ => "cwde",
            }
            .to_string();
        }
        0x99 => {
            mnemonic = match d.opsize() {
                8 => "cqo",
                2 => "cwd",
                _ => "cdq",
            }
            .to_string();
        }
```
