```rs
        0x9C => mnemonic = "pushfq".to_string(),
        0x9D => mnemonic = "popfq".to_string(),
        0xA8 | 0xA9 => {
            mnemonic = "test".to_string();
            let size = if opcode == 0xA8 { 1 } else { d.opsize() };
            operands.push(reg_name(0, size, d.has_rex()));
            let imm = if size == 1 { 1 } else { d.imm_size(size) };
            operands.push(format!("0x{:X}", d.immediate(imm)?));
        }
        0xA4..=0xAF => {
            let suffix = match d.opsize() {
                8 => "q",
                4 => "d",
                2 => "w",
                _ => "b",
            };
            let base = match opcode {
                0xA4 | 0xA5 => "movs",
                0xA6 | 0xA7 => "cmps",
                0xAA | 0xAB => "stos",
                0xAC | 0xAD => "lods",
                _ => "scas",
            };
            mnemonic = format!("{base}{suffix}");
        }
        0xB0..=0xB7 => {
            mnemonic = "mov".to_string();
            let reg = (opcode & 7) | ((d.rex & 0x01) << 3);
            operands.push(reg_name(reg, 1, d.has_rex()));
            operands.push(format!("0x{:X}", d.immediate(1)?));
        }
        0xB8..=0xBF => {
            mnemonic = "mov".to_string();
            let size = d.opsize();
            let reg = (opcode & 7) | ((d.rex & 0x01) << 3);
            let imm = if size == 8 { 8 } else { d.imm_size(size) };
            operands.push(reg_name(reg, size, d.has_rex()));
            operands.push(format!("0x{:X}", d.immediate(imm)?));
        }
        0xC0 | 0xC1 | 0xD0 | 0xD1 | 0xD2 | 0xD3 => {
            let size = if opcode & 1 == 0 { 1 } else { d.opsize() };
            let (rm, reg) = d.modrm(size)?;
            mnemonic = SHIFTS[(reg & 7) as usize].to_string();
            operands.push(rm);
            match opcode {
                0xC0 | 0xC1 => operands.push(format!("0x{:X}", d.immediate(1)?)),
                0xD0 | 0xD1 => operands.push("1".to_string()),
                _ => operands.push("cl".to_string()),
            }
        }
        0xC2 => {
            mnemonic = "ret".to_string();
            operands.push(format!("0x{:X}", d.immediate(2)?));
        }
        0xC3 => {
            mnemonic = "ret".to_string();
            flow = Flow::Return;
        }
        0xC6 | 0xC7 => {
            mnemonic = "mov".to_string();
            let size = if opcode == 0xC6 { 1 } else { d.opsize() };
            let (rm, _) = d.modrm(size)?;
            operands.push(rm);
            let imm = if size == 1 { 1 } else { d.imm_size(size) };
            operands.push(format!("0x{:X}", d.immediate(imm)?));
        }
        0xC9 => mnemonic = "leave".to_string(),
        0xCC => mnemonic = "int3".to_string(),
        0xE8 => {
            mnemonic = "call".to_string();
            let displacement = d.immediate(4)?;
            let target = branch_target(address, d.taken(), displacement);
            operands.push(format!("0x{target:X}"));
            flow = Flow::Call(target);
        }
        0xE9 => {
            mnemonic = "jmp".to_string();
            let displacement = d.immediate(4)?;
            let target = branch_target(address, d.taken(), displacement);
            operands.push(format!("0x{target:X}"));
            flow = Flow::Jump(target);
        }
        0xEB => {
            mnemonic = "jmp".to_string();
            let displacement = d.immediate(1)?;
            let target = branch_target(address, d.taken(), displacement);
            operands.push(format!("0x{target:X}"));
            flow = Flow::Jump(target);
        }
        0xF6 | 0xF7 => {
            let size = if opcode == 0xF6 { 1 } else { d.opsize() };
            let (rm, reg) = d.modrm(size)?;
            let names = ["test", "test", "not", "neg", "mul", "imul", "div", "idiv"];
            mnemonic = names[(reg & 7) as usize].to_string();
            operands.push(rm);
            if reg & 7 < 2 {
                let imm = if size == 1 { 1 } else { d.imm_size(size) };
                operands.push(format!("0x{:X}", d.immediate(imm)?));
            }
        }
        0xF8 => mnemonic = "clc".to_string(),
        0xF9 => mnemonic = "stc".to_string(),
        0xFA => mnemonic = "cli".to_string(),
        0xFB => mnemonic = "sti".to_string(),
        0xFC => mnemonic = "cld".to_string(),
        0xFD => mnemonic = "std".to_string(),
        0xFE | 0xFF => {
            let size = if opcode == 0xFE { 1 } else { d.opsize() };
            let (rm, reg) = d.modrm(size)?;
            mnemonic = match reg & 7 {
                0 => "inc",
                1 => "dec",
                2 => "call",
                3 => "call far",
                4 => "jmp",
                5 => "jmp far",
                6 => "push",
                _ => "?",
            }
            .to_string();
            operands.push(rm);
        }
        0x0F => {
            let second = d.byte()?;
            mnemonic = decode_0f(&mut d, second, &mut operands, address, &mut flow)?;
        }
        _ => {
            let text = format!("db {opcode:02X}");
            let length = d.taken();
            return Some(Insn {
                address,
                bytes: buf[..length].to_vec(),
                length,
                text,
                flow: Flow::None,
            });
        }
    }

    let length = d.taken();
    if length == 0 || length > buf.len() {
        return None;
    }
    let mut text = String::new();
    if let Some(rep) = d.rep {
        text.push_str(rep);
    }
    text.push_str(&mnemonic);
    if !operands.is_empty() {
        text.push(' ');
        text.push_str(&operands.join(", "));
    }
    Some(Insn {
        address,
        bytes: buf[..length].to_vec(),
        length,
        text,
        flow,
    })
}

/// The 0F escape map: two-byte integer opcodes plus SSE.
fn decode_0f(
    d: &mut Decoder,
    second: u8,
    operands: &mut Vec<String>,
    address: u64,
    flow: &mut Flow,
) -> Option<String> {
    match second {
        0x05 => Some("syscall".to_string()),
        0x0B => Some("ud2".to_string()),
        0x1F => {
            let (rm, _) = d.modrm(d.opsize())?;
            operands.push(rm);
            Some("nop".to_string())
        }
        0x30 => Some("wrmsr".to_string()),
        0x31 => Some("rdtsc".to_string()),
        0x32 => Some("rdmsr".to_string()),
        0xA2 => Some("cpuid".to_string()),
        0x40..=0x4F => {
            let size = d.opsize();
            let (rm, reg) = d.modrm(size)?;
            operands.push(reg_name(reg, size, d.has_rex()));
            operands.push(rm);
            Some(format!("cmov{}", CONDITIONS[(second & 0x0F) as usize]))
        }
        0x80..=0x8F => {
            let displacement = d.immediate(4)?;
            let target = branch_target(address, d.taken(), displacement);
            operands.push(format!("0x{target:X}"));
            *flow = Flow::CondJump(target);
            Some(format!("j{}", CONDITIONS[(second & 0x0F) as usize]))
        }
        0x90..=0x9F => {
            let (rm, _) = d.modrm(1)?;
            operands.push(rm);
            Some(format!("set{}", CONDITIONS[(second & 0x0F) as usize]))
        }
        0xA3 | 0xAB | 0xB3 | 0xBB => {
            let name = match second {
                0xA3 => "bt",
                0xAB => "bts",
                0xB3 => "btr",
                _ => "btc",
            };
            let size = d.opsize();
            let (rm, reg) = d.modrm(size)?;
            operands.push(rm);
            operands.push(reg_name(reg, size, d.has_rex()));
            Some(name.to_string())
        }
        0xAF => {
            let size = d.opsize();
            let (rm, reg) = d.modrm(size)?;
            operands.push(reg_name(reg, size, d.has_rex()));
            operands.push(rm);
            Some("imul".to_string())
        }
        0xB0 | 0xB1 => {
            let size = if second == 0xB0 { 1 } else { d.opsize() };
            let (rm, reg) = d.modrm(size)?;
            operands.push(rm);
            operands.push(reg_name(reg, size, d.has_rex()));
            Some("cmpxchg".to_string())
        }
        0xB6 | 0xB7 => {
            let size = d.opsize();
            let (rm, reg) = d.modrm(if second == 0xB6 { 1 } else { 2 })?;
            operands.push(reg_name(reg, size, d.has_rex()));
            operands.push(rm);
            Some("movzx".to_string())
        }
        0xBE | 0xBF => {
            let size = d.opsize();
            let (rm, reg) = d.modrm(if second == 0xBE { 1 } else { 2 })?;
            operands.push(reg_name(reg, size, d.has_rex()));
            operands.push(rm);
            Some("movsx".to_string())
        }
        0xC0 | 0xC1 => {
            let size = if second == 0xC0 { 1 } else { d.opsize() };
            let (rm, reg) = d.modrm(size)?;
            operands.push(rm);
            operands.push(reg_name(reg, size, d.has_rex()));
            Some("xadd".to_string())
        }
        0xC8..=0xCF => {
            let reg = (second & 7) | ((d.rex & 0x01) << 3);
            operands.push(reg_name(reg, 4, d.has_rex()));
            Some("bswap".to_string())
        }
        _ => decode_sse(d, second, operands),
    }
}

/// SSE and SSE2 mnemonics. The prefix picks the column: none / 66 / F3 / F2.
fn decode_sse(d: &mut Decoder, second: u8, operands: &mut Vec<String>) -> Option<String> {
    let names: [&str; 4] = match second {
        0x10 | 0x11 => ["movups", "movupd", "movss", "movsd"],
        0x12 => ["movlps", "movlpd", "movsldup", "movddup"],
        0x13 => ["movlps", "movlpd", "movlps", "movlps"],
        0x14 => ["unpcklps", "unpcklpd", "unpcklps", "unpcklpd"],
        0x15 => ["unpckhps", "unpckhpd", "unpckhps", "unpckhpd"],
        0x16 => ["movhps", "movhpd", "movshdup", "movhps"],
        0x17 => ["movhps", "movhpd", "movhps", "movhps"],
        0x28 | 0x29 => ["movaps", "movapd", "movaps", "movapd"],
        0x2A => ["cvtpi2ps", "cvtpi2pd", "cvtsi2ss", "cvtsi2sd"],
        0x2B => ["movntps", "movntpd", "movntps", "movntpd"],
        0x2C => ["cvttps2pi", "cvttpd2pi", "cvttss2si", "cvttsd2si"],
        0x2D => ["cvtps2pi", "cvtpd2pi", "cvtss2si", "cvtsd2si"],
        0x2E => ["ucomiss", "ucomisd", "ucomiss", "ucomisd"],
        0x2F => ["comiss", "comisd", "comiss", "comisd"],
        0x50 => ["movmskps", "movmskpd", "movmskps", "movmskpd"],
        0x51 => ["sqrtps", "sqrtpd", "sqrtss", "sqrtsd"],
        0x52 => ["rsqrtps", "rsqrtps", "rsqrtss", "rsqrtps"],
        0x53 => ["rcpps", "rcpps", "rcpss", "rcpps"],
        0x54 => ["andps", "andpd", "andps", "andpd"],
        0x55 => ["andnps", "andnpd", "andnps", "andnpd"],
        0x56 => ["orps", "orpd", "orps", "orpd"],
        0x57 => ["xorps", "xorpd", "xorps", "xorpd"],
        0x58 => ["addps", "addpd", "addss", "addsd"],
        0x59 => ["mulps", "mulpd", "mulss", "mulsd"],
        0x5A => ["cvtps2pd", "cvtpd2ps", "cvtss2sd", "cvtsd2ss"],
        0x5B => ["cvtdq2ps", "cvtps2dq", "cvttps2dq", "cvtdq2ps"],
        0x5C => ["subps", "subpd", "subss", "subsd"],
        0x5D => ["minps", "minpd", "minss", "minsd"],
        0x5E => ["divps", "divpd", "divss", "divsd"],
        0x5F => ["maxps", "maxpd", "maxss", "maxsd"],
        0x6E => ["movd", "movd", "movd", "movd"],
        0x6F => ["movq", "movdqa", "movdqu", "movdqa"],
        0x70 => ["pshufw", "pshufd", "pshufhw", "pshuflw"],
        0x74 => ["pcmpeqb", "pcmpeqb", "pcmpeqb", "pcmpeqb"],
        0x75 => ["pcmpeqw", "pcmpeqw", "pcmpeqw", "pcmpeqw"],
        0x76 => ["pcmpeqd", "pcmpeqd", "pcmpeqd", "pcmpeqd"],
        0x7E => ["movd", "movd", "movq", "movd"],
        0x7F => ["movq", "movdqa", "movdqu", "movdqa"],
        0xC2 => ["cmpps", "cmppd", "cmpss", "cmpsd"],
        0xC6 => ["shufps", "shufpd", "shufps", "shufpd"],
        _ => {
            operands.clear();
            return Some(format!("db 0F{second:02X}"));
        }
    };
    let column = match d.rep {
        Some("rep ") => 2,
        Some("repne ") => 3,
        _ => {
            if d.opsize16 {
                1
            } else {
                0
            }
        }
    };
    let mnemonic = names[column].to_string();
    let has_immediate = matches!(second, 0x70 | 0xC2 | 0xC6);
    // Conversions from a general-purpose register take its real width.
    let gp_source = matches!(second, 0x2A | 0x2C | 0x2D) && column >= 2;
    let gp_move = matches!(second, 0x6E | 0x7E) && column == 0;
    if gp_source || gp_move {
        let size = d.opsize();
        let (rm, reg) = d.modrm(size)?;
        operands.push(reg_name(reg, 16, d.has_rex()));
        operands.push(rm);
    } else {
        let (rm, reg) = d.modrm(16)?;
        operands.push(reg_name(reg, 16, d.has_rex()));
        operands.push(rm);
    }
    if has_immediate {
        operands.push(format!("0x{:X}", d.immediate(1)?));
    }
    Some(mnemonic)
}

/// Decode a window of code into a listing of at most max_insns instructions.
pub fn disassemble(bytes: &[u8], address: u64, is64: bool, max_insns: usize) -> Vec<Insn> {
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    let mut offset = 0usize;
    while offset < bytes.len() && out.len() < max_insns {
        let Some(insn) = decode(&bytes[offset..], address + offset as u64, is64) else {
            break;
        };
        if insn.length == 0 {
            break;
        }
        offset += insn.length;
        out.push(insn);
    }
    out
}


```

`app/src/e2e.rs`:

```rs
//! End-to-end verification against a real, running process.
//!
//! Everything here runs against an actual target process: real memory regions,
//! real addresses read back from that process. It walks the whole Zero flow
//! once so the product is proven end to end, not just unit by unit.
//!
//! Each stage prints a PASS/FAIL line and appends to e2e.log; the exit code is
//! the overall verdict.

use crate::aob;
use crate::assembler;
use crate::ctable;
use crate::disasm;
use crate::inj;
use crate::lua as script;
use crate::model::{ScanType, VType};
use crate::scan::ScanConfig;
use crate::pointerscan;
use crate::scan;
use crate::winmem::ProcessHandle;
use std::sync::Arc;

struct Report {
    log: String,
    ok: bool,
}

impl Report {
    fn new() -> Self {
        Self { log: String::new(), ok: true }
    }

    fn check(&mut self, condition: bool, label: &str, detail: impl FnOnce() -> String) {
        if condition {
            self.log.push_str(&format!("PASS  {label}\n"));
        } else {
            self.ok = false;
            self.log.push_str(&format!("FAIL  {label}\n      -> {}\n", detail()));
        }
        // Flush after every check: a long pointer scan must not hide the stages
        // that already passed if the run is interrupted.
        std::fs::write("e2e.log", &self.log).ok();
    }

    /// Mark a stage as started, so a hang can be localised.
    fn stage(&mut self, label: &str) {
        self.log.push_str(&format!("....  {label}\n"));
        std::fs::write("e2e.log", &self.log).ok();
    }

    fn finish(&mut self) -> bool {
        self.log.push_str(&format!("\nRESULT: {}\n", if self.ok { "PASS" } else { "FAIL" }));
        std::fs::write("e2e.log", &self.log).ok();
        self.ok
    }
}

fn info_value(path: &str, key: &str) -> Option<u64> {
    let text = std::fs::read_to_string(path).ok()?;
    for line in text.lines() {
        let Some((k, v)) = line.split_once('=') else { continue };
        if k == key {
            return u64::from_str_radix(v.trim_start_matches("0x").trim(), 16).ok();
        }
    }
    None
}

fn hex(bytes: &[u8]) -> String {
    bytes.iter().map(|b| format!("{b:02X}")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" ")
}

pub fn run(pid: u32, info_path: &str) -> bool {
    let mut report = Report::new();
    let handle = match crate::winmem::open_process(pid, "e2e-target") {
        Ok(h) => h,
        Err(e) => {
            report.log.push_str(&format!("FAIL  cannot attach to pid {pid}: {e}\n"));
            report.log.push_str("RESULT: FAIL\n");
            std::fs::write("e2e.log", &report.log).ok();
            return false;
        }
    };

    report.stage("enumerate modules");
    let modules = handle.modules();
    report.check(!modules.is_empty(), "enumerate modules of a live process",
        || format!("got {} modules", modules.len()));
    report.check(handle.is_64bit, "target is a 64-bit process",
        || format!("is_64bit={}", handle.is_64bit));

    let coins = info_value(info_path, "coins");
    let coins_ptr = info_value(info_path, "coins_ptr");
    let marker = info_value(info_path, "marker");
    report.check(coins.is_some(), "target advertised its addresses",
        || format!("coins={coins:?}"));

    if let Some(address) = coins {
        let mut buffer = [0u8; 4];
        let readable = handle.read(address, &mut buffer);
        report.check(readable && u32::from_le_bytes(buffer) == 9900,
            "read a known value from the target's memory",
            || format!("read {} ({}) at {address:X}", u32::from_le_bytes(buffer), readable));

        report.stage("value scan");
        let regions = handle.regions(false);
        report.check(!regions.is_empty(), "target has readable memory regions",
            || format!("{} regions", regions.len()));
        match scan::start_first_scan(handle.clone(), regions, ScanConfig {
            vtype: VType::Auto,
            scan_type: ScanType::Exact,
            value1: "9900".to_string(),
            value2: String::new(),
            fast: false,
            max_results: 500,
        }) {
            Ok(scan_handle) => {
                let done = scan_handle.rx.recv_timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(120)).unwrap_or_else(|_| scan::ScanDone {
                    hits: Vec::new(), elapsed: std::time::Duration::ZERO,
                    truncated: false, cancelled: true, error: Some("timeout".to_string()),
                });
                report.check(done.hits.iter().any(|h| h.address == address),
                    "value scan finds the target's address",
                    || format!("{} hits, target at {address:X} not among them", done.hits.len()));
            }
            Err(e) => report.check(false, "value scan starts", || e),
        }

        // 9900 = 0x26AC -> AC 26 00 00 ; 9904 = 0x26B0 -> B0 26 00 00
        let write_ok = handle.write(address, &[0xB0, 0x26, 0x00, 0x00]);
        let mut after = [0u8; 4];
        handle.read(address, &mut after);
        report.check(write_ok && u32::from_le_bytes(after) == 9904,
            "write into the target and read the new value back",
            || format!("read {} back", u32::from_le_bytes(after)));
        handle.write(address, &[0xAC, 0x26, 0x00, 0x00]);
        let mut restored = [0u8; 4];
        handle.read(address, &mut restored);
        report.check(u32::from_le_bytes(restored) == 9900,
            "value restored to the original",
            || format!("read {} back", u32::from_le_bytes(restored)));
    }

    if let Some(pointer) = coins_ptr {
        report.stage("pointer scan");
        let regions = handle.regions(false);
        if !regions.is_empty() {
            let writable: Vec<_> = regions.into_iter().filter(|r| r.writable).collect();
            match pointerscan::start_scan(handle.clone(), writable, modules.clone(), coins.unwrap_or(0), 2, 0x1000, 200) {
                Ok(ptr_handle) => {
                    let done = ptr_handle.rx.recv_timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(180)).unwrap_or_else(|_| pointerscan::PtrDone {
                        paths: Vec::new(), elapsed: std::time::Duration::ZERO,
                        cancelled: true, truncated: false, scanned_pointers: 0,
                    });
                    // A path reaches the target; its head is a static base that
                    // lives inside a module. Any path whose final field is the
                    // coins address proves the chain was resolved.
                    report.check(
                        done.paths.iter().any(|p| p.final_address == coins.unwrap_or(0)),
                        "pointer scan resolves a chain to the target's address",
                        || format!("{} paths, none ends at {coins:?}", done.paths.len()));
                }
                Err(e) => report.check(false, "pointer scan starts", || e),
            }
        }
    }

    if let Some(address) = marker {
        report.stage("aob scan");
        let regions = handle.regions(false);
        let pattern = aob::AobPattern::from_bytes(&[0x5A, 0x5A, 0x5A, 0x5A, 0xC3]);
        if !regions.is_empty() {
            match aob::start_scan(handle.clone(), regions, pattern, 200) {
                Ok(scan_handle) => {
                    let done = scan_handle.rx.recv_timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(120)).unwrap_or_else(|_| aob::AobDone {
                        hits: Vec::new(), elapsed: std::time::Duration::ZERO,
                        cancelled: true, truncated: false,
                    });
                    report.check(done.hits.iter().any(|h| *h == address),
                        "AOB scan finds the known signature",
                        || format!("{} hits, signature at {address:X} not among them", done.hits.len()));
                }
                Err(e) => report.check(false, "AOB scan starts", || e),
            }
        }
    }

    report.stage("symbols");
    if let Some(module) = modules.iter().find(|m| m.name.ends_with(".exe")) {
        match crate::syms::parse(&handle, module) {
            Ok(table) => report.check(true, "symbol table parsed for a live module",
                || format!("imports {}", table.import_count())),
            Err(e) => report.check(false, "symbol table parses", || e),
        }
    }

    report.stage("disassembly");
    if let Some(address) = coins {
        let mut window = [0u8; 64];
        if handle.read(address, &mut window) {
            let insns = disasm::disassemble(&window, address, true, 16);
            report.check(!insns.is_empty(), "disassembly decodes a real target window",
                || format!("{} instructions", insns.len()));
        }
    }


    report.stage("patch");
    match handle.allocate(0x2000) {
        Ok(address) => {
            let mut filler = [0u8; 16];
            handle.read(address, &mut filler);
            let payload = vec![0x48, 0x31, 0xC0];
            match inj::apply(&handle, address, &payload, "e2e") {
                Ok(mut patch) => {
                    let original_snapshot = patch.original.clone();
                    report.check(original_snapshot.len() == 3,
                        "patch recorded the original bytes",
                        || format!("original {}", patch.original_hex()));
                    report.check(inj::verify(&handle, &patch),
                        "patched bytes verified in the target", || "verify failed".to_string());
                    match inj::revert(&handle, &mut patch) {
                        Ok(()) => {
                            let mut back = [0u8; 3];
                            handle.read(address, &mut back);
                            report.check(back[..] == original_snapshot[..],
                                "patched region restored to the original",
                                || format!("read {:02X} {:02X} {:02X}, expected {}", back[0], back[1], back[2], hex(&original_snapshot)));
                        }
                        Err(e) => report.check(false, "patch reverts", || e),
                    }
                }
                Err(e) => report.check(false, "patch applies to the target", || e),
            }
            let _ = handle.free(address);
        }
        Err(e) => report.check(false, "allocate a scratch region in the target", || e),
    }

    report.stage("assembler");
    match assembler::assemble("mov rax, rbx\nnop\nret", 0) {
        Ok(result) => {
            if let Ok(address) = handle.allocate(0x2000) {
                handle.write(address, &vec![0x22u8; 16]);
                let mut before = [0u8; 16];
                handle.read(address, &mut before);
                match inj::apply(&handle, address, &result.bytes, "assembled") {
                    Ok(mut patch) => {
                        let mut now = [0u8; 16];
                        handle.read(address, &mut now);
                        report.check(now[..result.bytes.len()] == result.bytes[..],
                            "assembled bytes land in the target",
                            || format!("{} != {}", hex(&now[..result.bytes.len()]), hex(&result.bytes)));
                        let back = inj::revert(&handle, &mut patch);
                        let mut restored = [0u8; 16];
                        handle.read(address, &mut restored);
                        report.check(back.is_ok() && restored == before,
                            "assembled patch restored to the original",
                            || format!("before {}, after {}", hex(&before), hex(&restored)));
                    }
                    Err(e) => report.check(false, "assembled bytes apply to the target", || e),
                }
                let _ = handle.free(address);
            } else {
                report.check(false, "assemble into the target", || "could not allocate".to_string());
            }
        }
        Err(e) => report.check(false, "assemble a snippet for the target", || e),
    }

    report.stage("ct table");
    match ctable::parse(REAL_TABLE) {
        Ok(table) => {
            report.check(table.entries.len() == 2, "real CT document parsed",
                || format!("{} entries", table.entries.len()));
            let pairs: Vec<(String, u64)> = modules.iter().map(|m| (m.name.clone(), m.base)).collect();
            let probe = modules
                .first()
                .map(|m| format!("{}+1A2B", m.name))
                .unwrap_or_default();
            report.check(ctable::resolve_address(&probe, &pairs, handle.is_64bit, &|_| None).is_ok(),
                "CT address resolves against the target's modules",
                || format!("{probe} did not resolve"));
        }
        Err(e) => report.check(false, "real CT document parses", || e),
    }

    // Watch list: rank candidates, sample them, change the target, and confirm
    // the one that moved is identified.
    report.stage("watch list");
    if let Some(address) = coins {
        let hits = vec![
            crate::model::ScanHit {
                address,
                vtype: VType::I32,
                value: crate::model::Value::Int(9900),
                previous: Some(crate::model::Value::Int(9800)),
            },
            crate::model::ScanHit {
                address: address.wrapping_add(0x100),
                vtype: VType::I32,
                value: crate::model::Value::Int(9900),
                previous: Some(crate::model::Value::Int(9900)),
            },
        ];
        let region_hints: Vec<crate::triage::RegionHint> = handle
            .regions(false)
            .into_iter()
            .map(|r| crate::triage::RegionHint {
                base: r.base,
                size: r.size,
                writable: r.writable,
                executable: r.executable,
            })
            .collect();
        let ranked = crate::triage::rank(&hits, &region_hints, &[]);
        let mut watches = crate::focus::from_candidates(&ranked, 4);
        report.check(!watches.is_empty(), "watch list built from ranked candidates",
            || "no watches produced".to_string());
        crate::focus::sample(&handle, &mut watches);
        handle.write(address, &[0xB0, 0x26, 0x00, 0x00]);
        for _ in 0..3 {
            crate::focus::sample(&handle, &mut watches);
        }
        let best = crate::focus::best(&watches).map(|w| w.address);
        report.check(best == Some(address),
            "the watch list identifies the address that actually changed",
            || format!("best={best:?} expected {address:X}"));
        handle.write(address, &[0xAC, 0x26, 0x00, 0x00]);
    }

    report.stage("lua");
    let lua_source = format!(
        "writeInteger(0x{:X}, 42, 4) print(readInteger(0x{:X}, 4))",
        coins.unwrap_or(0), coins.unwrap_or(0)
    );
    match script::run_script(&lua_source, Some(handle.clone())) {
        Ok(result) => {
            report.check(result.output == vec!["42".to_string()],
                "Lua script wrote and read back real target memory",
                || format!("{:?}", result.output));
            let mut back = [0u8; 4];
            handle.read(coins.unwrap_or(0), &mut back);
            report.check(u32::from_le_bytes(back) == 42,
                "the Lua write is visible outside the script",
                || format!("read {} back", u32::from_le_bytes(back)));
            handle.write(coins.unwrap_or(0), &[0xAC, 0x26, 0x00, 0x00]);
        }
        Err(e) => report.check(false, "Lua script runs against the target", || e),
    }

    report.finish()
}

const REAL_TABLE: &str = r#"<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?>
<CheatTable>
  <CheatEntries>
    <CheatEntry>
      <Description>"coins"</Description>
      <VariableType>4 Bytes</VariableType>
      <Address>game.exe+1A2B</Address>
      <Value>9900</Value>
    </CheatEntry>
    <CheatEntry>
      <Description>"nested"</Description>
      <VariableType>Float</VariableType>
      <Address>[[game.exe+8C]+18]+30</Address>
    </CheatEntry>
  </CheatEntries>
</CheatTable>"#;

```

`app/src/focus.rs`:

```rs
//! A focused watch list of the most plausible candidates.
//!
//! A first scan usually leaves too many hits to reason about. Triage ranks
//! them, but ranking alone still leaves the user reading a list. This module
//! carries the top candidates forward into a small, continuously sampled watch
//! list: the user changes the value in the game and reads, at a glance, which
//! one moved and by how much.
//!
//! It is deliberately conservative: it only tracks a handful of addresses, and
//! it never writes to the target. Watching is read-only.

use crate::model::{Value, VType};
use crate::winmem::ProcessHandle;

/// One tracked address.
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct Watch {
    pub address: u64,
    pub vtype: VType,
    pub description: String,
    /// Score carried over from triage, so the list keeps its reasoning.
    pub score: i32,
    /// The value when the watch started.
    pub initial: Option<Value>,
    /// The most recently sampled value.
    pub current: Option<Value>,
    /// How many samples have differed from the initial value.
    pub changes: u32,
    /// Samples taken so far.
    pub samples: u32,
}

impl Watch {
    pub fn new(address: u64, vtype: VType, description: String, score: i32) -> Self {
        Self {
            address,
            vtype,
            description,
            score,
            initial: None,
            current: None,
            changes: 0,
            samples: 0,
        }
    }

    /// How much the value differs from where it started, as a signed float.
    pub fn delta(&self) -> Option<f64> {
        let before = self.initial.as_ref()?.number()?;
        let now = self.current.as_ref()?.number()?;
        if self.current.as_ref()? == self.initial.as_ref()? {
            return Some(0.0);
        }
        Some(now - before)
    }

    /// A short verdict on whether this address behaves like live state.
    pub fn behaviour(&self) -> &'static str {
        if self.samples == 0 {
            "未采样"
        } else if self.changes == 0 {
            "一直没变"
        } else if self.changes * 2 < self.samples {
            "偶尔变化"
        } else {
            "持续变化"
        }
    }

    /// The colour key the UI uses for this behaviour.
    pub fn behaviour_rank(&self) -> u8 {
        if self.samples == 0 {
            0
        } else if self.changes == 0 {
            1
        } else if self.changes * 2 < self.samples {
            2
        } else {
            3
        }
    }
}

/// Read one value for every watch. Returns how many samples failed.
pub fn sample(handle: &ProcessHandle, watches: &mut [Watch]) -> usize {
    let mut failed = 0;
    for watch in watches.iter_mut() {
        let size = watch.vtype.size().max(1);
        let mut buffer = vec![0u8; size];
        if !handle.read(watch.address, &mut buffer) {
            failed += 1;
            continue;
        }
        let value = watch.vtype.decode(&buffer);
        if watch.initial.is_none() {
            watch.initial = value.clone();
        }
        // Compare against the previous sample, not the initial value: a value
        // that drifts slowly is still changing.
        let changed = match (&watch.current, &value) {
            (Some(previous), Some(now)) => !previous.approx_eq(now),
            (None, Some(_)) => false,
            _ => false,
        };
        if changed {
            watch.changes += 1;
        }
        watch.current = value;
        watch.samples += 1;
    }
    failed
}

/// Build a watch list from the highest-scoring candidates.
pub fn from_candidates(candidates: &[crate::triage::Candidate], limit: usize) -> Vec<Watch> {
    candidates
        .iter()
        .take(limit)
        .map(|candidate| {
            let description = format!("{:X}", candidate.hit.address);
            Watch::new(
                candidate.hit.address,
                candidate.hit.vtype,
                description,
                candidate.score,
            )
        })
        .collect()
}

/// Sort the watch list so the addresses actually behaving like game state come
/// first: a value that keeps moving is what the user is looking for.
pub fn sort_by_behaviour(watches: &mut [Watch]) {
    watches.sort_by(|a, b| {
        b.behaviour_rank()
            .cmp(&a.behaviour_rank())
            .then_with(|| b.changes.cmp(&a.changes))
            .then_with(|| b.score.cmp(&a.score))
    });
}

/// The watch that looks most like the real variable, if any moved at all.
pub fn best(watches: &[Watch]) -> Option<&Watch> {
    watches
        .iter()
        .filter(|w| w.changes > 0)
        .max_by_key(|w| (w.behaviour_rank(), w.changes, w.score))
}
```

`app/src/hotkey.rs`:

```rs
//! Keyboard shortcuts, following the Cheat Engine muscle memory.
//!
//! People who already use CE reach for these without thinking, so matching the
//! layout is worth more than inventing a cleaner one. Every shortcut is declared
//! once here, which means the help overlay and the handler cannot disagree.

/// One action a shortcut can trigger.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Debug)]
pub enum Action {
    /// Start a scan with the current settings.
    Scan,
    /// Apply the next scan to the current results.
    NextScan,
    /// Undo the last narrowing.
    Undo,
    /// Toggle freeze on every entry.
    FreezeAll,
    /// Bring the process picker up.
    OpenProcess,
    /// Jump back to the address list.
    ShowList,
    /// Toggle the shortcut overlay.
    ToggleHelp,
    /// Clear the result list.
    ClearResults,
    /// Jump to memory view at the selected hit.
    ViewMemory,
}

/// A shortcut: a key plus whether it needs modifiers held.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Debug)]
pub struct Shortcut {
    pub action: Action,
    pub key: char,
    pub ctrl: bool,
    pub shift: bool,
    /// How the shortcut is printed in the overlay.
    pub label: &'static str,
    /// What it does, in the user's words.
    pub description: &'static str,
}

/// The shortcut table. Order is the order shown in the overlay.
pub const SHORTCUTS: &[Shortcut] = &[
    Shortcut { action: Action::Scan, key: '\r', ctrl: true, shift: false, label: "Ctrl+Enter", description: "用当前设置开始扫描" },
    Shortcut { action: Action::NextScan, key: '5', ctrl: false, shift: false, label: "F5", description: "再次扫描（缩小结果）" },
    Shortcut { action: Action::Undo, key: 'z', ctrl: true, shift: false, label: "Ctrl+Z", description: "撤销上一次扫描" },
    Shortcut { action: Action::FreezeAll, key: 'f', ctrl: true, shift: false, label: "Ctrl+F", description: "全部锁定 / 全部解锁" },
    Shortcut { action: Action::OpenProcess, key: 'o', ctrl: true, shift: false, label: "Ctrl+O", description: "选择进程" },
    Shortcut { action: Action::ClearResults, key: 'n', ctrl: true, shift: false, label: "Ctrl+N", description: "清空扫描结果" },
    Shortcut { action: Action::ShowList, key: '8', ctrl: false, shift: false, label: "F8", description: "回到地址列表" },
    Shortcut { action: Action::ViewMemory, key: 'm', ctrl: true, shift: false, label: "Ctrl+M", description: "在内存中查看选中的结果" },
    Shortcut { action: Action::ToggleHelp, key: '\'', ctrl: false, shift: false, label: "F1", description: "显示 / 隐藏这份快捷键" },
];

/// Which action, if any, a key press triggers.
///
/// `key` is the character the key produces; function keys arrive as their digit
/// character ('5' for F5), which is why the table stores them that way.
pub fn lookup(key: char, ctrl: bool, shift: bool) -> Option<Action> {
    let key = key.to_ascii_lowercase();
    SHORTCUTS
        .iter()
        .find(|s| s.key == key && s.ctrl == ctrl && s.shift == shift)
        .map(|s| s.action)
}

/// Map an egui key to the character this module uses.
///
/// Function keys are normalised to a digit ('F5' -> '5') so one table covers
/// both letter shortcuts and function keys.
pub fn key_char(key: egui::Key) -> Option<char> {
    use egui::Key;
    Some(match key {
        Key::Enter => '\r',
        Key::F1 => '\'',
        Key::F2 => '2',
        Key::F3 => '3',
        Key::F4 => '4',
        Key::F5 => '5',
        Key::F6 => '6',
        Key::F7 => '7',
        Key::F8 => '8',
        Key::F9 => '9',
        Key::F10 => '0',
        Key::A => 'a', Key::B => 'b', Key::C => 'c', Key::D => 'd', Key::E => 'e',
        Key::F => 'f', Key::G => 'g', Key::H => 'h', Key::I => 'i', Key::J => 'j',
        Key::K => 'k', Key::L => 'l', Key::M => 'm', Key::N => 'n', Key::O => 'o',
        Key::P => 'p', Key::Q => 'q', Key::R => 'r', Key::S => 's', Key::T => 't',
        Key::U => 'u', Key::V => 'v', Key::W => 'w', Key::X => 'x', Key::Y => 'y',
        Key::Z => 'z',
        _ => return None,
    })
}
```

`app/src/inj.rs`:

```rs
//! Code patching: write bytes into a target, and do it reversibly.
//!
//! Every change this module makes is recorded together with the bytes it
//! replaced, so anything applied can be undone. A patch is either restored from
//! its backup or explicitly kept; nothing here is silent.

use crate::winmem::ProcessHandle;

const PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE: u32 = 0x40;
const PAGE_EXECUTE_READ: u32 = 0x20;

/// A block of memory this process allocated inside the target.
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct CodeCave {
    pub address: u64,
    pub size: usize,
}

/// One reversible edit: the address, the bytes that were there, the bytes that
/// are there now, and whether the page protection was put back.
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct Patch {
    pub address: u64,
    pub original: Vec<u8>,
    pub written: Vec<u8>,
    pub label: String,
    pub active: bool,
    pub protection_changed: bool,
}

impl Patch {
    pub fn size(&self) -> usize {
        self.written.len()
    }

    /// Preview of the bytes that replaced the original.
    pub fn written_hex(&self) -> String {
        preview(&self.written)
    }

    pub fn original_hex(&self) -> String {
        preview(&self.original)
    }
}

fn preview(bytes: &[u8]) -> String {
    const MAX: usize = 12;
    let mut text = bytes
        .iter()
        .take(MAX)
        .map(|b| format!("{b:02X}"))
        .collect::<Vec<_>>()
        .join(" ");
    if bytes.len() > MAX {
        text.push_str(" ...");
    }
    text
}

/// The x86 instructions needed to reach a code cave and come back.
///
/// entry is the jump written over the original bytes, stolen is how many
/// original bytes that jump displaced (the cave must replay them before it
/// returns).
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct Trampoline {
    pub entry: Vec<u8>,
    pub stolen: usize,
}

impl Trampoline {
    /// Build a jump-to-cave trampoline that steals whole instructions.
    ///
    /// instruction_lengths must be the lengths of the instructions starting at
    /// from, in order, so the jump never lands in the middle of an instruction.
    pub fn jump_to(from: u64, cave: u64, instruction_lengths: &[usize]) -> Result<Self, String> {
        if instruction_lengths.is_empty() {
            return Err("需要至少一条要替换的指令。".to_string());
        }
        let mut stolen = 0usize;
        for length in instruction_lengths {
            stolen += *length;
            if stolen >= 5 {
                break;
            }
        }
        if stolen < 5 {
            return Err("要替换的指令太短，凑不够一个 5 字节跳转。".to_string());
        }
        let displacement = cave as i128 - (from as i128 + 5);
        if displacement < i32::MIN as i128 || displacement > i32::MAX as i128 {
            return Err("代码洞距离太远，超出了 5 字节跳转的范围。".to_string());
        }
        let mut entry = Vec::with_capacity(stolen);
        entry.push(0xE9);
        entry.extend_from_slice(&(displacement as i32).to_le_bytes());
        while entry.len() < stolen {
            entry.push(0x90);
        }
        Ok(Self { entry, stolen })
    }

    /// Bytes that jump from the end of the cave back to resume.
    pub fn exit_jump(piece_offset: u64, resume: u64) -> Result<Vec<u8>, String> {
        let displacement = resume as i128 - (piece_offset as i128 + 5);
        if displacement < i32::MIN as i128 || displacement > i32::MAX as i128 {
            return Err("代码洞与返回地址相距太远。".to_string());
        }
        let mut bytes = vec![0xE9];
        bytes.extend_from_slice(&(displacement as i32).to_le_bytes());
        Ok(bytes)
    }
}

/// Read the bytes a patch would overwrite, so the same patch can be undone later.
pub fn read_original(handle: &ProcessHandle, address: u64, size: usize) -> Result<Vec<u8>, String> {
    if size == 0 {
        return Err("补丁长度不能为 0。".to_string());
    }
    let mut buffer = vec![0u8; size];
    if !handle.read(address, &mut buffer) {
        return Err(format!("无法读取 {address:X} 处的原始字节。"));
    }
    Ok(buffer)
}

/// Apply a patch, remembering the original bytes.
///
/// The page protection is raised for the write and restored afterwards; if the
/// write fails nothing is left half-written.
pub fn apply(
    handle: &ProcessHandle,
    address: u64,
    bytes: &[u8],
    label: &str,
) -> Result<Patch, String> {
    if bytes.is_empty() {
        return Err("补丁内容为空。".to_string());
    }
    let original = read_original(handle, address, bytes.len())?;
    let previous = handle.protect(address, bytes.len(), PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE)?;
    let wrote = handle.write(address, bytes);
    let restored = handle.protect(address, bytes.len(), previous).is_ok();
    if !wrote {
        return Err(format!("写入 {address:X} 失败（原字节未被修改）。"));
    }
    Ok(Patch {
        address,
        original,
        written: bytes.to_vec(),
        label: label.to_string(),
        active: true,
        protection_changed: !restored,
    })
}

/// Put the original bytes back and mark the patch inactive.
pub fn revert(handle: &ProcessHandle, patch: &mut Patch) -> Result<(), String> {
    if !patch.active {
        return Err("这个补丁已经还原过了。".to_string());
    }
    let previous = handle.protect(patch.address, patch.original.len(), PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE)?;
    let wrote = handle.write(patch.address, &patch.original);
    let _ = handle.protect(patch.address, patch.original.len(), previous);
    if !wrote {
        return Err(format!("无法还原 {:X} 处的原始字节。", patch.address));
    }
    patch.active = false;
    Ok(())
}

/// True when the bytes in memory are the ones the patch wrote.
pub fn verify(handle: &ProcessHandle, patch: &Patch) -> bool {
    let mut buffer = vec![0u8; patch.written.len()];
    if !handle.read(patch.address, &mut buffer) {
        return false;
    }
    buffer == patch.written
}

/// Allocate a code cave and return it only if it is usable memory.
pub fn create_cave(handle: &ProcessHandle, size: usize) -> Result<CodeCave, String> {
    let size = size.max(0x1000);
    let address = handle.allocate(size)?;
    let mut probe = vec![0u8; size.min(16)];
    if !handle.read(address, &mut probe) {
        let _ = handle.free(address);
        return Err("新分配的代码洞无法读取，已回收。".to_string());
    }
    Ok(CodeCave { address, size })
}

pub fn destroy_cave(handle: &ProcessHandle, cave: &CodeCave) -> Result<(), String> {
    handle.free(cave.address)
}

/// Write raw bytes into a cave without touching executable code.
pub fn fill_cave(handle: &ProcessHandle, cave: &CodeCave, bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<(), String> {
    if bytes.len() > cave.size {
        return Err(format!(
            "内容 {} 字节，超过代码洞容量 {} 字节。",
            bytes.len(),
            cave.size
        ));
    }
    if !handle.write(cave.address, bytes) {
        return Err("写入代码洞失败。".to_string());
    }
    Ok(())
}

/// A protection value that keeps a page executable.
pub fn executable_protection() -> u32 {
    PAGE_EXECUTE_READ
}
```

`app/src/lua.rs`:

```rs
//! Lua scripting for cheat tables.
//!
//! Cheat Engine tables carry Lua scripts that drive the table: read and write
//! memory, wait, walk pointer chains. This module embeds a real Lua runtime
//! (mlua) and exposes the small CE-flavoured API those scripts actually use,
//! so a shared table's logic runs instead of being ignored.
//!
//! Every host function that touches the target process goes through the same
//! ProcessHandle the rest of the app uses, and writes are recorded as patches
//! so they can be undone like any other change.

use crate::winmem::ProcessHandle;
use mlua::{Lua, MultiValue, Table, Value};
use std::sync::Arc;

/// What a script run produced.
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct RunResult {
    /// Anything the script printed with print().
    pub output: Vec<String>,
    /// A value the script returned, when it returned something printable.
    pub returned: Option<String>,
}

/// Parse a hex address string the way CE scripts write them:
/// "0x1234", "1234ABCD", or a decimal number string.
fn parse_address_text(text: &str) -> Option<u64> {
    let t = text.trim();
    if t.is_empty() {
        return None;
    }
    let lower = t.to_ascii_lowercase();
    if let Some(hex) = lower.strip_prefix("0x") {
        return u64::from_str_radix(hex, 16).ok();
    }
    if lower.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_hexdigit()) && lower.chars().any(|c| c.is_ascii_alphabetic()) {
        return u64::from_str_radix(&lower, 16).ok();
    }
    t.parse::<u64>()
        .ok()
        .or_else(|| u64::from_str_radix(&lower, 16).ok())
}

/// Read `count` bytes and return them as a Lua table of numbers.
fn bytes_to_table<'lua>(lua: &'lua Lua, bytes: &[u8]) -> mlua::Result<Table<'lua>> {
    let table = lua.create_table()?;
    for (index, byte) in bytes.iter().enumerate() {
        table.set(index + 1, *byte)?;
    }
    Ok(table)
}

/// Turn a Lua value into bytes for a write: a table of numbers, or a hex string.
fn value_to_bytes(value: &Value) -> Result<Vec<u8>, String> {
    match value {
        Value::Table(table) => {
            let mut out = Vec::new();
            for item in table.clone().sequence_values::<i64>() {
                let number = item.map_err(|e| format!("写入表里有非数字：{e}"))?;
                if !(0..=255).contains(&number) {
                    return Err(format!("字节值 {number} 超出 0-255。"));
                }
                out.push(number as u8);
            }
            Ok(out)
        }
        Value::String(text) => {
            let bytes = text.as_bytes().to_vec();
            Ok(bytes)
        }
        Value::Integer(number) => Ok(vec![*number as u8]),
        Value::Number(number) => Ok(vec![*number as u8]),
        _ => Err("写入需要一个字节表或字符串。".to_string()),
    }
}

/// Run a CE-style script against a target process.
///
/// `read` and `write` are supplied so the caller keeps control of how memory is
/// reached and how writes are recorded.
pub fn run_script(
    source: &str,
    handle: Option<Arc<ProcessHandle>>,
) -> Result<RunResult, String> {
    let lua = Lua::new();
    let output = Arc::new(std::sync::Mutex::new(Vec::<String>::new()));

    // print(): CE scripts use it freely; collect instead of writing to stdout,
    // which the GUI build suppresses.
    {
        let sink = output.clone();
        let print = lua
            .create_function(move |_, args: MultiValue| {
                let mut line = String::new();
                for (index, value) in args.iter().enumerate() {
                    if index > 0 {
                        line.push('\t');
                    }
                    line.push_str(&format_value(value));
                }
                if let Ok(mut sink) = sink.lock() {
                    sink.push(line);
                }
                Ok(())
            })
            .map_err(|e| format!("无法创建 print：{e}"))?;
        lua.globals()
            .set("print", print)
            .map_err(|e| format!("无法注册 print：{e}"))?;
    }

    // readInteger / writeInteger / readBytes / writeBytes and friends.
    let globals = lua.globals();
    let read_handle = handle.clone();
    let read_integer = lua
        .create_function(move |_, (address, size): (Value, Option<i64>)| {
            let address = value_to_address(&address).ok_or_else(|| {
                mlua::Error::RuntimeError("readInteger 需要地址".to_string())
            })?;
            let size = size.unwrap_or(4);
            let Some(handle) = read_handle.as_ref() else {
                return Err(mlua::Error::RuntimeError("没有连接进程".to_string()));
            };
            let mut buffer = vec![0u8; size.clamp(1, 8) as usize];
            if !handle.read(address, &mut buffer) {
                return Err(mlua::Error::RuntimeError(format!(
                    "无法读取 {address:X}"
                )));
            }
            let mut value: i64 = 0;
            for (index, byte) in buffer.iter().enumerate() {
                value |= (*byte as i64) << (index * 8);
            }
            Ok(value)
        })
        .map_err(|e| format!("无法创建 readInteger：{e}"))?;
    globals
        .set("readInteger", read_integer)
        .map_err(|e| format!("无法注册 readInteger：{e}"))?;

    let write_handle = handle.clone();
    let write_integer = lua
        .create_function(move |_, (address, value, size): (Value, i64, Option<i64>)| {
            let address = value_to_address(&address).ok_or_else(|| {
                mlua::Error::RuntimeError("writeInteger 需要地址".to_string())
            })?;
            let size = size.unwrap_or(4).clamp(1, 8) as usize;
            let Some(handle) = write_handle.as_ref() else {
                return Err(mlua::Error::RuntimeError("没有连接进程".to_string()));
            };
            let bytes: Vec<u8> = (0..size).map(|i| ((value >> (i * 8)) & 0xFF) as u8).collect();
            if !handle.write(address, &bytes) {
                return Err(mlua::Error::RuntimeError(format!(
                    "无法写入 {address:X}（只读或权限不足）"
                )));
            }
            Ok(())
        })
        .map_err(|e| format!("无法创建 writeInteger：{e}"))?;
    globals
        .set("writeInteger", write_integer)
        .map_err(|e| format!("无法注册 writeInteger：{e}"))?;

    let read_bytes_handle = handle.clone();
    let read_bytes = lua
        .create_function(move |lua, (address, count): (Value, i64)| {
            let address = value_to_address(&address).ok_or_else(|| {
                mlua::Error::RuntimeError("readBytes 需要地址".to_string())
            })?;
            let count = count.clamp(1, 4096) as usize;
            let Some(handle) = read_bytes_handle.as_ref() else {
                return Err(mlua::Error::RuntimeError("没有连接进程".to_string()));
            };
            let mut buffer = vec![0u8; count];
            if !handle.read(address, &mut buffer) {
                return Err(mlua::Error::RuntimeError(format!(
                    "无法读取 {address:X}"
                )));
            }
            bytes_to_table(lua, &buffer)
        })
        .map_err(|e| format!("无法创建 readBytes：{e}"))?;
    globals
        .set("readBytes", read_bytes)
        .map_err(|e| format!("无法注册 readBytes：{e}"))?;

    let write_bytes_handle = handle.clone();
    let write_bytes = lua
        .create_function(move |_, (address, bytes): (Value, Value)| {
            let address = value_to_address(&address).ok_or_else(|| {
                mlua::Error::RuntimeError("writeBytes 需要地址".to_string())
            })?;
            let payload = value_to_bytes(&bytes).map_err(mlua::Error::RuntimeError)?;
            let Some(handle) = write_bytes_handle.as_ref() else {
                return Err(mlua::Error::RuntimeError("没有连接进程".to_string()));
            };
            if !handle.write(address, &payload) {
                return Err(mlua::Error::RuntimeError(format!(
                    "无法写入 {address:X}（只读或权限不足）"
                )));
            }
            Ok(())
        })
        .map_err(|e| format!("无法创建 writeBytes：{e}"))?;
    globals
        .set("writeBytes", write_bytes)
        .map_err(|e| format!("无法注册 writeBytes：{e}"))?;

    // getAddress: "module+offset" against the target's modules.
    let addr_handle = handle.clone();
    let get_address = lua
        .create_function(move |_, text: String| {
            let Some(handle) = addr_handle.as_ref() else {
                return Err(mlua::Error::RuntimeError("没有连接进程".to_string()));
            };
            let modules = handle.modules();
            let pairs: Vec<(String, u64)> = modules.iter().map(|m| (m.name.clone(), m.base)).collect();
            match crate::ctable::resolve_address(&text, &pairs, handle.is_64bit, &|a| {
                handle.read_pointer(a)
            }) {
                Ok(address) => Ok(address),
                Err(e) => Err(mlua::Error::RuntimeError(e)),
            }
        })
        .map_err(|e| format!("无法创建 getAddress：{e}"))?;
    globals
        .set("getAddress", get_address)
        .map_err(|e| format!("无法注册 getAddress：{e}"))?;

    let result = lua
        .load(source)
        .set_name("cheat script")
        .exec();

    let mut collected = output.lock().map(|o| o.clone()).unwrap_or_default();
    match result {
        Ok(()) => Ok(RunResult {
            output: std::mem::take(&mut collected),
            returned: None,
        }),
        Err(e) => Err(format!("{e}")),
    }
}

fn format_value(value: &Value) -> String {
    match value {
        Value::Nil => "nil".to_string(),
        Value::Boolean(b) => b.to_string(),
        Value::Integer(i) => i.to_string(),
        Value::Number(n) => {
            if n.fract() == 0.0 {
                format!("{n:.0}")
            } else {
                format!("{n}")
            }
        }
        Value::String(s) => s.to_string_lossy().to_string(),
        other => format!("{other:?}"),
    }
}

/// Accept a number or a hex string as an address.
fn value_to_address(value: &Value) -> Option<u64> {
    match value {
        Value::Integer(i) => Some(*i as u64),
        Value::Number(n) => Some(*n as u64),
        Value::String(s) => parse_address_text(&s.to_string_lossy()),
        _ => None,
    }
}
```

`app/src/main.rs`:

```rs
#![windows_subsystem = "windows"]

mod aob;
mod assembler;
mod ctable;
mod app;
mod disasm;
mod e2e;
mod focus;
mod hotkey;
mod inj;
mod lua;
mod mcp;
mod model;
mod pointerscan;
mod practice;
mod scan;
mod selftest;
mod session;
mod smart;
mod speed;
mod syms;
mod theme;
mod triage;
mod ui;
mod winmem;

use app::{Tab, ZeroApp};
use eframe::egui;
use std::path::PathBuf;

fn main() -> eframe::Result<()> {
    let args: Vec<String> = std::env::args().collect();
    if args.iter().any(|a| a == "--practice-target") {
        return practice::run();
    }
    if let Some(pos) = args.iter().position(|a| a == "--e2e") {
        // --e2e <pid> <info-file>
        let pid = args.get(pos + 1).and_then(|s| s.parse::<u32>().ok());
        let info = args.get(pos + 2).cloned().unwrap_or_else(|| "e2e_target.info".to_string());
        let ok = match pid {
            Some(pid) => e2e::run(pid, &info),
            None => {
                println!("usage: zero-scanner --e2e <pid> [info-file]");
                false
            }
        };
        std::process::exit(if ok { 0 } else { 1 });
    }
    if let Some(pos) = args.iter().position(|a| a == "--verify") {
        // --verify <pid>: the same one-click check the UI runs.
        let ok = match args.get(pos + 1).and_then(|s| s.parse::<u32>().ok()) {
            Some(pid) => match winmem::open_process(pid, "verify") {
                Ok(handle) => {
                    let (log, ok) = app::ZeroApp::verify_process(&handle, &handle.name);
                    std::fs::write("verify.log", &log).ok();
                    println!("{log}");
                    ok
                }
                Err(e) => {
                    eprintln!("无法打开进程 {pid}：{e}");
                    false
                }
            },
            None => {
                eprintln!("usage: zero-scanner --verify <pid>");
                false
            }
        };
        std::process::exit(if ok { 0 } else { 1 });
    }
    if let Some(pos) = args.iter().position(|a| a == "--mcp") {
        // Headless MCP service: useful for testing and for running Zero as a
        // pure backend without the window.
        let port = args
            .get(pos + 1)
            .and_then(|p| p.parse::<u16>().ok())
            .unwrap_or(3777);
        match mcp::start(port) {
            Ok(service) => {
                println!("MCP listening on {}", service.url());
                println!("Press Ctrl+C to stop.");
                loop {
                    std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_secs(3600));
                }
            }
            Err(e) => {
                eprintln!("{e}");
                std::process::exit(1);
            }
        }
    }
    if args.iter().any(|a| a == "--selftest") {
        let ok = selftest::run();
        std::process::exit(if ok { 0 } else { 1 });
    }

    let screenshot = parse_screenshot(&args);
    if let Some(path) = &screenshot {
        if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {
            let _ = std::fs::create_dir_all(parent);
        }
    }

    let mut viewport = egui::ViewportBuilder::default()
        .with_inner_size([1440.0, 960.0])
        .with_min_inner_size([1120.0, 720.0])
        .with_title("Zero · 智能内存工作台")
        .with_active(screenshot.is_none())
        .with_icon(app_icon());

    #[cfg(target_os = "windows")]
    if screenshot.is_some() {
        viewport = viewport.with_window_level(egui::WindowLevel::AlwaysOnBottom);
    }

    let options = eframe::NativeOptions {
        viewport,
        centered: true,
        vsync: true,
        ..Default::default()
    };

    let path = screenshot.clone();
    let demo = args.iter().any(|a| a == "--demo");
    let show_hotkeys = args.iter().any(|a| a == "--hotkeys");
    let show_settings = args.iter().any(|a| a == "--settings");
    let initial_tab = parse_tab(&args);
    eframe::run_native(
        "Zero",
        options,
        Box::new(move |cc| {
            let mut app = ZeroApp::new(cc, path.clone(), initial_tab, demo);
            app.show_hotkeys = show_hotkeys;
            app.show_settings = show_settings;
            Box::new(app)
        }),
    )
}

fn parse_tab(args: &[String]) -> Option<Tab> {
    let mut i = 0;
    while i < args.len() {
        let value = if args[i] == "--tab" {
            args.get(i + 1).map(|s| s.as_str())
        } else {
            args[i].strip_prefix("--tab=")
        };
        if let Some(value) = value {
            return match value {
                "scan" => Some(Tab::Scan),
                "ptr" | "pointer" => Some(Tab::PtrScan),
                "aob" => Some(Tab::Aob),
                "list" => Some(Tab::List),
                "memory" => Some(Tab::Memory),
                "assistant" | "smart" => Some(Tab::Assistant),
                "practice" => Some(Tab::Practice),
                "symbols" | "syms" => Some(Tab::Symbols),
                "disasm" | "code" => Some(Tab::Disasm),
                "inject" | "patch" => Some(Tab::Inject),
                "asm" | "assemble" => Some(Tab::Asm),
                "table" | "ct" => Some(Tab::Table),
                "speed" => Some(Tab::Speed),
                "script" | "lua" => Some(Tab::Script),
                _ => Some(Tab::Scan),
            };
        }
        i += 1;
    }
    None
}

fn parse_screenshot(args: &[String]) -> Option<PathBuf> {
    let mut i = 1;
    while i < args.len() {
        let arg = &args[i];
        if arg == "--screenshot" {
            if let Some(path) = args.get(i + 1) {
                return Some(PathBuf::from(path));
            }
            return Some(PathBuf::from("screenshot.png"));
        }
        if let Some(value) = arg.strip_prefix("--screenshot=") {
            return Some(PathBuf::from(value));
        }
        i += 1;
    }
    None
}

fn app_icon() -> egui::IconData {
    let size = 64usize;
    let mut rgba = vec![0u8; size * size * 4];
    for y in 0..size {
        for x in 0..size {
            let t = (x + y) as f32 / (2.0 * size as f32);
            let r = (92.0 + (52.0 - 92.0) * t) as u8;
            let g = (140.0 + (211.0 - 140.0) * t) as u8;
            let b = (255.0 + (190.0 - 255.0) * t) as u8;
            let edge = {
                let cx = x as f32 - 31.5;
                let cy = y as f32 - 31.5;
                let d = (cx * cx + cy * cy).sqrt();
                if d > 30.0 {
                    0
                } else if d > 28.5 {
                    180
                } else {
                    255
                }
            };
            let idx = (y * size + x) * 4;
            rgba[idx] = r;
            rgba[idx + 1] = g;
            rgba[idx + 2] = b;
            rgba[idx + 3] = edge;
            let in_z = (x as i32 - y as i32).abs() < 5
                && x > 17
                && x < 47
                && y > 17
                && y < 47;
            if in_z && edge > 0 {
                rgba[idx] = 9;
                rgba[idx + 1] = 18;
                rgba[idx + 2] = 29;
                rgba[idx + 3] = 255;
            }
        }
    }
    egui::IconData {
        rgba,
        width: size as u32,
        height: size as u32,
    }
}
```

`app/src/mcp.rs`:

```rs
//! A toggleable MCP (Model Context Protocol) service.
//!
//! Zero is a windowed app, so it cannot be launched as a stdio MCP server the
//! way most clients expect. Instead this runs a small HTTP server on localhost
//! that speaks MCP: press the toggle to start it, point an AI agent at the URL,
//! press it again to stop. Nothing listens while it is off.
//!
//! The server is deliberately self-contained. It owns its own engine session
//! (its own process handle, its own scan state) rather than reaching into the
//! GUI: sharing mutable state between a network thread and the UI thread is the
//! kind of thing that works until it does not.
//!
//! Transport: MCP over HTTP with JSON-RPC 2.0. Requests are POSTed to /mcp and
//! answered with a JSON body. That covers what local agent clients need without
//! pulling in an async runtime.

use crate::ctable;
use crate::inj;
use crate::model::{ScanHit, ScanType, VType};
use crate::scan::{self, ScanConfig};
use crate::triage;
use crate::winmem::{self, ProcessHandle};
use serde_json::{json, Value as Json};
use std::io::{Read, Write};
use std::net::{TcpListener, TcpStream};
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering};
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
use std::time::{Duration, SystemTime};

/// The running service's handle to its thread.
pub struct Service {
    pub port: u16,
    pub token: String,
    stop: Arc<AtomicBool>,
    /// Set once the listener is bound, so the UI can report a real port.
    pub bound: Arc<AtomicBool>,
}

impl Service {
    pub fn url(&self) -> String {
        format!("http://127.0.0.1:{}/mcp?token={}", self.port, self.token)
    }

    pub fn stop(&self) {
        self.stop.store(true, Ordering::Relaxed);
        // Nudge the accept loop: a connecting socket wakes it out of accept().
        let _ = TcpStream::connect(("127.0.0.1", self.port));
    }
}

/// Per-session engine state owned by the MCP service.
struct Engine {
    handle: Option<Arc<ProcessHandle>>,
    hits: Vec<ScanHit>,
    last_kind: String,
    addresses: Vec<(String, u64, VType)>,
    feedback: Vec<triage::RejectedTrait>,
    /// A watch session that outlives one request. An agent has to be able to
    /// start watching, go change the value in the game, then come back and ask
    /// what moved; a single blocking call cannot do that.
    watch: Option<WatchSession>,
    /// Every write this session made, kept so it can be undone. The GUI has a
    /// patch list for exactly this reason; an MCP client that can write without
    /// being able to restore is worse than one that cannot write at all.
    patches: Vec<inj::Patch>,
}

/// A background watch: its own thread keeps sampling while the agent works.
struct WatchSession {
    watches: Arc<Mutex<Vec<crate::focus::Watch>>>,
    stop: Arc<AtomicBool>,
    started: std::time::Instant,
}

impl Engine {
    fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            handle: None,
            hits: Vec::new(),
            last_kind: String::new(),
            addresses: Vec::new(),
            feedback: Vec::new(),
            watch: None,
            patches: Vec::new(),
        }
    }
}

/// Start the service on a port. Returns as soon as the listener is bound.
pub fn start(port: u16) -> Result<Service, String> {
    let listener = TcpListener::bind(("127.0.0.1", port))
        .map_err(|e| format!("端口 {port} 无法绑定：{e}"))?;
    let actual = listener
        .local_addr()
        .map(|a| a.port())
        .unwrap_or(port);
    let token = make_token();
    let stop = Arc::new(AtomicBool::new(false));
    let bound = Arc::new(AtomicBool::new(true));
    let engine = Arc::new(Mutex::new(Engine::new()));

    let stop_thread = stop.clone();
    let token_thread = token.clone();
    std::thread::Builder::new()
        .name("zero-mcp".to_string())
        .spawn(move || {
            for stream in listener.incoming() {
                if stop_thread.load(Ordering::Relaxed) {
                    break;
                }
                let Ok(stream) = stream else { continue };
                let engine = engine.clone();
                let token = token_thread.clone();
                // One thread per request: an agent may pipeline calls, and a
                // slow scan must not block the next request.
                std::thread::spawn(move || {
                    let _ = handle_connection(stream, &engine, &token);
                });
            }
        })
        .map_err(|e| format!("无法启动 MCP 线程：{e}"))?;

    Ok(Service {
        port: actual,
        token,
        stop,
        bound,
    })
}

/// A token that is unguessable enough for a loopback service.
///
/// Always available, not only in debug builds: the --selftest entry point is a
/// runtime check, so it has to compile into release builds too.
pub fn make_token_for_test() -> String {
    make_token()
}

pub fn tool_definitions_for_test() -> Vec<Json> {
    tool_definitions()
}

fn make_token() -> String {
    // A rotl on a 128-bit value drops the high bits, which left the first
    // thirteen hex digits zero in an earlier version. Mix with a proper
    // avalanche instead: xor-fold the shifted halves, then run a splitmix
    // style finaliser twice.
    let nanos = SystemTime::now()
        .duration_since(SystemTime::UNIX_EPOCH)
        .map(|d| d.as_nanos())
        .unwrap_or(0);
    let pid = std::process::id() as u128;
    let seed = nanos ^ (pid << 64) ^ 0x9E37_79B9_7F4A_7C15_1234_5678_9ABC_DEF1u128;

    let mut state = (seed ^ (seed >> 64)) as u64;
    let mut out = String::with_capacity(32);
    for _ in 0..2 {
        state = state.wrapping_add(0x9E37_79B9_7F4A_7C15);
        let mut z = state;
        z = (z ^ (z >> 30)).wrapping_mul(0xBF58_476D_1CE4_E5B9);
        z = (z ^ (z >> 27)).wrapping_mul(0x94D0_49BB_1331_11EB);
        z ^= z >> 31;
        out.push_str(&format!("{z:016x}"));
    }
    out
}

fn handle_connection(
    mut stream: TcpStream,
    engine: &Arc<Mutex<Engine>>,
    token: &str,
) -> std::io::Result<()> {
    stream.set_read_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(10)))?;
    // Read headers first, then exactly Content-Length bytes.
    let mut buffer: Vec<u8> = Vec::new();
    let mut chunk = [0u8; 4096];
    let header_end = loop {
        if let Some(pos) = find_subsequence(&buffer, b"\r\n\r\n") {
            break pos + 4;
        }
        let read = stream.read(&mut chunk)?;
        if read == 0 {
            return Ok(());
        }
        buffer.extend_from_slice(&chunk[..read]);
        if buffer.len() > 1_000_000 {
            return write_response(&mut stream, 413, "text/plain", "request too large");
        }
    };
    let header_text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&buffer[..header_end]).to_string();
    let request_line = header_text.lines().next().unwrap_or("").to_string();
    let content_length = header_text
        .lines()
        .find_map(|line| {
            let lower = line.to_ascii_lowercase();
            lower
                .strip_prefix("content-length:")
                .and_then(|v| v.trim().parse::<usize>().ok())
        })
        .unwrap_or(0);
    let mut body = buffer[header_end..].to_vec();
    while body.len() < content_length {
        let read = stream.read(&mut chunk)?;
        if read == 0 {
            break;
        }
        body.extend_from_slice(&chunk[..read]);
    }

    // Every request must carry the token: this is the only thing standing
    // between a local port and any other program on the machine.
    if !request_line.contains(token) && !header_text.contains(token) {
        return write_response(&mut stream, 401, "text/plain", "unauthorized");
    }

    if request_line.starts_with("GET ") {
        // Health check: lets a client verify the service is up without a token
        // in the body.
        let body = json!({
            "service": "zero-scanner",
            "protocol": "mcp",
            "transport": "http",
        })
        .to_string();
        return write_response(&mut stream, 200, "application/json", &body);
    }
    if !request_line.starts_with("POST ") {
        return write_response(&mut stream, 405, "text/plain", "method not allowed");
    }

    let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&body).to_string();
    let Ok(request) = serde_json::from_str::<Json>(&text) else {
        let body = json!({
            "jsonrpc": "2.0",
            "id": Json::Null,
            "error": { "code": -32700, "message": "parse error" }
        })
        .to_string();
        return write_response(&mut stream, 200, "application/json", &body);
    };

    match dispatch(&request, engine) {
        Some(response) => write_response(&mut stream, 200, "application/json", &response.to_string()),
        // Notifications have no reply.
        None => write_response(&mut stream, 202, "text/plain", ""),
    }
}

fn write_response(
    stream: &mut TcpStream,
    status: u16,
    content_type: &str,
    body: &str,
) -> std::io::Result<()> {
    let reason = match status {
        200 => "OK",
        202 => "Accepted",
        401 => "Unauthorized",
        405 => "Method Not Allowed",
        413 => "Payload Too Large",
        _ => "OK",
    };
    let response = format!(
        "HTTP/1.1 {status} {reason}\r\nContent-Type: {content_type}\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\nConnection: close\r\n\r\n{body}",
        body.len()
    );
    stream.write_all(response.as_bytes())?;
    stream.flush()
}

fn find_subsequence(haystack: &[u8], needle: &[u8]) -> Option<usize> {
    if needle.is_empty() || haystack.len() < needle.len() {
        return None;
    }
    haystack
        .windows(needle.len())
        .position(|window| window == needle)
}

/// Handle one JSON-RPC message. Returns None for notifications.
fn dispatch(request: &Json, engine: &Arc<Mutex<Engine>>) -> Option<Json> {
    let method = request.get("method").and_then(|m| m.as_str()).unwrap_or("");
    let id = request.get("id").cloned();
    let params = request.get("params").cloned().unwrap_or(Json::Null);

    // Anything without an id is a notification and must not be answered.
    let id = id?;

    let result = match method {
        "initialize" => Ok(json!({
            "protocolVersion": "2024-11-05",
            "capabilities": { "tools": {} },
            "serverInfo": { "name": "zero-scanner", "version": "0.1.0" }
        })),
        "tools/list" => Ok(json!({ "tools": tool_definitions() })),
        "tools/call" => {
            let name = params.get("name").and_then(|n| n.as_str()).unwrap_or("");
            let args = params.get("arguments").cloned().unwrap_or(json!({}));
            match call_tool(name, &args, engine) {
                Ok(text) => Ok(json!({
                    "content": [{ "type": "text", "text": text }],
                    "isError": false
                })),
                Err(e) => Ok(json!({
                    "content": [{ "type": "text", "text": e }],
                    "isError": true
                })),
            }
        }
        "ping" => Ok(json!({})),
        other => Err(format!("unknown method: {other}")),
    };

    Some(match result {
        Ok(value) => json!({ "jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": id, "result": value }),
        Err(message) => json!({
            "jsonrpc": "2.0",
            "id": id,
            "error": { "code": -32601, "message": message }
        }),
    })
}
```
