```rs
fn parse_hex_address(text: &str) -> Option<u64> {
    let t = text.trim().trim_start_matches("0x").trim_start_matches("0X");
    u64::from_str_radix(t, 16).ok()
}

fn vtype_of(text: &str) -> VType {
    match text.trim().to_ascii_lowercase().as_str() {
        "i8" | "byte" => VType::I8,
        "i16" | "word" => VType::I16,
        "i64" | "qword" => VType::I64,
        "f32" | "float" => VType::F32,
        "f64" | "double" => VType::F64,
        "str" | "string" => VType::Str,
        "auto" => VType::Auto,
        _ => VType::I32,
    }
}

fn scan_type_of(text: &str) -> ScanType {
    match text.trim().to_ascii_lowercase().as_str() {
        "bigger" => ScanType::Bigger,
        "smaller" => ScanType::Smaller,
        "between" => ScanType::Between,
        "unknown" => ScanType::Unknown,
        "increased" => ScanType::Increased,
        "decreased" => ScanType::Decreased,
        "changed" => ScanType::Changed,
        "unchanged" => ScanType::Unchanged,
        _ => ScanType::Exact,
    }
}

fn engine_handle(engine: &Arc<Mutex<Engine>>) -> Result<Arc<ProcessHandle>, String> {
    let guard = engine.lock().map_err(|_| "引擎状态不可用".to_string())?;
    guard
        .handle
        .clone()
        .ok_or_else(|| "还没有连接进程，先调用 zero_attach".to_string())
}

fn call_tool(name: &str, args: &Json, engine: &Arc<Mutex<Engine>>) -> Result<String, String> {
    match name {
        "zero_attach" => {
            // Clients send numbers inconsistently: some serialise a numeric id
            // as a JSON number, some as a string. Accept both rather than
            // failing an attach over a formatting difference.
            let pid = args.get("pid").and_then(|p| {
                p.as_u64()
                    .map(|n| n as u32)
                    .or_else(|| p.as_str().and_then(|s| s.trim().parse::<u32>().ok()))
            });
            let resolved = match pid {
                Some(pid) => Some((pid, format!("pid {pid}"))),
                None => {
                    let wanted = args
                        .get("name")
                        .and_then(|n| n.as_str())
                        .unwrap_or("")
                        .to_ascii_lowercase();
                    if wanted.is_empty() {
                        return Err("需要 pid 或 name".to_string());
                    }
                    winmem::list_processes()
                        .into_iter()
                        .find(|p| p.name.to_ascii_lowercase().contains(&wanted))
                        .map(|p| (p.pid, p.name))
                }
            };
            let Some((pid, label)) = resolved else {
                return Err("没有找到这个进程".to_string());
            };
            let handle = winmem::open_process(pid, &label)?;
            let summary = format!(
                "已连接 {}（PID {}，{}，{}）",
                handle.name,
                handle.pid,
                if handle.is_64bit { "x64" } else { "x86" },
                if handle.writable { "可写" } else { "只读" }
            );
            let mut guard = engine.lock().map_err(|_| "引擎状态不可用".to_string())?;
            guard.handle = Some(handle);
            guard.hits.clear();
            guard.addresses.clear();
            guard.feedback.clear();
            Ok(summary)
        }
        "zero_status" => {
            let guard = engine.lock().map_err(|_| "引擎状态不可用".to_string())?;
            let attached = guard
                .handle
                .as_ref()
                .map(|h| format!("{}（PID {}）", h.name, h.pid))
                .unwrap_or_else(|| "未连接".to_string());
            let mut text = format!(
                "进程：{attached}\n候选：{} 个\n已保存地址：{} 个",
                guard.hits.len(),
                guard.addresses.len()
            );
            if !guard.addresses.is_empty() {
                text.push_str("\n\n地址列表：");
                for (desc, address, vtype) in &guard.addresses {
                    text.push_str(&format!("\n  {address:016X}  {}  {}", vtype.short(), desc));
                }
            }
            Ok(text)
        }
        "zero_scan" => {
            let handle = engine_handle(engine)?;
            let value = args.get("value").and_then(|v| v.as_str()).unwrap_or("").to_string();
            let vtype = vtype_of(args.get("vtype").and_then(|v| v.as_str()).unwrap_or("i32"));
            let mode = scan_type_of(args.get("mode").and_then(|v| v.as_str()).unwrap_or("exact"));
            let regions = handle.regions(false);
            if regions.is_empty() {
                return Err("目标进程没有可读内存区".to_string());
            }
            let cfg = ScanConfig {
                vtype,
                scan_type: mode,
                value1: value.clone(),
                value2: String::new(),
                fast: false,
                max_results: 400_000,
            };
            let job = scan::start_first_scan(handle.clone(), regions, cfg)?;
            let done = job
                .rx
                .recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(120))
                .map_err(|_| "扫描超时".to_string())?;
            if let Some(error) = done.error {
                return Err(error);
            }
            let count = done.hits.len();
            let mut guard = engine.lock().map_err(|_| "引擎状态不可用".to_string())?;
            guard.hits = done.hits;
            guard.last_kind = format!("{} {}", mode.label(), value);
            guard.feedback.clear();
            let hits = guard.hits.clone();
            drop(guard);
            Ok(format!(
                "扫描完成：{count} 个候选（{} {}）。\n\n{}",
                mode.label(),
                value,
                summarize_candidates(&handle, &hits)
            ))
        }
        "zero_next_scan" => {
            let handle = engine_handle(engine)?;
            let value = args.get("value").and_then(|v| v.as_str()).unwrap_or("").to_string();
            let mode = scan_type_of(args.get("mode").and_then(|v| v.as_str()).unwrap_or("exact"));
            let previous = {
                let guard = engine.lock().map_err(|_| "引擎状态不可用".to_string())?;
                guard.hits.clone()
            };
            if previous.is_empty() {
                return Err("还没有首次扫描结果，先调用 zero_scan".to_string());
            }
            let cfg = ScanConfig {
                vtype: VType::Auto,
                scan_type: mode,
                value1: value.clone(),
                value2: String::new(),
                fast: false,
                max_results: 400_000,
            };
            let job = scan::start_next_scan(handle.clone(), previous, cfg)?;
            let done = job
                .rx
                .recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(120))
                .map_err(|_| "扫描超时".to_string())?;
            if let Some(error) = done.error {
                return Err(error);
            }
            let count = done.hits.len();
            let mut guard = engine.lock().map_err(|_| "引擎状态不可用".to_string())?;
            guard.hits = done.hits;
            let hits = guard.hits.clone();
            drop(guard);
            Ok(format!(
                "再次扫描完成：{count} 个候选。\n\n{}",
                summarize_candidates(&handle, &hits)
            ))
        }
        "zero_candidates" => {
            let handle = engine_handle(engine)?;
            let limit = args
                .get("limit")
                .and_then(|l| l.as_u64())
                .unwrap_or(10)
                .clamp(1, 100) as usize;
            let hits = {
                let guard = engine.lock().map_err(|_| "引擎状态不可用".to_string())?;
                guard.hits.clone()
            };
            if hits.is_empty() {
                return Err("还没有扫描结果".to_string());
            }
            let regions: Vec<triage::RegionHint> = handle
                .regions(false)
                .into_iter()
                .map(|r| triage::RegionHint {
                    base: r.base,
                    size: r.size,
                    writable: r.writable,
                    executable: r.executable,
                })
                .collect();
            let modules: Vec<(String, u64, u64)> = handle
                .modules()
                .iter()
                .map(|m| (m.name.clone(), m.base, m.size))
                .collect();
            let ranked = triage::rank(&hits, &regions, &modules);
            let mut text = String::new();
            for candidate in ranked.iter().take(limit) {
                text.push_str(&format!(
                    "{:016X}  {}  {} 分\n    理由：{}\n",
                    candidate.hit.address,
                    candidate.hit.value.display(),
                    candidate.score,
                    candidate.headline()
                ));
            }
            Ok(text)
        }
        "zero_read_memory" => {
            let handle = engine_handle(engine)?;
            let address = args
                .get("address")
                .and_then(|a| a.as_str())
                .and_then(parse_hex_address)
                .ok_or_else(|| "地址格式不对".to_string())?;
            let size = args
                .get("size")
                .and_then(|s| s.as_u64())
                .unwrap_or(16)
                .clamp(1, 4096) as usize;
            let mut buffer = vec![0u8; size];
            if !handle.read(address, &mut buffer) {
                return Err(format!("无法读取 {address:016X}"));
            }
            let hex: Vec<String> = buffer.iter().map(|b| format!("{b:02X}")).collect();
            Ok(format!("{address:016X}: {}", hex.join(" ")))
        }
        "zero_write_memory" => {
            let handle = engine_handle(engine)?;
            let address = args
                .get("address")
                .and_then(|a| a.as_str())
                .and_then(parse_hex_address)
                .ok_or_else(|| "地址格式不对".to_string())?;
            let raw = args
                .get("bytes")
                .and_then(|b| b.as_str())
                .ok_or_else(|| "需要 bytes".to_string())?;
            let mut payload = Vec::new();
            for token in raw.split_whitespace() {
                let t = token.trim_start_matches("0x");
                if t.len() != 2 || !t.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_hexdigit()) {
                    return Err(format!("「{token}」不是有效字节"));
                }
                payload.push(u8::from_str_radix(t, 16).map_err(|_| "字节解析失败".to_string())?);
            }
            if payload.is_empty() {
                return Err("要写入的字节为空".to_string());
            }
            let patch = inj::apply(&handle, address, &payload, "mcp")?;
            let summary = format!(
                "已写入 {} 字节到 {address:016X}。原字节：{}",
                patch.size(),
                patch.original_hex()
            );
            // Record it before reporting success: a write the caller believes is
            // undoable must actually be undoable.
            let mut guard = engine.lock().map_err(|_| "引擎状态不可用".to_string())?;
            guard.patches.push(patch);
            let total = guard.patches.iter().filter(|p| p.active).count();
            Ok(format!("{summary}（当前 {} 个写入可用 zero_undo 还原）", total))
        }
        "zero_ct_table" => {
            let action = args.get("action").and_then(|a| a.as_str()).unwrap_or("read");
            // Every entry the table should contain: either the live address list
            // or whatever a previous read parsed.
            let mut table = ctable::CtTable::default();
            {
                let guard = engine.lock().map_err(|_| "引擎状态不可用".to_string())?;
                if action != "read" {
                    if guard.addresses.is_empty() {
                        return Err("地址列表是空的，先用 zero_add_address 添加".to_string());
                    }
                    for (description, address, vtype) in &guard.addresses {
                        table.entries.push(ctable::CtEntry {
                            description: description.clone(),
                            vtype: *vtype,
                            address: format!("{address:X}"),
                            value: None,
                            active: false,
                        });
                    }
                }
            }
            if action == "build" {
                let xml = ctable::to_xml(&table);
                return Ok(format!(
                    "由地址列表生成 {} 个条目：\n\n{xml}",
                    table.entries.len()
                ));
            }
            let path = args
                .get("path")
                .and_then(|p| p.as_str())
                .ok_or_else(|| "需要 path".to_string())?;
            if action == "write" {
                // Refuse to clobber an existing table unless asked: a .ct can
                // hold work that took a long time to narrow down.
                if std::path::Path::new(path).exists()
                    && !args
                        .get("overwrite")
                        .and_then(|o| o.as_bool())
                        .unwrap_or(false)
                {
                    return Err(format!(
                        "{path} 已存在。要覆盖请传 overwrite: true"
                    ));
                }
                let xml = ctable::to_xml(&table);
                std::fs::write(path, &xml).map_err(|e| format!("写不到 {path}：{e}"))?;
                return Ok(format!(
                    "已写入 {path}：{} 个条目，{} 字节",
                    table.entries.len(),
                    xml.len()
                ));
            }
            let source = std::fs::read_to_string(path)
                .map_err(|e| format!("读不到 {path}：{e}"))?;
            let table = ctable::parse(&source)?;
            if table.is_empty() {
                return Ok(format!("{path} 里没有条目。"));
            }
            let mut text = format!("{path}：{} 个条目\n", table.entries.len());
            for entry in &table.entries {
                text.push_str(&format!(
                    "  [{}] {}  {}  {}\n",
                    if entry.active { "x" } else { " " },
                    entry.description,
                    ctable::vtype_to_ct(entry.vtype),
                    entry.address
                ));
            }
            Ok(text)
        }
        "zero_lua" => {
            let source = args
                .get("source")
                .and_then(|s| s.as_str())
                .ok_or_else(|| "需要 source".to_string())?;
            let handle = engine_handle(engine)?;
            let result = crate::lua::run_script(source, Some(handle))?;
            let mut text = String::new();
            for line in &result.output {
                text.push_str(line);
                text.push('\n');
            }
            if let Some(value) = &result.returned {
                text.push_str(&format!("=> {value}\n"));
            }
            if text.is_empty() {
                text.push_str("脚本执行完毕，没有输出。");
            }
            Ok(text)
        }
        "zero_undo" => {
            let handle = engine_handle(engine)?;
            let which = args.get("address").and_then(|a| a.as_str());
            let target = match which {
                Some(text) => parse_hex_address(text)
                    .ok_or_else(|| "地址格式不对".to_string())?,
                None => 0,
            };
            let mut guard = engine.lock().map_err(|_| "引擎状态不可用".to_string())?;
            let active: Vec<usize> = guard
                .patches
                .iter()
                .enumerate()
                .filter(|(_, p)| p.active)
                .filter(|(_, p)| target == 0 || p.address == target)
                .map(|(index, _)| index)
                .collect();
            if active.is_empty() {
                return Ok(if target == 0 {
                    "没有需要还原的写入。".to_string()
                } else {
                    format!("{target:016X} 没有可还原的写入。")
                });
            }
            let mut restored = Vec::new();
            let mut failed = Vec::new();
            for index in active {
                let patch = &mut guard.patches[index];
                let address = patch.address;
                match inj::revert(&handle, patch) {
                    Ok(()) => restored.push(address),
                    Err(e) => failed.push(format!("{address:016X}: {e}")),
                }
            }
            let mut text = format!("已还原 {} 处写入。", restored.len());
            if !failed.is_empty() {
                text.push_str(&format!("\n失败 {} 处：\n  {}", failed.len(), failed.join("\n  ")));
            }
            Ok(text)
        }
        "zero_add_address" => {
            let address = args
                .get("address")
                .and_then(|a| a.as_str())
                .and_then(parse_hex_address)
                .ok_or_else(|| "地址格式不对".to_string())?;
            let description = args
                .get("description")
                .and_then(|d| d.as_str())
                .unwrap_or("MCP 添加")
                .to_string();
            let vtype = vtype_of(args.get("vtype").and_then(|v| v.as_str()).unwrap_or("i32"));
            let mut guard = engine.lock().map_err(|_| "引擎状态不可用".to_string())?;
            guard.addresses.push((description.clone(), address, vtype));
            Ok(format!("已添加 {address:016X}（{description}）"))
        }
        "zero_pointer_scan" => {
            let handle = engine_handle(engine)?;
            let address = args
                .get("address")
                .and_then(|a| a.as_str())
                .and_then(parse_hex_address)
                .ok_or_else(|| "地址格式不对".to_string())?;
            let depth = args
                .get("depth")
                .and_then(|d| d.as_u64())
                .unwrap_or(3)
                .clamp(1, 6) as usize;
            let max_offset = args
                .get("max_offset")
                .and_then(|m| m.as_u64())
                .unwrap_or(0x1000)
                .clamp(0x10, 0x10000);
            let limit = args
                .get("limit")
                .and_then(|l| l.as_u64())
                .unwrap_or(10)
                .clamp(1, 50) as usize;
            // Pointer scanning over every readable region is expensive; restrict
            // it to writable memory, which is where pointer fields actually live.
            let regions: Vec<_> = handle
                .regions(false)
                .into_iter()
                .filter(|r| r.writable)
                .collect();
            if regions.is_empty() {
                return Err("目标进程没有可写内存区".to_string());
            }
            let modules = handle.modules();
            let job = crate::pointerscan::start_scan(
                handle.clone(),
                regions,
                modules,
                address,
                depth,
                max_offset,
                2000,
            )?;
            let done = job
                .rx
                .recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(180))
                .map_err(|_| "指针扫描超时".to_string())?;
            if done.paths.is_empty() {
                return Ok(format!(
                    "没有找到通向 {address:016X} 的指针链。可以把 depth 调大，或确认这个地址确实由指针引用。"
                ));
            }
            let mut text = format!(
                "找到 {} 条指针链（只显示前 {limit} 条）：\n",
                done.paths.len()
            );
            for path in done.paths.iter().take(limit) {
                text.push_str(&format!("  {}\n", path.display()));
            }
            Ok(text)
        }
        "zero_disassemble" => {
            let handle = engine_handle(engine)?;
            let address = args
                .get("address")
                .and_then(|a| a.as_str())
                .and_then(parse_hex_address)
                .ok_or_else(|| "地址格式不对".to_string())?;
            let count = args
                .get("count")
                .and_then(|c| c.as_u64())
                .unwrap_or(20)
                .clamp(1, 200) as usize;
            // Read more than count*15 bytes so long instructions still fit.
            let window = (count * 15).clamp(64, 4096);
            let mut buffer = vec![0u8; window];
            if !handle.read(address, &mut buffer) {
                return Err(format!("无法读取 {address:016X}"));
            }
            let insns = crate::disasm::disassemble(&buffer, address, handle.is_64bit, count);
            if insns.is_empty() {
                return Err("这段内存无法解码成指令".to_string());
            }
            let mut text = String::new();
            for insn in &insns {
                text.push_str(&format!(
                    "{:016X}  {:<24}  {}\n",
                    insn.address,
                    insn.hex(),
                    insn.text
                ));
            }
            Ok(text)
        }
        "zero_watch" => {
            // action=start begins a background session and returns immediately;
            // action=status reads it; action=stop ends it. Blocking the call for
            // the whole duration made the tool useless: the agent could not
            // change the value in the game while it was sampling, so every
            // address looked static.
            let action = args
                .get("action")
                .and_then(|a| a.as_str())
                .unwrap_or("start");
            if action == "stop" {
                let mut guard = engine.lock().map_err(|_| "引擎状态不可用".to_string())?;
                if let Some(session) = guard.watch.take() {
                    session.stop.store(true, Ordering::Relaxed);
                    return Ok("已停止观察。".to_string());
                }
                return Ok("当前没有在观察。".to_string());
            }
            if action == "status" {
                let guard = engine.lock().map_err(|_| "引擎状态不可用".to_string())?;
                let Some(session) = &guard.watch else {
                    return Ok("当前没有在观察。用 action=start 开始。".to_string());
                };
                let watches = session.watches.lock().map_err(|_| "观察状态不可用".to_string())?;
                let elapsed = session.started.elapsed().as_secs_f32();
                let mut text = format!("已观察 {elapsed:.1} 秒：\n");
                for watch in watches.iter() {
                    text.push_str(&format!(
                        "  {:016X}  当前 {}  变化 {} 次  {}\n",
                        watch.address,
                        watch
                            .current
                            .as_ref()
                            .map(|v| v.display())
                            .unwrap_or_else(|| "?".to_string()),
                        watch.changes,
                        watch.behaviour()
                    ));
                }
                match crate::focus::best(&watches) {
                    Some(best) => text.push_str(&format!(
                        "\n最像是它：{:016X}（{} 次变化，{}）",
                        best.address,
                        best.changes,
                        best.behaviour()
                    )),
                    None => text.push_str(
                        "\n还没有任何候选发生变化。去游戏里改一下数值，再查一次。",
                    ),
                }
                return Ok(text);
            }

            let handle = engine_handle(engine)?;
            let limit = args
                .get("limit")
                .and_then(|l| l.as_u64())
                .unwrap_or(5)
                .clamp(1, 16) as usize;
            let hits = {
                let guard = engine.lock().map_err(|_| "引擎状态不可用".to_string())?;
                guard.hits.clone()
            };
            if hits.is_empty() {
                return Err("还没有扫描结果，先调用 zero_scan".to_string());
            }
            let regions: Vec<triage::RegionHint> = handle
                .regions(false)
                .into_iter()
                .map(|r| triage::RegionHint {
                    base: r.base,
                    size: r.size,
                    writable: r.writable,
                    executable: r.executable,
                })
                .collect();
            let modules: Vec<(String, u64, u64)> = handle
                .modules()
                .iter()
                .map(|m| (m.name.clone(), m.base, m.size))
                .collect();
            let ranked = triage::rank(&hits, &regions, &modules);
            let mut watches = crate::focus::from_candidates(&ranked, limit);
            if watches.is_empty() {
                return Err("没有可观察的候选".to_string());
            }
            // Take one baseline sample before handing the list to the thread.
            crate::focus::sample(&handle, &mut watches);
            let shared = Arc::new(Mutex::new(watches));
            let stop_flag = Arc::new(AtomicBool::new(false));
            let count = {
                let guard = shared.lock().map_err(|_| "观察状态不可用".to_string())?;
                guard.len()
            };
            // The sampling thread owns the list and never touches the engine
            // mutex, so a long watch cannot block other MCP calls.
            {
                let shared_thread = shared.clone();
                let stop_thread = stop_flag.clone();
                let handle_thread = handle.clone();
                std::thread::Builder::new()
                    .name("zero-mcp-watch".to_string())
                    .spawn(move || {
                        while !stop_thread.load(Ordering::Relaxed) {
                            std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(200));
                            if let Ok(mut list) = shared_thread.lock() {
                                crate::focus::sample(&handle_thread, &mut list);
                            }
                        }
                    })
                    .map_err(|e| format!("无法启动观察线程：{e}"))?;
            }
            let mut guard = engine.lock().map_err(|_| "引擎状态不可用".to_string())?;
            if let Some(previous) = guard.watch.take() {
                previous.stop.store(true, Ordering::Relaxed);
            }
            guard.watch = Some(WatchSession {
                watches: shared,
                stop: stop_flag,
                started: std::time::Instant::now(),
            });
            Ok(format!(
                "开始观察 {count} 个最可能的地址（{} 分档）。现在去游戏里改数值，然后用 action=status 回来查看谁跟着动了。",
                ranked.first().map(|c| c.score).unwrap_or(0)
            ))
        }
        other => Err(format!("未知工具：{other}")),
    }
}

fn summarize_candidates(handle: &ProcessHandle, hits: &[ScanHit]) -> String {
    if hits.is_empty() {
        return "没有匹配。可以让数值变化后再次扫描，或换一种数值类型。".to_string();
    }
    let regions: Vec<triage::RegionHint> = handle
        .regions(false)
        .into_iter()
        .map(|r| triage::RegionHint {
            base: r.base,
            size: r.size,
            writable: r.writable,
            executable: r.executable,
        })
        .collect();
    let modules: Vec<(String, u64, u64)> = handle
        .modules()
        .iter()
        .map(|m| (m.name.clone(), m.base, m.size))
        .collect();
    let ranked = triage::rank(hits, &regions, &modules);
    let strong = triage::strong_count(&ranked);
    let mut text = format!("其中 {strong} 个很可能。最靠前的几个：\n");
    for candidate in ranked.iter().take(5) {
        text.push_str(&format!(
            "  {:016X}  {}  {} 分  {}\n",
            candidate.hit.address,
            candidate.hit.value.display(),
            candidate.score,
            candidate.headline()
        ));
    }
    text
}

```

`app/src/model.rs`:

```rs
use std::fmt;

/// Value widths understood by the scanner. `Auto` is resolved per hit.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Debug, Hash)]
pub enum VType {
    Auto,
    I8,
    I16,
    I32,
    I64,
    F32,
    F64,
    Str,
}

impl VType {
    pub const SELECTABLE: [VType; 8] = [
        VType::Auto,
        VType::I32,
        VType::I64,
        VType::F32,
        VType::F64,
        VType::I16,
        VType::I8,
        VType::Str,
    ];

    pub fn label(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            VType::Auto => "自动",
            VType::I8 => "字节",
            VType::I16 => "2 字节",
            VType::I32 => "4 字节",
            VType::I64 => "8 字节",
            VType::F32 => "浮点",
            VType::F64 => "双精度",
            VType::Str => "字符串",
        }
    }

    pub fn short(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            VType::Auto => "AUTO",
            VType::I8 => "I8",
            VType::I16 => "I16",
            VType::I32 => "I32",
            VType::I64 => "I64",
            VType::F32 => "F32",
            VType::F64 => "F64",
            VType::Str => "TXT",
        }
    }

    pub fn size(self) -> usize {
        match self {
            VType::Auto => 0,
            VType::I8 | VType::Str => 1,
            VType::I16 => 2,
            VType::I32 | VType::F32 => 4,
            VType::I64 | VType::F64 => 8,
        }
    }

    pub fn concrete(self) -> &'static [VType] {
        match self {
            VType::Auto => &[VType::I32, VType::I64, VType::F32, VType::F64],
            VType::I8 => &[VType::I8],
            VType::I16 => &[VType::I16],
            VType::I32 => &[VType::I32],
            VType::I64 => &[VType::I64],
            VType::F32 => &[VType::F32],
            VType::F64 => &[VType::F64],
            VType::Str => &[VType::Str],
        }
    }

    pub fn parse(self, text: &str) -> Option<Value> {
        let text = text.trim();
        if text.is_empty() {
            return None;
        }
        match self {
            VType::Auto | VType::Str => Some(Value::Text(text.to_string())),
            VType::I8 => parse_int(text).map(|v| Value::Int(v as i8 as i64)),
            VType::I16 => parse_int(text).map(|v| Value::Int(v as i16 as i64)),
            VType::I32 => parse_int(text).map(|v| Value::Int(v as i32 as i64)),
            VType::I64 => parse_int(text).map(Value::Int),
            VType::F32 => text.parse::<f32>().ok().map(|v| Value::Float(v as f64)),
            VType::F64 => text.parse::<f64>().ok().map(Value::Float),
        }
    }

    pub fn decode(self, bytes: &[u8]) -> Option<Value> {
        match self {
            VType::Auto => None,
            VType::I8 => Some(Value::Int(i8::from_le_bytes([*bytes.first()?]) as i64)),
            VType::I16 => Some(Value::Int(i16::from_le_bytes(
                bytes.get(0..2)?.try_into().ok()?,
            ) as i64)),
            VType::I32 => Some(Value::Int(i32::from_le_bytes(
                bytes.get(0..4)?.try_into().ok()?,
            ) as i64)),
            VType::I64 => Some(Value::Int(i64::from_le_bytes(
                bytes.get(0..8)?.try_into().ok()?,
            ))),
            VType::F32 => Some(Value::Float(f32::from_le_bytes(
                bytes.get(0..4)?.try_into().ok()?,
            ) as f64)),
            VType::F64 => Some(Value::Float(f64::from_le_bytes(
                bytes.get(0..8)?.try_into().ok()?,
            ))),
            VType::Str => None,
        }
    }

    pub fn is_float(self) -> bool {
        matches!(self, VType::F32 | VType::F64)
    }
}

fn parse_int(text: &str) -> Option<i64> {
    let t = text.trim();
    let lower = t.to_ascii_lowercase();
    if lower.starts_with("0x") {
        i64::from_str_radix(&lower[2..], 16).ok()
    } else if lower.starts_with("-0x") {
        i64::from_str_radix(&lower[3..], 16).ok().map(|v| -v)
    } else {
        t.parse::<i64>().ok()
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub enum Value {
    Int(i64),
    Float(f64),
    Text(String),
}

impl Value {
    pub fn display(&self) -> String {
        match self {
            Value::Int(v) => v.to_string(),
            Value::Float(v) => format_float(*v),
            Value::Text(s) => {
                if s.chars().count() > 42 {
                    let cut: String = s.chars().take(40).collect();
                    format!("{cut}…")
                } else {
                    s.clone()
                }
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn number(&self) -> Option<f64> {
        match self {
            Value::Int(v) => Some(*v as f64),
            Value::Float(v) => Some(*v),
            Value::Text(_) => None,
        }
    }

    pub fn to_bytes(&self, ty: VType) -> Option<Vec<u8>> {
        match ty {
            VType::I8 => match self {
                Value::Int(v) => Some(vec![*v as i8 as u8]),
                _ => None,
            },
            VType::I16 => match self {
                Value::Int(v) => Some((*v as i16).to_le_bytes().to_vec()),
                _ => None,
            },
            VType::I32 => match self {
                Value::Int(v) => Some((*v as i32).to_le_bytes().to_vec()),
                _ => None,
            },
            VType::I64 => match self {
                Value::Int(v) => Some(v.to_le_bytes().to_vec()),
                _ => None,
            },
            VType::F32 => match self {
                Value::Float(v) => Some((*v as f32).to_le_bytes().to_vec()),
                _ => None,
            },
            VType::F64 => match self {
                Value::Float(v) => Some(v.to_le_bytes().to_vec()),
                _ => None,
            },
            VType::Str => match self {
                Value::Text(s) => Some(s.as_bytes().to_vec()),
                _ => None,
            },
            VType::Auto => None,
        }
    }

    pub fn approx_eq(&self, other: &Value) -> bool {
        match (self, other) {
            (Value::Float(a), Value::Float(b)) => {
                let scale = a.abs().max(b.abs()).max(1.0);
                (a - b).abs() <= 0.0001 * scale
            }
            (Value::Float(a), Value::Int(b)) => (*a - *b as f64).abs() <= 0.0001,
            (Value::Int(a), Value::Float(b)) => (*a as f64 - *b).abs() <= 0.0001,
            (Value::Int(a), Value::Int(b)) => a == b,
            (Value::Text(a), Value::Text(b)) => a == b,
            _ => false,
        }
    }
}

impl fmt::Display for Value {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        write!(f, "{}", self.display())
    }
}

pub fn format_float(v: f64) -> String {
    if !v.is_finite() {
        return v.to_string();
    }
    if v != 0.0 && (v.abs() >= 1.0e9 || v.abs() < 0.0001) {
        let s = format!("{v:.5e}");
        return s;
    }
    let mut s = format!("{v:.4}");
    while s.contains('.') && s.ends_with('0') {
        s.pop();
    }
    if s.ends_with('.') {
        s.pop();
    }
    if s == "-0" {
        s = "0".to_string();
    }
    s
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Debug)]
pub enum ScanType {
    Exact,
    Bigger,
    Smaller,
    Between,
    Unknown,
    Increased,
    Decreased,
    Changed,
    Unchanged,
}

impl ScanType {
    pub const PRIMARY: [ScanType; 5] = [
        ScanType::Exact,
        ScanType::Bigger,
        ScanType::Smaller,
        ScanType::Between,
        ScanType::Unknown,
    ];
    pub const RELATIVE: [ScanType; 4] = [
        ScanType::Increased,
        ScanType::Decreased,
        ScanType::Changed,
        ScanType::Unchanged,
    ];

    pub fn label(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            ScanType::Exact => "精确值",
            ScanType::Bigger => "大于",
            ScanType::Smaller => "小于",
            ScanType::Between => "数值范围",
            ScanType::Unknown => "未知初值",
            ScanType::Increased => "数值增加",
            ScanType::Decreased => "数值减少",
            ScanType::Changed => "数值变化",
            ScanType::Unchanged => "数值未变",
        }
    }

    pub fn needs_value(self) -> bool {
        !matches!(self, ScanType::Unknown | ScanType::Changed | ScanType::Unchanged)
    }

    pub fn needs_two(self) -> bool {
        matches!(self, ScanType::Between)
    }

    pub fn is_relative(self) -> bool {
        matches!(
            self,
            ScanType::Increased | ScanType::Decreased | ScanType::Changed | ScanType::Unchanged
        )
    }

    pub fn first_scan_ok(self) -> bool {
        !self.is_relative()
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct ScanHit {
    pub address: u64,
    pub vtype: VType,
    pub value: Value,
    pub previous: Option<Value>,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub struct Region {
    pub base: u64,
    pub size: u64,
    pub writable: bool,
    pub executable: bool,
}

impl Region {
    pub fn end(&self) -> u64 {
        self.base.saturating_add(self.size)
    }
    pub fn mb(&self) -> f64 {
        self.size as f64 / (1024.0 * 1024.0)
    }
}

pub fn parse_address(text: &str) -> Option<u64> {
    let t = text.trim().replace([' ', '_'], "");
    if t.is_empty() {
        return None;
    }
    let lower = t.to_ascii_lowercase();
    if lower.starts_with("0x") {
        u64::from_str_radix(&lower[2..], 16).ok()
    } else if lower.chars().any(|c| c.is_ascii_alphabetic()) {
        u64::from_str_radix(&lower, 16).ok()
    } else {
        lower.parse::<u64>().ok()
    }
}

pub fn format_bytes(bytes: u64) -> String {
    if bytes >= 1024 * 1024 * 1024 {
        format!("{:.2} GB", bytes as f64 / (1024.0 * 1024.0 * 1024.0))
    } else if bytes >= 1024 * 1024 {
        format!("{:.1} MB", bytes as f64 / (1024.0 * 1024.0))
    } else if bytes >= 1024 {
        format!("{:.1} KB", bytes as f64 / 1024.0)
    } else {
        format!("{bytes} B")
    }
}

pub struct ValueTypeParseError;

pub fn parse_smart_value(text: &str) -> Option<(String, f64)> {
    let mut start = None;
    let mut end = 0usize;
    let chars: Vec<char> = text.chars().collect();
    for (i, c) in chars.iter().enumerate() {
        if c.is_ascii_digit() || (*c == '-' && i + 1 < chars.len() && chars[i + 1].is_ascii_digit())
        {
            start = Some(i);
            end = i + 1;
            break;
        }
    }
    let s = start?;
    while end < chars.len()
        && (chars[end].is_ascii_digit() || matches!(chars[end], '.' | ',' | '_'))
    {
        end += 1;
    }
    let raw: String = chars[s..end]
        .iter()
        .filter(|c| **c != ',' && **c != '_')
        .collect();
    raw.parse::<f64>().ok().map(|v| (raw, v))
}
```

`app/src/pointerscan.rs`:

```rs
use crate::model::Region;
use crate::winmem::{ModuleInfo, ProcessHandle};
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, AtomicU64, Ordering};
use std::sync::{mpsc, Arc};
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

const CHUNK: u64 = 128 * 1024;
const ALIGNMENT: u64 = 4;

/// Where a pointer chain starts: a module plus a fixed offset (survives ASLR and
/// restarts) or, as a fallback, the module base itself.
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct StaticBase {
    pub module: String,
    pub module_base: u64,
    pub offset: u64,
}

impl StaticBase {
    pub fn address(&self) -> u64 {
        self.module_base.saturating_add(self.offset)
    }
    pub fn display(&self) -> String {
        if self.offset == 0 {
            format!("\"{}\"", self.module)
        } else {
            format!("\"{}\"+{:X}", self.module, self.offset)
        }
    }
}

/// One pointer chain: base -> [offset1, offset2, ...] -> final field.
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct PointerPath {
    pub base: StaticBase,
    pub offsets: Vec<u64>,
    pub final_address: u64,
    pub is_32bit: bool,
}

impl PointerPath {
    pub fn display(&self) -> String {
        let mut text = self.base.display();
        for offset in &self.offsets {
            if *offset == 0 {
                text.push_str(" -> +0");
            } else {
                text.push_str(&format!(" -> +{:X}", offset));
            }
        }
        text
    }

    pub fn depth(&self) -> usize {
        self.offsets.len()
    }
}

pub struct PtrHandle {
    pub rx: mpsc::Receiver<PtrDone>,
    pub progress: Arc<AtomicU64>,
    pub total: u64,
    pub cancel: Arc<AtomicBool>,
    pub started: Instant,
}

pub struct PtrDone {
    pub paths: Vec<PointerPath>,
    pub elapsed: Duration,
    pub cancelled: bool,
    pub truncated: bool,
    pub scanned_pointers: u64,
}

/// Result of the reverse walk: for every level, which addresses point into the
/// level below, and what value they store.
struct LevelLinks(HashMap<u64, Vec<(u64, u64)>>);

/// Find pointer chains that reach `target` within `max_depth` levels.
///
/// Pass 1 indexes every aligned pointer-sized value inside readable regions into
/// `value -> [locations]`. Pass 2 walks backwards from the target: for each level,
/// an address becomes a parent when some stored value plus an offset lands on a
/// level-below address. Chains whose head sits inside a loaded module are reported,
/// because `module + offset` is what survives a restart.
pub fn start_scan(
    handle: Arc<ProcessHandle>,
    regions: Vec<Region>,
    modules: Vec<ModuleInfo>,
    target: u64,
    max_depth: usize,
    max_offset: u64,
    max_results: usize,
) -> Result<PtrHandle, String> {
    if max_depth == 0 {
        return Err("层级至少为 1。".to_string());
    }
    if regions.is_empty() {
        return Err("这个进程没有可扫描的内存区域（试试取消「只扫描可写内存」）。".to_string());
    }
    if max_offset == 0 {
        return Err("最大偏移至少要为 1。".to_string());
    }
    let pointer_size: u64 = if handle.is_64bit { 8 } else { 4 };
    let total: u64 = regions.iter().map(|r| r.size).sum();
    let progress = Arc::new(AtomicU64::new(0));
    let cancel = Arc::new(AtomicBool::new(false));
    let started = Instant::now();
    let (tx, rx) = mpsc::channel();

    let progress_thread = progress.clone();
    let cancel_thread = cancel.clone();
    let is_64bit = handle.is_64bit;
    let max_offset = max_offset.min(0x1_0000);

    std::thread::Builder::new()
        .name("zero-pointer".to_string())
        .spawn(move || {
            // Pass 1: pointer value -> locations that store it.
            let mut value_map: HashMap<u64, Vec<u64>> = HashMap::new();
            let mut scanned: u64 = 0;
            let space = AddressSpace::new(&regions);
            'pass1: for region in &regions {
                if cancel_thread.load(Ordering::Relaxed) {
                    break;
                }
                let mut pos = 0u64;
                while pos < region.size {
                    if cancel_thread.load(Ordering::Relaxed) {
                        break 'pass1;
                    }
                    let read_len = CHUNK.min(region.size - pos);
                    let mut buf = vec![0u8; read_len as usize];
                    if handle.read(region.base + pos, &mut buf) {
                        collect_pointers(
                            &buf,
                            region.base + pos,
                            pointer_size,
                            &space,
                            &mut value_map,
                            &mut scanned,
                        );
                    }
                    progress_thread.fetch_add(read_len, Ordering::Relaxed);
                    pos += read_len;
                }
            }

            // Pass 2: reverse BFS from the target.
            let mut rev: Vec<Vec<u64>> = Vec::with_capacity(max_depth + 1);
            rev.push(vec![target]);
            let mut links: Vec<LevelLinks> = Vec::new();

            for level in 1..=max_depth {
                if cancel_thread.load(Ordering::Relaxed) {
                    break;
                }
                let previous = &rev[level - 1];
                let mut parents: Vec<u64> = Vec::new();
                let mut layer: HashMap<u64, Vec<(u64, u64)>> = HashMap::new();
                for &pointed in previous {
                    for off in 0..=max_offset {
                        let value = pointed.saturating_sub(off);
                        if let Some(locs) = value_map.get(&value) {
                            for &loc in locs {
                                parents.push(loc);
                                layer
                                    .entry(loc)
                                    .or_default()
                                    .push((pointed, value));
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                parents.sort_unstable();
                parents.dedup();
                if parents.is_empty() {
                    break;
                }
                links.push(LevelLinks(layer));
                rev.push(parents);
            }

            // Turn reachable addresses into module-anchored chains.
            let mut paths: Vec<PointerPath> = Vec::new();
            'level: for level in 1..rev.len() {
                let depth = level;
                let layer = &links[level - 1].0;
                for &candidate in &rev[level] {
                    let Some(module) = module_containing(&modules, candidate) else {
                        continue;
                    };
                    if let Some(offsets) = reconstruct(candidate, depth, &links) {
                        paths.push(PointerPath {
                            base: StaticBase {
                                module: module.name.clone(),
                                module_base: module.base,
                                offset: module.offset_of(candidate),
                            },
                            offsets,
                            final_address: target,
                            is_32bit: !is_64bit,
                        });
                    }
                    if paths.len() >= max_results {
                        break 'level;
                    }
                }
            }

            paths.sort_by(|a, b| {
                a.depth()
                    .cmp(&b.depth())
                    .then(a.base.module.cmp(&b.base.module))
                    .then(a.base.offset.cmp(&b.base.offset))
            });
            paths.dedup_by(|a, b| {
                a.base.module == b.base.module
                    && a.base.offset == b.base.offset
                    && a.offsets == b.offsets
            });
            let truncated = paths.len() >= max_results;
            paths.truncate(max_results);

            let _ = tx.send(PtrDone {
                paths,
                elapsed: started.elapsed(),
                cancelled: cancel_thread.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
                truncated,
                scanned_pointers: scanned,
            });
            progress_thread.fetch_add(total, Ordering::Relaxed);
        })
        .map_err(|e| format!("无法启动指针扫描线程：{e}"))?;

    Ok(PtrHandle {
        rx,
        progress,
        total: total.max(1),
        cancel,
        started,
    })
}

fn collect_pointers(
    buf: &[u8],
    base: u64,
    pointer_size: u64,
    space: &AddressSpace,
    map: &mut HashMap<u64, Vec<u64>>,
    scanned: &mut u64,
) {
    let step = pointer_size as usize;
    let mut offset = 0usize;
    while offset + step <= buf.len() {
        let address = base + offset as u64;
        if address % ALIGNMENT != 0 {
            offset += step;
            continue;
        }
        let value = if pointer_size == 8 {
            u64::from_le_bytes(buf[offset..offset + 8].try_into().unwrap())
        } else {
            u32::from_le_bytes(buf[offset..offset + 4].try_into().unwrap()) as u64
        };
        *scanned += 1;
        if value != 0 && space.is_plausible(value) {
            map.entry(value).or_default().push(address);
        }
        offset += step;
    }
}

/// Minimal address-space bounds used to drop junk pointer values.
struct AddressSpace {
    min: u64,
    max: u64,
}

impl AddressSpace {
    fn new(regions: &[Region]) -> Self {
        let min = regions.iter().map(|r| r.base).min().unwrap_or(0);
        let max = regions.iter().map(|r| r.end()).max().unwrap_or(0);
        Self {
            min: min & !0xFFFF,
            max,
        }
    }
    fn is_plausible(&self, value: u64) -> bool {
        value >= self.min && value <= self.max
    }
}

fn module_containing<'a>(modules: &'a [ModuleInfo], address: u64) -> Option<&'a ModuleInfo> {
    modules.iter().find(|m| m.contains(address))
}

/// Walk the stored links from a base address down to the target, collecting the
/// per-hop offsets (innermost first, then reversed so the list reads base->target).
fn reconstruct(base: u64, depth: usize, links: &[LevelLinks]) -> Option<Vec<u64>> {
    let mut offsets = Vec::with_capacity(depth);
    let mut current = base;
    for level in (1..=depth).rev() {
        let entry = links[level - 1].0.get(&current)?;
        let (child, value) = *entry.first()?;
        let off = child.saturating_sub(value);
        offsets.push(off);
        current = child;
    }
    offsets.reverse();
    Some(offsets)
}
```

```rs
use crate::theme;
use eframe::egui;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

pub struct PracticeApp {
    stats: Box<Stats>,
    auto: bool,
    last_tick: Instant,
    log: Vec<String>,
}

struct Stats {
    health: i32,
    ammo: i32,
    coins: i32,
    armor: f32,
}

impl PracticeApp {
    pub fn new(cc: &eframe::CreationContext<'_>) -> Self {
        theme::setup_fonts(&cc.egui_ctx);
        theme::apply_style(&cc.egui_ctx);
        Self {
            stats: Box::new(Stats {
                health: 100,
                ammo: 30,
                coins: 250,
                armor: 0.75,
            }),
            auto: false,
            last_tick: Instant::now(),
            log: vec!["靶场已就绪。回到 Zero，扫描这里看到的数值。".to_string()],
        }
    }

    fn note(&mut self, text: impl Into<String>) {
        self.log.push(text.into());
        if self.log.len() > 6 {
            self.log.remove(0);
        }
    }

    fn stat_card(&mut self, ui: &mut egui::Ui, title: &str, value: String, unit: &str, hint: &str, minus: &str, plus: &str, down: i32, up: i32) {
        theme::soft_card().show(ui, |ui| {
            ui.set_width(ui.available_width());
            ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                ui.vertical(|ui| {
                    theme::section_label(ui, title);
                    ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                        ui.label(
                            egui::RichText::new(value)
                                .size(34.0)
                                .strong()
                                .color(theme::TEXT),
                        );
                        ui.label(egui::RichText::new(unit).size(12.0).color(theme::FAINT));
                    });
                });
                ui.with_layout(egui::Layout::right_to_left(egui::Align::Center), |ui| {
                    ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                        if ui.add(egui::Button::new(plus).min_size(egui::vec2(34.0, 30.0))).clicked() {
                            let _ = up;
                        }
                        if ui.add(egui::Button::new(minus).min_size(egui::vec2(34.0, 30.0))).clicked() {
                            let _ = down;
                        }
                    });
                });
            });
            ui.label(egui::RichText::new(hint).size(11.5).color(theme::FAINT));
        });
    }
}

impl eframe::App for PracticeApp {
    fn update(&mut self, ctx: &egui::Context, _frame: &mut eframe::Frame) {
        if self.auto && self.last_tick.elapsed() > Duration::from_millis(1000) {
            self.last_tick = Instant::now();
            self.stats.health = (self.stats.health - 2).max(1);
            self.stats.coins += 3;
            self.note("自动模拟：生命 -2，金币 +3");

        ctx.request_repaint_after(Duration::from_millis(if self.auto { 250 } else { 1000 }));

        egui::CentralPanel::default()
            .frame(
                egui::Frame::none()
                    .fill(theme::BG)
                    .inner_margin(egui::Margin::same(22.0)),
            )
            .show(ctx, |ui| {
                theme::draw_background(ui);
                ui.horizontal(|ui| {

                            ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                                if ui.add(egui::Button::new("受伤 -10").min_size(egui::vec2(78.0, 30.0))).clicked() {
                                    self.stats.health = (self.stats.health - 10).max(1);

                            theme::section_label(ui, "弹药");

                                    self.stats.ammo = (self.stats.ammo + 30).min(90);
                                    self.note("弹药 +30");
                                }
                            });
                        });
                    });
                });
                ui.add_space(8.0);

                theme::soft_card().show(ui, |ui| {
                    ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                        ui.vertical(|ui| {
                            theme::section_label(ui, "金币");
                            ui.label(egui::RichText::new(self.stats.coins.to_string()).size(34.0).strong());
                        });

                                    self.stats.coins = (self.stats.coins - 100).max(0);
                                    self.note("金币 -100");

                        ui.vertical(|ui| {
                            theme::section_label(ui, "护甲（浮点练习）");
                            ui.label(

                                    .strong()
                                    .color(theme::ACCENT),

                        });
                        ui.with_layout(egui::Layout::right_to_left(egui::Align::Center), |ui| {
                            ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                                if ui.add(egui::Button::new("+0.05").min_size(egui::vec2(70.0, 30.0))).clicked() {
                                    self.stats.armor = (self.stats.armor + 0.05).min(1.0);
                                    self.note("护甲 +0.05");
                                }
                                if ui.add(egui::Button::new("-0.05").min_size(egui::vec2(70.0, 30.0))).clicked() {
                                    self.stats.armor = (self.stats.armor - 0.05).max(0.0);
                                    self.note("护甲 -0.05");
                                }
                            });
                        });
                    });
                });

                ui.add_space(10.0);
                theme::card().show(ui, |ui| {
                    theme::section_label(ui, "操作记录");
                    for line in self.log.iter().rev().take(4) {
                        ui.label(egui::RichText::new(format!("· {line}")).size(12.0).color(theme::MUTED));
                    }
                });

                ui.add_space(10.0);
                ui.label(
                    egui::RichText::new("提示：扫描时请选择本窗口的进程；数值变化后再回 Zero 用「数值减少 / 增加」再次扫描。")
                        .size(11.5)
                        .color(theme::FAINT),
                );
            });
    }
}

pub fn run() -> eframe::Result<()> {
    let options = eframe::NativeOptions {
        viewport: egui::ViewportBuilder::default()
            .with_inner_size([590.0, 760.0])
            .with_min_inner_size([520.0, 640.0])
            .with_title("Zero · 练习靶场"),
        ..Default::default()
    };
    eframe::run_native(
        "Zero 练习靶场",
        options,
        Box::new(|cc| Box::new(PracticeApp::new(cc))),
    )
}
```
