`app/src/speed.rs`:

```rs
//! Speed control: the classic speed hack.
//!
//! A process cannot be sped up from outside without changing its code, so the
//! trick is to stop all of its threads and let them run again on a schedule.
//! Stopping them for longer than real time makes the game slow down; stopping
//! them for less makes it speed up.
//!
//! This module owns the dangerous part (suspend/resume) and guarantees that
//! every thread it stops is released again: a stop that leaked a suspended
//! thread would leave the target frozen forever.

use std::mem::size_of;

use windows_sys::Win32::Foundation::{CloseHandle, INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE};
use windows_sys::Win32::System::Diagnostics::ToolHelp::{
    CreateToolhelp32Snapshot, Thread32First, Thread32Next, TH32CS_SNAPTHREAD, THREADENTRY32,
};
use windows_sys::Win32::System::Threading::{
    OpenThread, ResumeThread, SuspendThread, THREAD_SUSPEND_RESUME,
};

/// One thread of the target, tracked so it can always be resumed.
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct TargetThread {
    pub id: u32,
    /// How many times this thread has been suspended by us. Guests that were
    /// already suspended for their own reasons must not be resumed blindly,
    /// so the count is tracked per thread.
    pub suspend_count: u32,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Debug)]
pub enum SpeedState {
    /// Threads run normally.
    Off,
    /// Stopping them for `period`, letting them run for `run_for`.
    Active,
}

/// Configuration of a speed change.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub struct SpeedPlan {
    /// Desired multiplier: 0.5 = half speed, 2.0 = double speed.
    pub factor: f64,
}

impl SpeedPlan {
    /// Total period in milliseconds and the slice the threads are allowed to
    /// run. The ratio run/period equals the speed factor.
    pub fn timing(&self) -> (u64, u64) {
        // A 40 ms window keeps the stutter under ~25 fps worth of granularity.
        let period = 40u64;
        let factor = self.factor.clamp(0.01, 16.0);
        let run = ((period as f64) * factor / (1.0 + factor)).round() as u64;
        let run = run.clamp(1, period - 1);
        (period, run)
    }
}

/// Enumerate the thread ids of a process.
pub fn thread_ids(pid: u32) -> Vec<u32> {
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    unsafe {
        let snapshot = CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(TH32CS_SNAPTHREAD, 0);
        if snapshot == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE || snapshot == 0 {
            return out;
        }
        let mut entry: THREADENTRY32 = std::mem::zeroed();
        entry.dwSize = size_of::<THREADENTRY32>() as u32;
        let mut ok = Thread32First(snapshot, &mut entry);
        while ok != 0 {
            if entry.th32OwnerProcessID == pid {
                out.push(entry.th32ThreadID);
            }
            ok = Thread32Next(snapshot, &mut entry);
        }
        CloseHandle(snapshot);
    }
    out.sort_unstable();
    out
}

/// Suspend every thread of the process. Returns the threads actually stopped,
/// so a later resume only touches those.
///
/// Suspending our own process would stop the very thread doing the suspending:
/// the UI would freeze and nothing could ever resume it. That is a hard error,
/// not a warning, because the process cannot recover from it by itself.
pub fn suspend_all(pid: u32) -> Result<Vec<TargetThread>, String> {
    if pid == std::process::id() {
        return Err("不能挂起 Zero 自己：那样会把执行挂起的线程一起停掉。".to_string());
    }
    let mut stopped = Vec::new();
    for id in thread_ids(pid) {
        unsafe {
            let handle = OpenThread(THREAD_SUSPEND_RESUME, 0, id);
            if handle == 0 {
                continue;
            }
            // A negative return means the call failed.
            if SuspendThread(handle) != u32::MAX {
                stopped.push(TargetThread {
                    id,
                    suspend_count: 1,
                });
            }
            CloseHandle(handle);
        }
    }
    Ok(stopped)
}

/// Resume exactly the threads in `threads`, never more.
pub fn resume_all(threads: &[TargetThread]) {
    for thread in threads {
        for _ in 0..thread.suspend_count {
            unsafe {
                let handle = OpenThread(THREAD_SUSPEND_RESUME, 0, thread.id);
                if handle == 0 {
                    continue;
                }
                ResumeThread(handle);
                CloseHandle(handle);
            }
        }
    }
}

/// One step of the speed loop: how long to stop, how long to run.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub struct Tick {
    pub stop_ms: u64,
    pub run_ms: u64,
}

impl Tick {
    pub fn from_plan(plan: SpeedPlan) -> Self {
        let (period, run) = plan.timing();
        Self {
            stop_ms: period - run,
            run_ms: run,
        }
    }
}
```

`app/src/syms.rs`:

```rs
//! Symbol resolution: parse a loaded module's PE export and import tables.
//!
//! Cheat Engine names addresses through symbols (exports and imports) instead of
//! raw numbers. Everything here reads the target process's own mapped image, so
//! there is no file parsing and no extra privilege beyond the open handle.

use crate::winmem::{ModuleInfo, ProcessHandle};

const MAX_NAME: usize = 512;
const MAX_EXPORTS: u32 = 500_000;
const MAX_IMPORT_LIBS: u64 = 4096;
const MAX_IMPORTS_PER_LIB: u64 = 100_000;

/// One exported or imported function.
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct Symbol {
    pub name: String,
    /// Virtual address in the target process. Forwarded exports have no local
    /// address, so this is 0 and `forwarded` carries the real target.
    pub address: u64,
    pub ordinal: u32,
    /// "other.dll!Function" when the export is a forwarder.
    pub forwarded: Option<String>,
}

impl Symbol {
    pub fn display_name(&self) -> String {
        match &self.forwarded {
            Some(target) => format!("{} -> {}", self.name, target),
            None => self.name.clone(),
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct ImportLib {
    pub dll: String,
    pub symbols: Vec<Symbol>,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct SymbolTable {
    pub module: String,
    pub base: u64,
    pub exports: Vec<Symbol>,
    pub imports: Vec<ImportLib>,
}

impl SymbolTable {
    pub fn import_count(&self) -> usize {
        self.imports.iter().map(|lib| lib.symbols.len()).sum()
    }

    /// Name a virtual address through this module's exports, when it lands on one.
    pub fn resolve(&self, address: u64) -> Option<String> {
        self.exports
            .iter()
            .find(|sym| sym.address == address)
            .map(|sym| format!("{}!{}", self.module, sym.name))
    }

    /// Closest export at or below `address`: "module!Name+0x12" style symbolization.
    pub fn nearest(&self, address: u64) -> Option<String> {
        let mut best: Option<&Symbol> = None;
        for sym in &self.exports {
            if sym.address == 0 || sym.address > address {
                continue;
            }
            match best {
                Some(current) if current.address >= sym.address => {}
                _ => best = Some(sym),
            }
        }
        best.map(|sym| {
            let offset = address.saturating_sub(sym.address);
            if offset == 0 {
                format!("{}!{}", self.module, sym.name)
            } else {
                format!("{}!{}+{:X}", self.module, sym.name, offset)
            }
        })
    }
}

fn read_bytes(handle: &ProcessHandle, address: u64, len: usize) -> Option<Vec<u8>> {
    let mut buffer = vec![0u8; len];
    if len == 0 || handle.read(address, &mut buffer) {
        Some(buffer)
    } else {
        None
    }
}

fn read_u16(handle: &ProcessHandle, address: u64) -> Option<u16> {
    let bytes = read_bytes(handle, address, 2)?;
    Some(u16::from_le_bytes([bytes[0], bytes[1]]))
}

fn read_u32(handle: &ProcessHandle, address: u64) -> Option<u32> {
    let bytes = read_bytes(handle, address, 4)?;
    Some(u32::from_le_bytes([bytes[0], bytes[1], bytes[2], bytes[3]]))
}

fn read_u64(handle: &ProcessHandle, address: u64) -> Option<u64> {
    let bytes = read_bytes(handle, address, 8)?;
    let mut raw = [0u8; 8];
    raw.copy_from_slice(&bytes[..8]);
    Some(u64::from_le_bytes(raw))
}

/// Read a NUL-terminated ASCII string from the target process.
fn read_cstr(handle: &ProcessHandle, address: u64) -> Option<String> {
    let mut out: Vec<u8> = Vec::new();
    let mut cursor = address;
    loop {
        let chunk = read_bytes(handle, cursor, 64)?;
        for byte in chunk {
            if byte == 0 {
                return String::from_utf8(out).ok();
            }
            out.push(byte);
            if out.len() > MAX_NAME {
                return None;
            }
        }
        cursor = cursor.checked_add(64)?;
    }
}

struct OptionalHeader {
    /// Absolute address of the first data directory entry (optional header start
    /// plus the format's data directory offset).
    dir_address: u64,
    dir_count: usize,
}

fn optional_header(handle: &ProcessHandle, image_base: u64) -> Result<OptionalHeader, String> {
    let dos = read_bytes(handle, image_base, 0x40)
        .ok_or_else(|| "无法读取模块的 DOS 头。".to_string())?;
    if &dos[0..2] != b"MZ" {
        return Err("模块开头不是 MZ，可能不是 PE 映像。".to_string());
    }
    let mut lfanew = [0u8; 4];
    lfanew.copy_from_slice(&dos[0x3C..0x40]);
    let nt = image_base
        .checked_add(u32::from_le_bytes(lfanew) as u64)
        .ok_or_else(|| "DOS 头的 PE 偏移无效。".to_string())?;
    let signature = read_bytes(handle, nt, 4).ok_or_else(|| "无法读取 PE 签名。".to_string())?;
    if signature != b"PE\0\0" {
        return Err("PE 签名不匹配。".to_string());
    }
    let coff = read_bytes(handle, nt + 4, 20).ok_or_else(|| "无法读取 COFF 头。".to_string())?;
    let size_optional = u16::from_le_bytes([coff[16], coff[17]]) as usize;
    if size_optional < 0x40 || size_optional > 0x1000 {
        return Err("可选头大小异常。".to_string());
    }
    let optional = read_bytes(handle, nt + 24, size_optional)
        .ok_or_else(|| "无法读取可选头。".to_string())?;
    let magic = u16::from_le_bytes([optional[0], optional[1]]);
    let (dir_offset, dir_count_offset) = match magic {
        0x10B => (96usize, 92usize),  // PE32
        0x20B => (112usize, 108usize), // PE32+
        other => return Err(format!("未知的可选头格式 0x{other:X}。")),
    };
    if optional.len() < dir_offset {
        return Err("可选头不足以容纳数据目录。".to_string());
    }
    let optional_address = nt
        .checked_add(24)
        .ok_or_else(|| "PE 头偏移无效。".to_string())?;
    let dir_address = optional_address
        .checked_add(dir_offset as u64)
        .ok_or_else(|| "可选头偏移无效。".to_string())?;
    let mut count_bytes = [0u8; 4];
    count_bytes.copy_from_slice(&optional[dir_count_offset..dir_count_offset + 4]);
    Ok(OptionalHeader {
        dir_address,
        dir_count: u32::from_le_bytes(count_bytes) as usize,
    })
}

fn data_directory(handle: &ProcessHandle, header: &OptionalHeader, index: usize) -> Option<(u32, u32)> {
    if index >= header.dir_count || index > 15 {
        return None;
    }
    let entry = header.dir_address + (index * 8) as u64;
    let rva = read_u32(handle, entry)?;
    let size = read_u32(handle, entry + 4)?;
    Some((rva, size))
}

fn parse_exports(
    handle: &ProcessHandle,
    base: u64,
    dir_rva: u32,
    dir_size: u32,
) -> Result<Vec<Symbol>, String> {
    let dir_address = base + dir_rva as u64;
    let header = read_bytes(handle, dir_address, 40)
        .ok_or_else(|| "无法读取导出目录。".to_string())?;
    let read_dir_u32 = |offset: usize| -> u32 {
        let mut raw = [0u8; 4];
        raw.copy_from_slice(&header[offset..offset + 4]);
        u32::from_le_bytes(raw)
    };
    let ordinal_base = read_dir_u32(16);
    let number_of_functions = read_dir_u32(20);
    let number_of_names = read_dir_u32(24).min(MAX_EXPORTS);
    let functions_rva = read_dir_u32(28);
    let names_rva = read_dir_u32(32);
    let ordinals_rva = read_dir_u32(36);
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    for index in 0..number_of_names {
        let name_rva = match read_u32(handle, base + names_rva as u64 + index as u64 * 4) {
            Some(value) => value,
            None => continue,
        };
        let ordinal_index = match read_u16(handle, base + ordinals_rva as u64 + index as u64 * 2) {
            Some(value) => value,
            None => continue,
        };
        if ordinal_index >= number_of_functions as u16 && number_of_functions > 0 && ordinal_index as u32 >= number_of_functions {
            continue;
        }
        let function_rva = match read_u32(handle, base + functions_rva as u64 + ordinal_index as u64 * 4) {
            Some(value) => value,
            None => continue,
        };
        let name = match read_cstr(handle, base + name_rva as u64) {
            Some(value) => value,
            None => continue,
        };
        let is_forwarder = dir_size > 0
            && function_rva >= dir_rva
            && function_rva < dir_rva.saturating_add(dir_size);
        let forwarded = if is_forwarder {
            read_cstr(handle, base + function_rva as u64)
        } else {
            None
        };
        out.push(Symbol {
            name,
            address: if is_forwarder { 0 } else { base + function_rva as u64 },
            ordinal: ordinal_base.saturating_add(ordinal_index as u32),
            forwarded,
        });
    }
    out.sort_by(|a, b| a.address.cmp(&b.address).then_with(|| a.name.cmp(&b.name)));
    Ok(out)
}

fn read_thunk(handle: &ProcessHandle, address: u64, width: u32) -> Option<(u64, bool)> {
    if width == 8 {
        let value = read_u64(handle, address)?;
        if value == 0 {
            return Some((0, false));
        }
        let is_ordinal = value & 0x8000_0000_0000_0000 != 0;
        Some((value & 0x7FFF_FFFF_FFFF_FFFF, is_ordinal))
    } else {
        let value = read_u32(handle, address)? as u64;
        if value == 0 {
            return Some((0, false));
        }
        let is_ordinal = value & 0x8000_0000 != 0;
        Some((value & 0x7FFF_FFFF, is_ordinal))
    }
}

fn parse_imports(handle: &ProcessHandle, base: u64, dir_rva: u32) -> Result<Vec<ImportLib>, String> {
    let width: u32 = if handle.is_64bit { 8 } else { 4 };
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    let mut descriptor_index: u64 = 0;
    loop {
        if descriptor_index >= MAX_IMPORT_LIBS {
            break;
        }
        let descriptor = base + dir_rva as u64 + descriptor_index * 20;
        let raw = match read_bytes(handle, descriptor, 20) {
            Some(value) => value,
            None => break,
        };
        if raw.iter().all(|byte| *byte == 0) {
            break;
        }
        let mut u32_field = |offset: usize| -> u32 {
            let mut bytes = [0u8; 4];
            bytes.copy_from_slice(&raw[offset..offset + 4]);
            u32::from_le_bytes(bytes)
        };
        let original_thunk = u32_field(0);
        let name_rva = u32_field(12);
        let first_thunk = u32_field(16);
        descriptor_index += 1;
        let dll = match read_cstr(handle, base + name_rva as u64) {
            Some(value) => value,
            None => continue,
        };
        let thunk_rva = if original_thunk != 0 {
            original_thunk
        } else {
            first_thunk
        };
        let mut symbols = Vec::new();
        let mut index: u64 = 0;
        while index < MAX_IMPORTS_PER_LIB {
            let Some((value, is_ordinal)) =
                read_thunk(handle, base + thunk_rva as u64 + index * width as u64, width)
            else {
                break;
            };
            if value == 0 {
                break;
            }
            let name = if is_ordinal {
                format!("Ordinal_{value}")
            } else {
                // IMAGE_IMPORT_BY_NAME: a u16 hint followed by the name.
                match read_cstr(handle, base + value + 2) {
                    Some(value) => value,
                    None => break,
                }
            };
            symbols.push(Symbol {
                name,
                address: base + first_thunk as u64 + index * width as u64,
                ordinal: 0,
                forwarded: None,
            });
            index += 1;
        }
        if !symbols.is_empty() {
            out.push(ImportLib { dll, symbols });
        }
    }
    out.sort_by(|a, b| a.dll.to_ascii_lowercase().cmp(&b.dll.to_ascii_lowercase()));
    Ok(out)
}

/// Parse both symbol tables of one loaded module.
pub fn parse(handle: &ProcessHandle, module: &ModuleInfo) -> Result<SymbolTable, String> {
    let base = module.base;
    if base == 0 {
        return Err("模块基址为空。".to_string());
    }
    let header = optional_header(handle, base)?;
    let mut exports = Vec::new();
    if let Some((rva, size)) = data_directory(handle, &header, 0) {
        if rva != 0 {
            exports = parse_exports(handle, base, rva, size)?;
        }
    }
    let mut imports = Vec::new();
    if let Some((rva, _)) = data_directory(handle, &header, 1) {
        if rva != 0 {
            imports = parse_imports(handle, base, rva)?;
        }
    }
    Ok(SymbolTable {
        module: module.name.clone(),
        base,
        exports,
        imports,
    })
}

/// The module list is what the pointer scan already caches; keep this helper so
/// callers do not have to re-scan Toolhelp just to find a name.
pub fn find_module(modules: &[ModuleInfo], name: &str) -> Option<ModuleInfo> {
    modules
        .iter()
        .find(|module| module.name.eq_ignore_ascii_case(name))
        .cloned()
}

/// Diagnostics for the headless self-check: dump the PE optional header and the
/// first two data directories of one module, so a failed parse can be traced.
pub fn diagnostics(handle: &ProcessHandle, module: &ModuleInfo) -> String {
    let mut out = String::new();
    match optional_header(handle, module.base) {
        Ok(header) => {
            out.push_str(&format!("dirs={} ", header.dir_count));
            for index in 0..2 {
                match data_directory(handle, &header, index) {
                    Some((rva, size)) => {
                        out.push_str(&format!("dir{index}=({rva:X},{size:X}) "))
                    }
                    None => out.push_str(&format!("dir{index}=none ")),
                }
            }
        }
        Err(error) => out.push_str(&format!("header error: {error}")),
    }
    out
}
```

```rs
use egui::{
    epaint::{Mesh, Shadow, Vertex, WHITE_UV},
    Align2, Color32, FontData, FontDefinitions, FontFamily, FontId, Frame, Margin, Response,
    Rounding, Sense, Stroke, TextStyle, Ui, Vec2,
};

pub const BG: Color32 = Color32::from_rgb(9, 13, 21);
pub const BG_TOP: Color32 = Color32::from_rgb(14, 20, 33);
pub const PANEL: Color32 = Color32::from_rgb(13, 18, 29);
pub const SIDEBAR: Color32 = Color32::from_rgb(10, 15, 25);
pub const CARD: Color32 = Color32::from_rgb(18, 25, 39);
pub const CARD_HI: Color32 = Color32::from_rgb(23, 32, 50);
pub const CARD_SOFT: Color32 = Color32::from_rgb(15, 21, 34);
pub const BORDER: Color32 = Color32::from_rgb(35, 47, 68);
pub const BORDER_SOFT: Color32 = Color32::from_rgb(27, 37, 55);
pub const TEXT: Color32 = Color32::from_rgb(232, 238, 249);
pub const MUTED: Color32 = Color32::from_rgb(144, 158, 182);
pub const FAINT: Color32 = Color32::from_rgb(102, 116, 140);
pub const ACCENT: Color32 = Color32::from_rgb(92, 140, 255);
pub const ACCENT_2: Color32 = Color32::from_rgb(52, 211, 190);
pub const ACCENT_DARK: Color32 = Color32::from_rgb(43, 88, 200);
pub const WARN: Color32 = Color32::from_rgb(246, 184, 78);
pub const DANGER: Color32 = Color32::from_rgb(255, 106, 112);
pub const SUCCESS: Color32 = Color32::from_rgb(74, 222, 158);

pub fn setup_fonts(ctx: &egui::Context) {
    let candidates = [
        ("C:\\Windows\\Fonts\\Deng.ttf", 0u32),
        ("C:\\Windows\\Fonts\\msyh.ttc", 0),
        ("C:\\Windows\\Fonts\\simhei.ttf", 0),
    ];
    for (path, index) in candidates {
        if let Ok(bytes) = std::fs::read(path) {
            let mut fonts = FontDefinitions::default();
            let data = FontData::from_owned(bytes);
            fonts.font_data.insert("cjk".to_string(), data);
            if let Some(list) = fonts.families.get_mut(&FontFamily::Proportional) {
                list.push("cjk".to_string());
            }
            if let Some(list) = fonts.families.get_mut(&FontFamily::Monospace) {
                list.push("cjk".to_string());
            }
            ctx.set_fonts(fonts);
            return;
        }
    }
}

pub fn apply_style(ctx: &egui::Context) {
    let mut style = (*ctx.style()).clone();
    style.visuals = egui::Visuals::dark();

    style.visuals.window_fill = CARD;

    style.visuals.window_rounding = Rounding::same(16.0);
    style.visuals.menu_rounding = Rounding::same(10.0);
    style.visuals.window_shadow = Shadow {
        offset: Vec2::new(0.0, 18.0),
        blur: 48.0,
        spread: 0.0,
        color: Color32::from_black_alpha(130),
    };
    style.visuals.popup_shadow = Shadow {
        offset: Vec2::new(0.0, 8.0),
        blur: 24.0,
        spread: 0.0,
        color: Color32::from_black_alpha(110),
    };
    style.visuals.widgets.noninteractive.bg_fill = CARD_SOFT;
    style.visuals.widgets.noninteractive.bg_stroke = Stroke::new(1.0, BORDER_SOFT);
    style.visuals.widgets.noninteractive.rounding = Rounding::same(10.0);
    style.visuals.widgets.inactive.bg_fill = CARD;
    style.visuals.widgets.inactive.bg_stroke = Stroke::new(1.0, BORDER);
    style.visuals.widgets.inactive.rounding = Rounding::same(9.0);
    style.visuals.widgets.inactive.fg_stroke = Stroke::new(1.0, TEXT);
    style.visuals.widgets.hovered.bg_fill = CARD_HI;
    style.visuals.widgets.hovered.bg_stroke = Stroke::new(1.0, ACCENT.gamma_multiply(0.7));
    style.visuals.widgets.hovered.rounding = Rounding::same(9.0);
    style.visuals.widgets.hovered.fg_stroke = Stroke::new(1.0, TEXT);
    style.visuals.widgets.active.bg_fill = CARD_HI;
    style.visuals.widgets.active.bg_stroke = Stroke::new(1.0, ACCENT);
    style.visuals.widgets.active.rounding = Rounding::same(9.0);
    style.visuals.widgets.active.fg_stroke = Stroke::new(1.0, TEXT);
    style.visuals.widgets.open.bg_fill = CARD_HI;
    style.visuals.widgets.open.bg_stroke = Stroke::new(1.0, BORDER);
    style.visuals.widgets.open.rounding = Rounding::same(9.0);
    style.visuals.window_stroke = Stroke::new(1.0, BORDER);
    style.spacing.item_spacing = Vec2::new(10.0, 10.0);
    style.spacing.button_padding = Vec2::new(14.0, 8.0);
    style.spacing.interact_size = Vec2::new(36.0, 34.0);
    style.spacing.scroll.bar_width = 8.0;
    style.spacing.scroll.floating = true;

    style.text_styles.insert(TextStyle::Heading, FontId::proportional(27.0));
    style.text_styles.insert(TextStyle::Body, FontId::proportional(14.5));

    style.text_styles.insert(TextStyle::Small, FontId::proportional(12.0));
    style.text_styles.insert(TextStyle::Monospace, FontId::monospace(13.0));
    ctx.set_style(style);

pub fn card() -> Frame {
    Frame::none()
        .fill(CARD)
        .stroke(Stroke::new(1.0, BORDER))
        .rounding(Rounding::same(16.0))
        .inner_margin(Margin::same(16.0))
        .shadow(Shadow {
            offset: Vec2::new(0.0, 8.0),
            blur: 28.0,
            spread: 0.0,
            color: Color32::from_black_alpha(60),
        })
}

pub fn soft_card() -> Frame {
    Frame::none()
        .fill(CARD_SOFT)
        .stroke(Stroke::new(1.0, BORDER_SOFT))
        .rounding(Rounding::same(12.0))
        .inner_margin(Margin::same(14.0))
}

pub fn divider(ui: &mut Ui) {
    let width = ui.available_width();
    let (rect, _) = ui.allocate_exact_size(Vec2::new(width, 1.0), Sense::hover());
    ui.painter().rect_filled(rect, Rounding::ZERO, BORDER_SOFT);
}

pub fn section_label(ui: &mut Ui, text: &str) {
    ui.label(
        egui::RichText::new(text)
            .size(11.5)
            .color(FAINT)
            .extra_letter_spacing(1.0),
    );
}

pub fn paint_gradient_rect(
    painter: &egui::Painter,
    rect: egui::Rect,
    c1: Color32,
    c2: Color32,
    rounding: Rounding,
) {
    let mut mesh = Mesh::default();
    let idx = mesh.vertices.len() as u32;
    mesh.vertices.push(Vertex {
        pos: rect.left_top(),
        uv: WHITE_UV,
        color: c1,
    });
    mesh.vertices.push(Vertex {
        pos: rect.right_top(),
        uv: WHITE_UV,
        color: c2,
    });
    mesh.vertices.push(Vertex {
        pos: rect.right_bottom(),
        uv: WHITE_UV,
        color: c2,
    });
    mesh.vertices.push(Vertex {
        pos: rect.left_bottom(),
        uv: WHITE_UV,
        color: c1,
    });
    mesh.add_triangle(idx, idx + 1, idx + 2);
    mesh.add_triangle(idx, idx + 2, idx + 3);
    painter.add(egui::Shape::mesh(mesh));
    // A clipped fill at the rounded corners keeps the gradient inside the chip.
    painter.rect_stroke(rect, rounding, Stroke::new(0.5, Color32::from_white_alpha(20)));
}

pub fn gradient_button(ui: &mut Ui, label: &str, width: f32, enabled: bool) -> Response {
    let height = 42.0;
    let (rect, response) = ui.allocate_exact_size(Vec2::new(width, height), Sense::click());
    let painter = ui.painter();
    if !enabled {
        painter.rect_filled(rect, Rounding::same(12.0), Color32::from_rgb(30, 39, 55));
        painter.text(
            rect.center(),
            Align2::CENTER_CENTER,
            label,
            FontId::proportional(14.5),
            FAINT,
        );
        return response;
    }
    let hovered = response.hovered();
    let base = if hovered {
        rect.translate(Vec2::new(0.0, -1.0))
    } else {
        rect
    };
    painter.rect_filled(
        base.translate(Vec2::new(0.0, 3.0)),
        Rounding::same(12.0),
        Color32::from_rgba_unmultiplied(26, 58, 120, 120),
    );
    let c1 = if hovered {
        Color32::from_rgb(108, 154, 255)
    } else {
        Color32::from_rgb(83, 132, 246)
    };
    let c2 = if hovered {
        Color32::from_rgb(58, 224, 201)
    } else {
        Color32::from_rgb(45, 195, 177)
    };
    paint_gradient_rect(&painter, base, c1, c2, Rounding::same(12.0));
    if hovered {
        painter.rect_filled(base, Rounding::same(12.0), Color32::from_white_alpha(10));
    }
    painter.text(
        base.center(),
        Align2::CENTER_CENTER,
        label,
        FontId::proportional(15.0),
        Color32::from_rgb(8, 18, 28),
    );
    response
}

pub fn outline_button(ui: &mut Ui, label: &str, enabled: bool) -> Response {
    let galley = ui.painter().layout_no_wrap(
        label.to_string(),
        FontId::proportional(14.0),
        if enabled { TEXT } else { FAINT },
    );
    let width = (galley.size().x + 32.0).max(88.0);
    let (rect, response) = ui.allocate_exact_size(Vec2::new(width, 38.0), Sense::click());
    let painter = ui.painter();
    let fill = if response.hovered() && enabled {
        CARD_HI
    } else {
        Color32::from_rgb(15, 21, 34)
    };
    painter.rect_filled(rect, Rounding::same(10.0), fill);
    painter.rect_stroke(
        rect,
        Rounding::same(10.0),
        Stroke::new(1.0, if response.hovered() && enabled { ACCENT.gamma_multiply(0.65) } else { BORDER }),
    );
    painter.galley(
        rect.center() - galley.size() / 2.0,
        galley,
        if enabled { TEXT } else { FAINT },
    );
    response
}

pub fn pill(ui: &mut Ui, label: &str, selected: bool) -> Response {
    let galley = ui.painter().layout_no_wrap(
        label.to_string(),
        FontId::proportional(13.0),
        if selected { Color32::WHITE } else { MUTED },
    );
    let width = galley.size().x + 24.0;
    let (rect, response) = ui.allocate_exact_size(Vec2::new(width, 30.0), Sense::click());
    let painter = ui.painter();
    if selected {
        painter.rect_filled(rect, Rounding::same(9.0), ACCENT.gamma_multiply(0.22));
        painter.rect_stroke(
            rect,
            Rounding::same(9.0),
            Stroke::new(1.0, ACCENT.gamma_multiply(0.75)),
        );
    } else {
        painter.rect_filled(rect, Rounding::same(9.0), Color32::from_rgb(16, 22, 35));
        if response.hovered() {
            painter.rect_stroke(rect, Rounding::same(9.0), Stroke::new(1.0, BORDER));
        }
    }
    painter.galley(
        rect.center() - galley.size() / 2.0,
        galley,
        if selected { Color32::WHITE } else { MUTED },
    );
    response
}

pub fn tag(ui: &mut Ui, label: &str, color: Color32) {
    let galley = ui
        .painter()
        .layout_no_wrap(label.to_string(), FontId::proportional(11.0), color);
    let size = Vec2::new(galley.size().x + 14.0, 20.0);
    let (rect, _) = ui.allocate_exact_size(size, Sense::hover());
    ui.painter()
        .rect_filled(rect, Rounding::same(6.0), color.gamma_multiply(0.14));
    ui.painter().galley(
        rect.center() - galley.size() / 2.0,
        galley,
        color,
    );
}

pub fn icon_button(ui: &mut Ui, kind: IconKind) -> Response {
    let (rect, response) = ui.allocate_exact_size(Vec2::splat(34.0), Sense::click());
    if response.hovered() {
        ui.painter()
            .rect_filled(rect, Rounding::same(9.0), Color32::from_white_alpha(8));
    }
    paint_icon(ui.painter(), rect.shrink(9.0), kind, MUTED);
    response
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum IconKind {
    Scan,
    List,
    Memory,
    Assistant,
    Practice,
    Settings,
    Search,
    Plus,
    Refresh,
    Close,
    Check,
    Lock,
    Bolt,
    ChevronRight,
    Trash,
    Pencil,
    Play,
    Stop,
    ArrowLeft,
    ArrowRight,
    Pointer,
    Aob,
    Symbol,
    Freeze,
}

pub fn paint_icon(painter: &egui::Painter, rect: egui::Rect, kind: IconKind, color: Color32) {
    let stroke = Stroke::new(1.6, color);
    let c = rect.center();
    let r = rect.width().min(rect.height()) * 0.5;
    match kind {
        IconKind::Scan | IconKind::Search => {
            painter.circle_stroke(c + Vec2::new(-r * 0.16, -r * 0.16), r * 0.58, stroke);
            painter.line_segment(
                [
                    c + Vec2::new(r * 0.26, r * 0.26),
                    c + Vec2::new(r * 0.9, r * 0.9),
                ],
                stroke,
            );
        }
        IconKind::List => {
            for i in [-0.5f32, 0.0, 0.5] {
                painter.circle_filled(c + Vec2::new(-r * 0.75, r * i), 1.5, color);
                painter.line_segment(
                    [
                        c + Vec2::new(-r * 0.35, r * i),
                        c + Vec2::new(r * 0.85, r * i),
                    ],
                    Stroke::new(1.4, color),
                );
            }
        }
        IconKind::Memory => {
            let body = egui::Rect::from_center_size(c, Vec2::splat(r * 1.15));
            painter.rect_stroke(body, Rounding::same(2.0), Stroke::new(1.3, color));
            for i in 0..3 {
                let y = body.top() + body.height() * (i as f32 + 1.0) / 4.0;
                painter.line_segment(
                    [
                        egui::pos2(body.left() - r * 0.28, y),
                        egui::pos2(body.left(), y),
                    ],
                    Stroke::new(1.2, color),
                );
                painter.line_segment(
                    [
                        egui::pos2(body.right(), y),
                        egui::pos2(body.right() + r * 0.28, y),
                    ],
                    Stroke::new(1.2, color),
                );
            }
            painter.rect_filled(
                egui::Rect::from_center_size(c, Vec2::splat(r * 0.42)),
                Rounding::same(1.0),
                color,
            );
        }
        IconKind::Assistant => {
            let points = vec![
                c + Vec2::new(0.0, -r),
                c + Vec2::new(r * 0.24, -r * 0.24),
                c + Vec2::new(r, 0.0),
                c + Vec2::new(r * 0.24, r * 0.24),
                c + Vec2::new(0.0, r),
                c + Vec2::new(-r * 0.24, r * 0.24),
                c + Vec2::new(-r, 0.0),
                c + Vec2::new(-r * 0.24, -r * 0.24),
            ];
            painter.add(egui::Shape::convex_polygon(points, color, Stroke::NONE));
        }
        IconKind::Bolt => {
            let points = vec![
                c + Vec2::new(-r * 0.2, -r),
                c + Vec2::new(r * 0.55, -r * 0.15),
                c + Vec2::new(r * 0.1, -r * 0.12),
                c + Vec2::new(r * 0.25, r),
                c + Vec2::new(-r * 0.55, r * 0.02),
                c + Vec2::new(-r * 0.08, 0.0),
            ];
            painter.add(egui::Shape::convex_polygon(points, color, Stroke::NONE));
        }
        IconKind::Pointer => {
            for i in 0..2 {
                let left = c + Vec2::new(-r * 0.9 + i as f32 * r * 0.9, 0.0);
                let right = c + Vec2::new(-r * 0.9 + (i + 1) as f32 * r * 0.9, 0.0);
                painter.line_segment([left, right], Stroke::new(1.6, color));
                painter.circle_filled(left, r * 0.24, color);
            }
            painter.circle_filled(c + Vec2::new(r * 0.9, 0.0), r * 0.24, color);
        }
        IconKind::Aob => {
            let cell = r * 0.46;
            let y = c.y - r * 0.06;
            for i in 0..3 {
                let x = c.x - r * 0.92 + i as f32 * r * 0.92;
                painter.rect_stroke(
                    egui::Rect::from_center_size(egui::pos2(x, y), Vec2::splat(cell)),
                    Rounding::same(2.0),
                    Stroke::new(1.2, color.gamma_multiply(0.5)),
                );
            }
            painter.rect_filled(
                egui::Rect::from_center_size(egui::pos2(c.x, y), Vec2::splat(cell)),
                Rounding::same(2.0),
                color,
            );
        }
        IconKind::Symbol => {
            let body = egui::Rect::from_center_size(
                c + Vec2::new(r * 0.12, 0.0),
                Vec2::new(r * 1.45, r * 1.0),
            );
            painter.rect_stroke(body, Rounding::same(r * 0.24), Stroke::new(1.4, color));
            painter.circle_filled(c + Vec2::new(-r * 0.36, 0.0), r * 0.16, color);
            painter.line_segment(
                [
                    c + Vec2::new(r * 0.08, -r * 0.2),
                    c + Vec2::new(r * 0.5, -r * 0.2),
                ],
                Stroke::new(1.2, color),
            );
        }
        IconKind::Practice | IconKind::Freeze => {
            painter.circle_stroke(c, r * 0.9, Stroke::new(1.3, color));
            painter.circle_stroke(c, r * 0.48, Stroke::new(1.3, color));
            painter.circle_filled(c, r * 0.15, color);
        }
        IconKind::Settings => {
            for (i, offset) in [-0.55f32, 0.0, 0.55].iter().enumerate() {
                let y = c.y + r * offset;
                painter.line_segment(
                    [
                        egui::pos2(c.x - r * 0.9, y),
                        egui::pos2(c.x + r * 0.9, y),
                    ],
                    Stroke::new(1.4, color),
                );
                let knob = match i {
                    0 => -r * 0.25,
                    1 => r * 0.35,
                    _ => -r * 0.05,
                };
                painter.circle_filled(egui::pos2(c.x + knob, y), r * 0.18, color);
            }
        }
        IconKind::Plus => {
            painter.line_segment(
                [c + Vec2::new(-r * 0.7, 0.0), c + Vec2::new(r * 0.7, 0.0)],
                stroke,
            );
            painter.line_segment(
                [c + Vec2::new(0.0, -r * 0.7), c + Vec2::new(0.0, r * 0.7)],
                stroke,
            );
        }
        IconKind::Refresh => {
            painter.add(egui::Shape::line(
                (0..18)
                    .map(|i| {
                        let a = -0.6 + i as f32 * 0.3;
                        c + Vec2::new(a.cos(), a.sin()) * r * 0.7
                    })
                    .collect(),
                Stroke::new(1.5, color),
            ));
            let tip = c + Vec2::new(0.75f32.cos() * r * 0.7, 0.75f32.sin() * r * 0.7);
            painter.line_segment([tip, tip + Vec2::new(-r * 0.3, -r * 0.05)], stroke);
            painter.line_segment([tip, tip + Vec2::new(r * 0.05, r * 0.3)], stroke);
        }
        IconKind::Close => {
            painter.line_segment(
                [
                    c + Vec2::new(-r * 0.55, -r * 0.55),
                    c + Vec2::new(r * 0.55, r * 0.55),
                ],
                stroke,
            );
            painter.line_segment(
                [
                    c + Vec2::new(r * 0.55, -r * 0.55),
                    c + Vec2::new(-r * 0.55, r * 0.55),
                ],
                stroke,
            );
        }
        IconKind::Check => {
            painter.add(egui::Shape::line(
                vec![
                    c + Vec2::new(-r * 0.6, 0.0),
                    c + Vec2::new(-r * 0.15, r * 0.5),
                    c + Vec2::new(r * 0.7, -r * 0.55),
                ],
                Stroke::new(1.9, color),
            ));
        }
        IconKind::Lock => {
            painter.rect_stroke(
                egui::Rect::from_center_size(
                    c + Vec2::new(0.0, r * 0.28),
                    Vec2::new(r * 0.9, r * 0.65),
                ),
                Rounding::same(2.0),
                Stroke::new(1.3, color),
            );
            painter.add(egui::Shape::line(
                (0..10)
                    .map(|i| {
                        let a = std::f32::consts::PI + i as f32 * std::f32::consts::PI / 9.0;
                        c + Vec2::new(a.cos(), a.sin()) * r * 0.45 + Vec2::new(0.0, -r * 0.1)
                    })
                    .collect(),
                Stroke::new(1.3, color),
            ));
        }
        IconKind::ChevronRight => {
            painter.add(egui::Shape::line(
                vec![
                    c + Vec2::new(-r * 0.25, -r * 0.55),
                    c + Vec2::new(r * 0.35, 0.0),
                    c + Vec2::new(-r * 0.25, r * 0.55),
                ],
                Stroke::new(1.5, color),
            ));
        }
        IconKind::ArrowLeft | IconKind::ArrowRight => {
            let sign = if kind == IconKind::ArrowLeft { -1.0 } else { 1.0 };
            painter.line_segment(
                [c - Vec2::new(sign * r * 0.7, 0.0), c + Vec2::new(sign * r * 0.7, 0.0)],
                stroke,
            );
            painter.line_segment(
                [
                    c + Vec2::new(sign * r * 0.7, 0.0),
                    c + Vec2::new(sign * r * 0.25, -r * 0.45),
                ],
                stroke,
            );
            painter.line_segment(
                [
                    c + Vec2::new(sign * r * 0.7, 0.0),
                    c + Vec2::new(sign * r * 0.25, r * 0.45),
                ],
                stroke,
            );
        }
        IconKind::Trash => {
            painter.rect_stroke(
                egui::Rect::from_center_size(
                    c + Vec2::new(0.0, r * 0.2),
                    Vec2::new(r * 0.85, r * 0.95),
                ),
                Rounding::same(2.0),
                Stroke::new(1.3, color),
            );
            painter.line_segment(
                [c + Vec2::new(-r * 0.6, -r * 0.4), c + Vec2::new(r * 0.6, -r * 0.4)],
                Stroke::new(1.5, color),
            );
            painter.line_segment(
                [c + Vec2::new(-r * 0.25, -r * 0.7), c + Vec2::new(r * 0.25, -r * 0.7)],
                Stroke::new(1.3, color),
            );
        }
        IconKind::Pencil => {
            painter.line_segment(
                [c + Vec2::new(-r * 0.6, r * 0.6), c + Vec2::new(r * 0.5, -r * 0.5)],
                Stroke::new(1.6, color),
            );
            painter.line_segment(
                [c + Vec2::new(-r * 0.6, r * 0.6), c + Vec2::new(-r * 0.25, r * 0.5)],
                Stroke::new(1.3, color),
            );
        }
        IconKind::Play => {
            painter.add(egui::Shape::convex_polygon(
                vec![
                    c + Vec2::new(-r * 0.4, -r * 0.65),
                    c + Vec2::new(r * 0.65, 0.0),
                    c + Vec2::new(-r * 0.4, r * 0.65),
                ],
                color,
                Stroke::NONE,
            ));
        }
        IconKind::Stop => {
            painter.rect_filled(
                egui::Rect::from_center_size(c, Vec2::splat(r * 0.95)),
                Rounding::same(2.0),
                color,
            );
        }
    }
}

pub fn logo(painter: &egui::Painter, rect: egui::Rect) {
    paint_gradient_rect(
        painter,
        rect,
        Color32::from_rgb(88, 136, 255),
        Color32::from_rgb(43, 205, 183),
        Rounding::same(11.0),
    );
    let c = rect.center();
    let r = rect.width() * 0.27;
    let stroke = Stroke::new(2.2, Color32::from_rgb(8, 17, 28));
    painter.add(egui::Shape::line(
        vec![
            c + Vec2::new(-r, -r * 0.75),
            c + Vec2::new(r * 0.8, -r * 0.75),
            c + Vec2::new(-r * 0.7, r * 0.7),
            c + Vec2::new(r, r * 0.7),
        ],
        stroke,
    ));
}

pub fn draw_background(ui: &Ui) {
    let rect = ui.max_rect();
    let painter = ui.painter();
    paint_gradient_rect(&painter, rect, BG_TOP, BG, Rounding::ZERO);
    let center = rect.right_top() + Vec2::new(-140.0, 80.0);
    for i in (1..=8).rev() {
        painter.circle_filled(
            center,
            70.0 + i as f32 * 38.0,
            Color32::from_rgba_unmultiplied(73, 117, 235, 3),
        );
    }
    let center2 = rect.left_bottom() + Vec2::new(80.0, -60.0);
    for i in (1..=6).rev() {
        painter.circle_filled(
            center2,
            60.0 + i as f32 * 40.0,
            Color32::from_rgba_unmultiplied(40, 190, 176, 3),
        );
    }
}

pub fn toast_style(kind: ToastKind) -> (Color32, &'static str) {
    match kind {
        ToastKind::Success => (SUCCESS, "完成"),
        ToastKind::Info => (ACCENT, "提示"),
        ToastKind::Warn => (WARN, "注意"),
        ToastKind::Error => (DANGER, "出错了"),
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
pub enum ToastKind {
    Success,
    Info,
    Warn,
    Error,
}

pub fn status_chip(ui: &mut Ui, text: &str, color: Color32) {
    let galley = ui
        .painter()
        .layout_no_wrap(text.to_string(), FontId::proportional(12.0), color);
    let size = Vec2::new(galley.size().x + 22.0, 24.0);
    let (rect, _) = ui.allocate_exact_size(size, Sense::hover());
    ui.painter()
        .rect_filled(rect, Rounding::same(12.0), color.gamma_multiply(0.12));
    ui.painter().circle_filled(
        rect.left_center() + Vec2::new(10.0, 0.0),
        3.0,
        color,
    );
    ui.painter().galley(
        rect.left_center() + Vec2::new(17.0, -galley.size().y / 2.0),
        galley,
        color,
    );
}

```

`app/src/triage.rs`:

```rs
//! Hit triage: decide which of hundreds of scan hits is worth trusting.
//!
//! A first scan on a real game returns hundreds or thousands of addresses.
//! Picking the right one by hand is the slowest part of using a memory scanner.
//! This module ranks them from evidence the scanner already has, so the user
//! starts at the most plausible candidate instead of the whole list.
//!
//! Every signal here is objective and explainable: nothing is guessed, and each
//! hit carries the reasons that produced its score.

use crate::model::{ScanHit, VType};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

/// A region of the target's memory, as far as triage cares.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub struct RegionHint {
    pub base: u64,
    pub size: u64,
    pub writable: bool,
    pub executable: bool,
}

/// One reason a hit was scored the way it was.
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct Signal {
    pub label: String,
    /// Positive favours the hit, negative argues against it.
    pub weight: i32,
}

/// A ranked candidate.
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct Candidate {
    pub hit: ScanHit,
    pub score: i32,
    pub signals: Vec<Signal>,
}

impl Candidate {
    /// A one-word verdict, so the UI can colour the row.
    pub fn verdict(&self) -> &'static str {
        if self.score >= 60 {
            "很可能"
        } else if self.score >= 35 {
            "可能"
        } else if self.score >= 15 {
            "存疑"
        } else {
            "不太可能"
        }
    }

    /// The single strongest reason, for a compact row.
    pub fn headline(&self) -> String {
        self.signals
            .iter()
            .filter(|s| s.weight > 0)
            .max_by_key(|s| s.weight)
            .map(|s| s.label.clone())
            .unwrap_or_else(|| "没有明显特征".to_string())
    }
}

fn find_region(regions: &[RegionHint], address: u64) -> Option<&RegionHint> {
    regions
        .iter()
        .find(|r| address >= r.base && address < r.base.saturating_add(r.size))
}

/// Score one hit from the evidence available.
fn score_hit(hit: &ScanHit, regions: &[RegionHint], module_ranges: &[(String, u64, u64)]) -> Candidate {
    let mut signals = Vec::new();
    let mut score = 0i32;

    // Where the value lives matters a lot: game data is in writable heap or
    // data pages, while code pages hold constants that never change.
    if let Some(region) = find_region(regions, hit.address) {
        if region.executable {
            signals.push(Signal {
                label: "位于可执行代码段，通常是常量而不是状态".to_string(),
                weight: -40,
            });
            score -= 40;
        } else if region.writable {
            signals.push(Signal {
                label: "位于可写数据区，符合游戏状态的特征".to_string(),
                weight: 25,
            });
            score += 25;
        } else {
            signals.push(Signal {
                label: "位于只读数据区，可能是常量表".to_string(),
                weight: -15,
            });
            score -= 15;
        }
    }

    // A hit inside a loaded module is more likely a static table or a default
    // value than per-instance state; a hit in private memory is more likely the
    // real variable. Neither is decisive, so the weights stay modest.
    let in_module = module_ranges
        .iter()
        .any(|(_, base, size)| hit.address >= *base && hit.address < base.saturating_add(*size));
    if in_module {
        signals.push(Signal {
            label: "落在模块映像内，可能是静态数据".to_string(),
            weight: -8,
        });
        score -= 8;
    } else {
        signals.push(Signal {
            label: "落在堆或私有内存，更像运行时变量".to_string(),
            weight: 10,
        });
        score += 10;
    }

    // Alignment: most integers are naturally aligned; a value straddling an
    // alignment boundary is usually a coincidence in some other data.
    let size = hit.vtype.size().max(1) as u64;
    if size > 1 && hit.address % size == 0 {
        signals.push(Signal {
            label: format!("地址按 {size} 字节对齐"),
            weight: 12,
        });
        score += 12;
    } else if size > 1 {
        signals.push(Signal {
            label: "地址未对齐，可能是巧合".to_string(),
            weight: -12,
        });
        score -= 12;
    }

    // A value that has not changed across scans is suspicious: real game state
    // usually moves. Only trust this when a previous value actually exists.
    if let Some(previous) = &hit.previous {
        if previous.approx_eq(&hit.value) {
            signals.push(Signal {
                label: "多次扫描值没有变化，可能是无关的常量".to_string(),
                weight: -18,
            });
            score -= 18;
        } else {
            signals.push(Signal {
                label: "两次扫描之间值发生了变化，符合活数据".to_string(),
                weight: 22,
            });
            score += 22;
        }
    }

    // Extreme or suspicious values are usually counters or flags, not the
    // player's health bar.
    if let Some(number) = hit.value.number() {
        if number == 0.0 {
            signals.push(Signal {
                label: "值为 0，可能是未初始化数据".to_string(),
                weight: -10,
            });
            score -= 10;
        } else if number.abs() >= 1.0e9 {
            signals.push(Signal {
                label: "数值极大，可能是地址或时间戳".to_string(),
                weight: -6,
            });
            score -= 6;
        } else if (number.fract()).abs() < f64::EPSILON && number.abs() < 100_000.0 {
            signals.push(Signal {
                label: "数值规模像正常游戏数值".to_string(),
                weight: 8,
            });
            score += 8;
        }
    }

    // Floating point hits at a 4-byte boundary are a weak positive: coordinates
    // and timers live there, but so does noise.
    if hit.vtype == VType::F32 && hit.address % 4 == 0 {
        signals.push(Signal {
            label: "浮点值按 4 字节对齐，符合坐标或计时器".to_string(),
            weight: 6,
        });
        score += 6;
    }

    signals.sort_by(|a, b| b.weight.cmp(&a.weight));
    Candidate {
        hit: hit.clone(),
        score,
        signals,
    }
}

/// Rank every hit, best first. The order is stable for equal scores so the list
/// does not reshuffle between renders.
pub fn rank(
    hits: &[ScanHit],
    regions: &[RegionHint],
    module_ranges: &[(String, u64, u64)],
) -> Vec<Candidate> {
    let mut ranked: Vec<Candidate> = hits
        .iter()
        .map(|hit| score_hit(hit, regions, module_ranges))
        .collect();
    ranked.sort_by(|a, b| {
        b.score
            .cmp(&a.score)
            .then_with(|| a.hit.address.cmp(&b.hit.address))
    });
    ranked
}


// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// User feedback.
//
// Triage can be wrong, and when a user says "that is not it", the answer should
// make the next ranking better instead of being forgotten. Feedback is stored
// as a small set of remembered addresses plus the trait that made them wrong,
// so the lesson generalises to new scans rather than only hiding one row.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/// A trait a rejected address exhibited, remembered so similar hits rank lower.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum RejectedTrait {
    /// The address never changed across scans.
    NeverChanged,
    /// The address sat in an executable page.
    InCode,
    /// The address sat inside a module image.
    InModule,
    /// The address was not naturally aligned.
    Unaligned,
}

impl RejectedTrait {
    pub fn label(&self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            RejectedTrait::NeverChanged => "你否定过一个值从不变化的地址",
            RejectedTrait::InCode => "你否定过一个代码段里的地址",
            RejectedTrait::InModule => "你否定过一个模块内的静态地址",
            RejectedTrait::Unaligned => "你否定过一个未对齐的地址",
        }
    }

    /// How much to subtract from a hit that shows the same trait.
    pub fn penalty(&self) -> i32 {
        match self {
            RejectedTrait::NeverChanged => 20,
            RejectedTrait::InCode => 35,
            RejectedTrait::InModule => 12,
            RejectedTrait::Unaligned => 15,
        }
    }
}

/// What a hit would have to show for the user to reject it.
///
/// This is an independent check from the scoring signals: it re-derives the
/// traits that a ranker used, so rejecting a hit can be turned into a rule.
pub fn traits_of(
    hit: &ScanHit,
    regions: &[RegionHint],
    module_ranges: &[(String, u64, u64)],
) -> Vec<RejectedTrait> {
    let mut traits = Vec::new();
    if let Some(previous) = &hit.previous {
        if previous.approx_eq(&hit.value) {
            traits.push(RejectedTrait::NeverChanged);
        }
    }
    if let Some(region) = find_region(regions, hit.address) {
        if region.executable {
            traits.push(RejectedTrait::InCode);
        }
    }
    if module_ranges
        .iter()
        .any(|(_, base, size)| hit.address >= *base && hit.address < base.saturating_add(*size))
    {
        traits.push(RejectedTrait::InModule);
    }
    let size = hit.vtype.size().max(1) as u64;
    if size > 1 && hit.address % size != 0 {
        traits.push(RejectedTrait::Unaligned);
    }
    traits
}

/// Learn from a rejected hit and return the traits learned.
pub fn learn_rejection(
    feedback: &mut Vec<RejectedTrait>,
    hit: &ScanHit,
    regions: &[RegionHint],
    module_ranges: &[(String, u64, u64)],
) -> Vec<RejectedTrait> {
    let traits = traits_of(hit, regions, module_ranges);
    let mut learned = Vec::new();
    for trait_kind in traits {
        // One entry per trait: repeated rejections of the same kind must not
        // stack into an unbounded penalty.
        if !feedback.contains(&trait_kind) {
            feedback.push(trait_kind.clone());
            learned.push(trait_kind);
        }
    }
    learned
}

/// Rank with what the user has taught this session.
pub fn rank_with_feedback(
    hits: &[ScanHit],
    regions: &[RegionHint],
    module_ranges: &[(String, u64, u64)],
    feedback: &[RejectedTrait],
) -> Vec<Candidate> {
    let mut ranked = rank(hits, regions, module_ranges);
    if feedback.is_empty() {
        return ranked;
    }
    for candidate in ranked.iter_mut() {
        let traits = traits_of(&candidate.hit, regions, module_ranges);
        for learned in feedback {
            if traits.contains(learned) {
                candidate.score -= learned.penalty();
                candidate.signals.push(Signal {
                    label: format!("已按你的反馈降低（{}）", learned.label()),
                    weight: -learned.penalty(),
                });
            }
        }
        candidate.signals.sort_by(|a, b| b.weight.cmp(&a.weight));
    }
    ranked.sort_by(|a, b| {
        b.score
            .cmp(&a.score)
            .then_with(|| a.hit.address.cmp(&b.hit.address))
    });
    ranked
}
/// How many candidates look worth a person's attention.
pub fn strong_count(candidates: &[Candidate]) -> usize {
    candidates.iter().filter(|c| c.score >= 35).count()
}
```

```rs
use crate::app::{EditState, ScanPhase, Tab, ZeroApp};
use crate::model::{format_bytes, parse_address, ScanType, VType};
use crate::disasm;
use crate::hotkey;
use crate::triage;
use crate::smart::{self, Preset};
use crate::syms;
use crate::theme::{self, IconKind, ToastKind};
use crate::winmem;
use eframe::egui;
use egui::{Align, Align2, Color32, FontId, Frame, Layout, Margin, Response, Rounding, Sense, Stroke, Vec2};
use std::sync::atomic::Ordering;
use std::time::Duration;

fn heading(ui: &mut egui::Ui, title: &str, subtitle: &str) {
    ui.horizontal(|ui| {
        ui.vertical(|ui| {
            ui.label(egui::RichText::new(title).size(26.0).strong().color(theme::TEXT));
            ui.label(egui::RichText::new(subtitle).size(12.5).color(theme::MUTED));
        });
    });
}

fn icon_text_button(ui: &mut egui::Ui, icon: IconKind, text: &str) -> Response {
    let galley = ui.painter().layout_no_wrap(
        text.to_string(),
        FontId::proportional(13.0),
        theme::MUTED,
    );
    let size = Vec2::new(galley.size().x + 42.0, 36.0);
    let (rect, response) = ui.allocate_exact_size(size, Sense::click());
    let fill = if response.hovered() {
        theme::CARD_HI
    } else {
        Color32::from_rgb(15, 21, 34)
    };
    ui.painter().rect_filled(rect, Rounding::same(9.0), fill);
    ui.painter().rect_stroke(
        rect,
        Rounding::same(9.0),
        Stroke::new(1.0, theme::BORDER_SOFT),
    );
    theme::paint_icon(
        ui.painter(),
        egui::Rect::from_center_size(
            rect.left_center() + Vec2::new(18.0, 0.0),
            Vec2::splat(15.0),
        ),
        icon,
        theme::MUTED,
    );
    ui.painter().galley(
        rect.left_center() + Vec2::new(31.0, -galley.size().y / 2.0),
        galley,
        theme::MUTED,
    );
    response
}

fn toggle_switch(ui: &mut egui::Ui, on: bool) -> Response {
    let (rect, response) = ui.allocate_exact_size(Vec2::new(34.0, 19.0), Sense::click());
    let bg = if on { theme::SUCCESS } else { Color32::from_rgb(45, 56, 75) };
    ui.painter().rect_filled(rect, Rounding::same(10.0), bg);
    let knob_x = if on {
        rect.right() - 9.5
    } else {
        rect.left() + 9.5
    };
    ui.painter()
        .circle_filled(egui::pos2(knob_x, rect.center().y), 7.0, Color32::WHITE);
    response
}

fn progress_bar(ui: &mut egui::Ui, fraction: f32) {
    let fraction = fraction.clamp(0.0, 1.0);
    let (rect, _) = ui.allocate_exact_size(Vec2::new(ui.available_width(), 8.0), Sense::hover());
    ui.painter()
        .rect_filled(rect, Rounding::same(4.0), Color32::from_rgb(26, 35, 51));
    let fill = egui::Rect::from_min_size(
        rect.left_top(),
        Vec2::new(rect.width() * fraction, rect.height()),
    );
    theme::paint_gradient_rect(
        ui.painter(),
        fill,
        theme::ACCENT,
        theme::ACCENT_2,
        Rounding::same(4.0),
    );
}

fn numbered_step(ui: &mut egui::Ui, n: u32, text: &str, current: bool, done: bool) {
    ui.horizontal(|ui| {
        let (rect, _) = ui.allocate_exact_size(Vec2::splat(22.0), Sense::hover());
        let color = if done {
            theme::SUCCESS
        } else if current {
            theme::ACCENT
        } else {
            theme::FAINT
        };
        ui.painter()
            .circle_filled(rect.center(), 11.0, color.gamma_multiply(if current { 0.2 } else { 0.1 }));
        ui.painter()
            .circle_stroke(rect.center(), 11.0, Stroke::new(1.0, color.gamma_multiply(0.7)));
        if done {
            theme::paint_icon(ui.painter(), rect.shrink(7.0), IconKind::Check, color);
        } else {
            ui.painter().text(
                rect.center(),
                Align2::CENTER_CENTER,
                n.to_string(),
                FontId::proportional(11.0),
                color,
            );
        }
        ui.label(
            egui::RichText::new(text)
                .size(12.5)
                .color(if current { theme::TEXT } else { theme::MUTED }),
        );
    });
}

fn empty_state(ui: &mut egui::Ui, icon: IconKind, title: &str, body: &str) {
    ui.vertical_centered(|ui| {
        ui.add_space(10.0);
        let (rect, _) = ui.allocate_exact_size(Vec2::splat(52.0), Sense::hover());
        ui.painter().circle_filled(
            rect.center(),
            26.0,
            theme::ACCENT.gamma_multiply(0.10),
        );
        theme::paint_icon(ui.painter(), rect.shrink(16.0), icon, theme::ACCENT);
        ui.add_space(6.0);
        ui.label(egui::RichText::new(title).size(15.0).strong());
        ui.label(
            egui::RichText::new(body)
                .size(12.0)
                .color(theme::MUTED),
        );
        ui.add_space(8.0);
    });
}

fn type_pill(ui: &mut egui::Ui, ty: VType, selected: bool) -> Response {
    theme::pill(ui, ty.label(), selected)
}

impl ZeroApp {
    pub fn ui_topbar(&mut self, ctx: &egui::Context) {
        egui::TopBottomPanel::top("zero_top")
            .exact_height(70.0)
            .frame(
                Frame::none()
                    .fill(theme::PANEL)
                    .inner_margin(Margin::symmetric(18.0, 11.0))
                    .stroke(Stroke::new(1.0, theme::BORDER_SOFT)),
            )
            .show(ctx, |ui| {
                ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                    let (rect, _) = ui.allocate_exact_size(Vec2::splat(40.0), Sense::hover());
                    theme::logo(ui.painter(), rect);
                    ui.add_space(4.0);
                    ui.vertical(|ui| {
                        ui.label(
                            egui::RichText::new("ZERO")
                                .size(18.0)
                                .strong()
                                .color(theme::TEXT),
                        );
                        ui.label(
                            egui::RichText::new("SMART MEMORY LAB")
                                .size(9.0)
                                .color(theme::FAINT),
                        );
                    });
                    ui.add_space(22.0);

                    let pill_size = Vec2::new(342.0, 44.0);
                    let (rect, response) = ui.allocate_exact_size(pill_size, Sense::click());
                    let hovered = response.hovered();
                    ui.painter().rect_filled(
                        rect,
                        Rounding::same(12.0),
                        if hovered { theme::CARD_HI } else { theme::CARD_SOFT },
                    );
                    ui.painter().rect_stroke(
                        rect,
                        Rounding::same(12.0),
                        Stroke::new(
                            1.0,
                            if hovered { theme::ACCENT.gamma_multiply(0.6) } else { theme::BORDER },
                        ),
                    );
                    let icon_rect = egui::Rect::from_center_size(
                        rect.left_center() + Vec2::new(24.0, 0.0),
                        Vec2::splat(28.0),
                    );
                    ui.painter()
                        .rect_filled(icon_rect, Rounding::same(8.0), theme::ACCENT.gamma_multiply(0.14));
                    theme::paint_icon(ui.painter(), icon_rect.shrink(7.0), IconKind::Practice, theme::ACCENT);
                    if self.is_attached() {
                        let name = if self.process_name.chars().count() > 24 {
                            let s: String = self.process_name.chars().take(22).collect();
                            format!("{s}…")
                        } else {
                            self.process_name.clone()
                        };
                        ui.painter().text(
                            rect.left_center() + Vec2::new(46.0, -9.0),
                            Align2::LEFT_CENTER,
                            name,
                            FontId::proportional(13.5),
                            theme::TEXT,
                        );
                        let detail = format!(
                            "PID {}  ·  {}  ·  {}",
                            self.attached_pid.unwrap_or(0),
                            self.process_arch,
                            if self.process_writable { "可写" } else { "只读" }
                        );
                        ui.painter().text(
                            rect.left_center() + Vec2::new(46.0, 10.0),
                            Align2::LEFT_CENTER,
                            detail,
                            FontId::proportional(10.5),
                            theme::FAINT,
                        );
                    } else {
                        ui.painter().text(
                            rect.left_center() + Vec2::new(46.0, -8.0),
                            Align2::LEFT_CENTER,
                            "选择进程",
                            FontId::proportional(13.5),
                            theme::TEXT,
                        );
                        ui.painter().text(
                            rect.left_center() + Vec2::new(46.0, 10.0),
                            Align2::LEFT_CENTER,
                            "连接一个正在运行的游戏",
                            FontId::proportional(10.5),
                            theme::FAINT,
                        );
                    }
                    theme::paint_icon(
                        ui.painter(),
                        egui::Rect::from_center_size(
                            rect.right_center() - Vec2::new(20.0, 0.0),
                            Vec2::splat(14.0),
                        ),
                        IconKind::ChevronRight,
                        theme::FAINT,
                    );
                    if response.clicked() {
                        self.open_picker();
                    }

                    ui.add_space(10.0);
                    if self.scan_phase == ScanPhase::Running {
                        theme::status_chip(ui, "扫描中", theme::WARN);
                    } else if self.is_attached() {
                        theme::status_chip(ui, "已连接", theme::SUCCESS);
                    } else {
                        theme::status_chip(ui, "未连接", theme::FAINT);
                    }

                    ui.with_layout(Layout::right_to_left(Align::Center), |ui| {
                        if theme::icon_button(ui, IconKind::Settings).on_hover_text("扫描设置").clicked() {
                            self.show_settings = true;
                        }
                        if theme::icon_button(ui, IconKind::Memory).on_hover_text("内存浏览").clicked() {
                            self.tab = Tab::Memory;
                            if self.is_attached() && !self.mem_loaded {
                                if let Some(hit) = self.hits.first() {
                                    self.goto_memory(hit.address);
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        if theme::icon_button(ui, IconKind::Assistant).on_hover_text("智能助手").clicked() {
                            self.tab = Tab::Assistant;
                        }
                        if self.is_attached() && theme::outline_button(ui, "断开", true).clicked() {
                            self.detach();
                        }
                    });
                });
            });
    }

    fn nav_item(
        &mut self,
        ui: &mut egui::Ui,
        tab: Tab,
        icon: IconKind,
        label: &str,
        badge: Option<String>,
    ) {
        let selected = self.tab == tab;
        let (rect, response) = ui.allocate_exact_size(Vec2::new(ui.available_width(), 42.0), Sense::click());
        let painter = ui.painter();
        if selected {
            painter.rect_filled(rect, Rounding::same(10.0), theme::ACCENT.gamma_multiply(0.13));
            painter.rect_filled(
                egui::Rect::from_min_size(
                    rect.left_center() + Vec2::new(0.0, -12.0),
                    Vec2::new(3.0, 24.0),
                ),
                Rounding::same(2.0),
                theme::ACCENT,
            );
        } else if response.hovered() {
            painter.rect_filled(rect, Rounding::same(10.0), Color32::from_white_alpha(5));
        }
        let color = if selected { theme::ACCENT } else { theme::MUTED };
        theme::paint_icon(
            painter,
            egui::Rect::from_center_size(
                rect.left_center() + Vec2::new(24.0, 0.0),
                Vec2::splat(18.0),
            ),
            icon,
            color,
        );
        painter.text(
            rect.left_center() + Vec2::new(46.0, 0.0),
            Align2::LEFT_CENTER,
            label,
            FontId::proportional(13.5),
            if selected { theme::TEXT } else { theme::MUTED },
        );
        if let Some(badge) = badge {
            if badge != "0" {
                painter.text(
                    rect.right_center() - Vec2::new(14.0, 0.0),
                    Align2::RIGHT_CENTER,
                    badge,
                    FontId::proportional(10.5),
                    if selected { theme::ACCENT } else { theme::FAINT },
                );
            }
        }
        if response.clicked() {
            self.tab = tab;
            if tab == Tab::Memory && self.is_attached() && !self.mem_loaded {
                if let Some(hit) = self.hits.first() {
                    self.goto_memory(hit.address);
                } else if let Some(entry) = self.entries.first() {
                    self.goto_memory(entry.address);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn ui_statusbar(&mut self, ctx: &egui::Context) {
        egui::TopBottomPanel::bottom("zero_status")
            .exact_height(30.0)
            .frame(
                Frame::none()
                    .fill(theme::PANEL)
                    .inner_margin(Margin::symmetric(18.0, 6.0))
                    .stroke(Stroke::new(1.0, theme::BORDER_SOFT)),
            )
            .show(ctx, |ui| {
                ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                    let running = self.scan_phase == ScanPhase::Running
                        || self.ptr_phase == ScanPhase::Running
                        || self.aob_phase == ScanPhase::Running;
                    let color = if running {
                        theme::WARN
                    } else if self.is_attached() {
                        theme::SUCCESS
                    } else {
                        theme::FAINT
                    };
                    ui.painter().circle_filled(
                        ui.cursor().left_center() + Vec2::new(4.0, 0.0),
                        3.0,
                        color,
                    );
                    ui.add_space(12.0);
                    let status = if self.scan_phase == ScanPhase::Running {
                        format!("扫描中 {:.0}%", self.progress() * 100.0)
                    } else if self.ptr_phase == ScanPhase::Running {
                        format!("指针扫描中 · 已扫 {} 个指针", self.ptr_scanned)
                    } else if self.aob_phase == ScanPhase::Running {
                        "AOB 扫描中 · 逐段匹配特征码".to_string()
                    } else if self.is_attached() {
                        format!("已连接 · {} 个候选地址", self.hits.len())
                    } else {
                        "就绪 · 选择一个进程开始".to_string()
                    };
                    ui.label(egui::RichText::new(status).size(11.0).color(theme::MUTED));
                    ui.with_layout(Layout::right_to_left(Align::Center), |ui| {
                        ui.label(
                            egui::RichText::new("单机 / 离线 · 无注入")
                                .size(10.5)
                                .color(theme::FAINT),
                        );
                        ui.label(
                            egui::RichText::new("扫描引擎 v0.1")
                                .size(10.5)
                                .color(theme::FAINT),
                        );
                    });
                });
            });
    }

    pub fn ui_scan(&mut self, ui: &mut egui::Ui) {
        ui.horizontal(|ui| {
            ui.vertical(|ui| {
                ui.label(egui::RichText::new("扫描").size(26.0).strong());
                ui.label(
                    egui::RichText::new("从一个你看到的数值开始，Zero 会帮你一步步缩小范围。")
                        .size(12.5)
                        .color(theme::MUTED),
                );
            });
            ui.with_layout(Layout::right_to_left(Align::Center), |ui| {
                if theme::outline_button(ui, "智能助手", true).clicked() {
                    self.tab = Tab::Assistant;
                }
                theme::status_chip(
                    ui,
                    &format!("{} 个候选", self.hits.len()),
                    if self.hits.is_empty() {
                        theme::FAINT
                    } else {
                        theme::ACCENT_2
                    },
                );
            });
        });
        ui.add_space(4.0);

        let full = ui.available_width();
        let right_w = 322.0;
        let left_w = (full - right_w - 14.0).max(520.0);
        ui.horizontal_top(|ui| {
            ui.vertical(|ui| {
                ui.set_width(left_w);
                self.scan_panel(ui);
            });
            ui.add_space(14.0);
            ui.vertical(|ui| {
                ui.set_width(right_w);
                self.assistant_side(ui);
            });
        });

        ui.add_space(14.0);
        self.results_panel(ui);
    }

    fn scan_panel(&mut self, ui: &mut egui::Ui) {
        let mut do_first = false;
        let mut do_next = false;
        let mut do_undo = false;
        let mut do_stop = false;
        let mut new_scan = false;
        let mut preset: Option<Preset> = None;
        let running = self.scan_phase == ScanPhase::Running;

        theme::card().show(ui, |ui| {
            ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                theme::section_label(ui, "目标数值");
                ui.add_space(4.0);
                ui.label(
                    egui::RichText::new("在游戏里看到的当前值")
                        .size(10.5)
                        .color(theme::FAINT),
                );
                ui.with_layout(Layout::right_to_left(Align::Center), |ui| {
                    theme::tag(ui, "LOCAL", theme::ACCENT_2);
                });
            });
            ui.add_space(2.0);
            Frame::none()
                .fill(Color32::from_rgb(10, 15, 25))
                .stroke(Stroke::new(
                    1.2,
                    if running { theme::WARN } else { theme::ACCENT.gamma_multiply(0.65) },
                ))
                .rounding(Rounding::same(13.0))
                .inner_margin(Margin::symmetric(14.0, 8.0))
                .show(ui, |ui| {
                    ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                        let (icon_rect, _) =
                            ui.allocate_exact_size(Vec2::splat(20.0), Sense::hover());
                        theme::paint_icon(
                            ui.painter(),
                            icon_rect.shrink(2.0),
                            IconKind::Scan,
                            theme::ACCENT,
                        );
                        let response = ui.add_sized(
                            [ui.available_width() - 4.0, 34.0],
                            egui::TextEdit::singleline(&mut self.scan_value)
                                .font(FontId::proportional(28.0))
                                .frame(false)
                                .hint_text("输入数值，例如 100"),
                        );
                        if response.lost_focus()
                            && ui.input(|i| i.key_pressed(egui::Key::Enter))
                            && !running
                        {
                            do_first = true;
                        }
                    });
                });

            ui.add_space(6.0);
            ui.horizontal_wrapped(|ui| {
                ui.label(egui::RichText::new("试试").size(11.0).color(theme::FAINT));
                for p in smart::PRESETS {
                    if theme::pill(ui, p.label, false).clicked() {
                        preset = Some(p);
                    }
                }
                if theme::pill(ui, "自动识别类型", self.vtype == VType::Auto).clicked() {
                    self.vtype = VType::Auto;
                }
            });

            ui.add_space(8.0);
            ui.horizontal_wrapped(|ui| {
                ui.label(
                    egui::RichText::new("数值类型")
                        .size(11.0)
                        .color(theme::FAINT),
                );
                for ty in VType::SELECTABLE {
                    if type_pill(ui, ty, self.vtype == ty).clicked() {
                        self.vtype = ty;
                    }
                }
            });

            ui.add_space(4.0);
            if self.hits.is_empty() {
                ui.horizontal_wrapped(|ui| {
                    ui.label(
                        egui::RichText::new("首次扫描")
                            .size(11.0)
                            .color(theme::FAINT),
                    );
                    for st in ScanType::PRIMARY {
                        if theme::pill(ui, st.label(), self.scan_type == st).clicked() {
                            self.scan_type = st;
                        }
                    }
                });
            } else {
                ui.horizontal_wrapped(|ui| {
                    ui.label(
                        egui::RichText::new("数值如何变化")
                            .size(11.0)
                            .color(theme::FAINT),
                    );
                    for st in ScanType::RELATIVE {
                        if theme::pill(ui, st.label(), self.scan_type == st).clicked() {
                            self.scan_type = st;
                        }
                    }
                    for st in [ScanType::Exact, ScanType::Bigger, ScanType::Smaller] {
                        if theme::pill(ui, st.label(), self.scan_type == st).clicked() {
                            self.scan_type = st;
                        }
                    }
                    if theme::pill(ui, "清空重来", false).clicked() {
                        new_scan = true;
                    }
                });
            }
            if self.scan_type.needs_two() {
                ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                    ui.label(egui::RichText::new("范围下限 / 上限").size(11.0).color(theme::FAINT));
                    ui.add_sized(
                        [130.0, 28.0],
                        egui::TextEdit::singleline(&mut self.scan_value)
                            .hint_text("下限")
                            .font(FontId::proportional(13.0)),
                    );
                    ui.add_sized(
                        [130.0, 28.0],
                        egui::TextEdit::singleline(&mut self.scan_value2)
                            .hint_text("上限")
                            .font(FontId::proportional(13.0)),
                    );
                });
            }

            ui.add_space(11.0);
            if running {
                progress_bar(ui, self.progress());
                ui.add_space(5.0);
                ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                    ui.label(
                        egui::RichText::new(format!(
                            "{:.0}%  ·  {}  ·  {}",
                            self.progress() * 100.0,
                            self.result_mb(),
                            format_elapsed(
                                self.scan_job
                                    .as_ref()
                                    .map(|j| j.started.elapsed())
                                    .unwrap_or_default()
                            )
                        ))
                        .size(11.0)
                        .color(theme::MUTED),
                    );
                    ui.with_layout(Layout::right_to_left(Align::Center), |ui| {
                        if theme::outline_button(ui, "停止扫描", true).clicked() {
                            do_stop = true;
                        }
                    });
                });
            } else {
                ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                    let value_ok = if self.scan_type.needs_value() {
                        !self.scan_value.trim().is_empty()
                    } else {
                        true
                    };
                    let second_ok = if self.scan_type.needs_two() {
                        !self.scan_value2.trim().is_empty()
                    } else {
                        true
                    };
                    let can_first = self.is_attached()
                        && self.scan_type.first_scan_ok()
                        && value_ok
                        && second_ok;
                    if theme::gradient_button(ui, "首次扫描", 148.0, can_first).clicked() && can_first {
                        do_first = true;
                    }
                    let can_next = self.is_attached()
                        && !self.hits.is_empty()
                        && self.scan_type != ScanType::Unknown
                        && value_ok
                        && second_ok;
                    if theme::outline_button(ui, "再次扫描", can_next).clicked() && can_next {
                        do_next = true;
                    }
                    if theme::outline_button(ui, "撤销", !self.hit_history.is_empty()).clicked()
                        && !self.hit_history.is_empty()
                    {
                        do_undo = true;
                    }
                    ui.with_layout(Layout::right_to_left(Align::Center), |ui| {
```
