```rs
            ui.set_width(ui.available_width());
            ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                ui.label(
                    egui::RichText::new("目标地址")
                        .size(11.0)
                        .color(theme::FAINT),
                );
                ui.add_sized(
                    [220.0, 32.0],
                    egui::TextEdit::singleline(&mut self.inj_addr)
                        .font(FontId::monospace(13.0))
                        .hint_text("0x7FF6..."),
                );
                ui.add_space(8.0);
                ui.label(egui::RichText::new("字节").size(11.0).color(theme::FAINT));
                ui.add_sized(
                    [280.0, 32.0],
                    egui::TextEdit::singleline(&mut self.inj_bytes)
                        .font(FontId::monospace(13.0))
                        .hint_text("90 90 C3"),
                );
            });
            ui.add_space(8.0);
            ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                if theme::gradient_button(ui, "写入并记录", 138.0, has_process).clicked()
                    && has_process
                {
                    apply = true;
                }
                ui.add_space(8.0);
                if theme::outline_button(ui, "全部还原", !self.inj_patches.is_empty()).clicked()
                    && !self.inj_patches.is_empty()
                {
                    revert_all = true;
                }
            });
            if let Some(error) = &self.inj_error {
                ui.add_space(4.0);
                ui.label(egui::RichText::new(error).size(11.0).color(theme::DANGER));
            }
            ui.add_space(6.0);
            ui.label(
                egui::RichText::new(
                    "写入前会先读出原始字节，成功后才记录；页面保护写前抬高、写后恢复。",
                )
                .size(10.5)
                .color(theme::FAINT),
            );
        });
        ui.add_space(14.0);

        theme::card().show(ui, |ui| {
            ui.set_width(ui.available_width());
            ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                theme::section_label(ui, "代码洞");
                ui.add_space(8.0);
                ui.add_sized(
                    [90.0, 28.0],
                    egui::TextEdit::singleline(&mut self.inj_cave_size)
                        .font(FontId::monospace(12.0)),
                );
                if theme::outline_button(ui, "分配", has_process).clicked() && has_process {
                    allocate = true;
                }
                if !self.inj_caves.is_empty()
                    && theme::outline_button(ui, "释放所选", has_process).clicked()
                    && has_process
                {
                    free_cave = true;
                }
            });
            ui.add_space(6.0);
            if self.inj_caves.is_empty() {
                ui.label(
                    egui::RichText::new("还没有代码洞。分配一块可执行内存，用来放跳板或自己的代码。")
                        .size(12.0)
                        .color(theme::FAINT),
                );
            } else {
                for (index, cave) in self.inj_caves.iter().enumerate() {
                    theme::soft_card().show(ui, |ui| {
                        ui.set_width(ui.available_width());
                        ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                            let selected = index == self.inj_selected_cave;
                            if ui
                                .selectable_label(
                                    selected,
                                    egui::RichText::new(format!("{:016X}", cave.address))
                                        .size(12.5)
                                        .font(FontId::monospace(12.5)),
                                )
                                .clicked()
                            {
                                self.inj_selected_cave = index;
                            }
                            theme::tag(
                                ui,
                                &format!("{} 字节", cave.size),
                                if selected { theme::ACCENT } else { theme::MUTED },
                            );
                            ui.with_layout(Layout::right_to_left(Align::Center), |ui| {
                                if theme::outline_button(ui, "看代码", true).clicked() {
                                    jump_to_disasm = Some(cave.address);
                                }
                            });
                        });
                    });
                    ui.add_space(4.0);
                }
            }
        });
        ui.add_space(14.0);

        theme::card().show(ui, |ui| {
            ui.set_width(ui.available_width());
            ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                theme::section_label(ui, "补丁记录");
                ui.add_space(4.0);
                ui.label(
                    egui::RichText::new("每条都带着原始字节，可逐条还原。")
                        .size(10.5)
                        .color(theme::FAINT),
                );
            });
            ui.add_space(8.0);
            if self.inj_patches.is_empty() {
                ui.label(
                    egui::RichText::new("还没有补丁。填入地址和字节后点「写入并记录」。")
                        .size(12.0)
                        .color(theme::FAINT),
                );
                return;
            }
            egui::ScrollArea::vertical()
                .max_height(360.0)
                .auto_shrink([false, false])
                .show(ui, |ui| {
                    for (index, patch) in self.inj_patches.iter().enumerate() {
                        theme::soft_card().show(ui, |ui| {
                            ui.set_width(ui.available_width());
                            ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                                ui.vertical(|ui| {
                                    ui.label(
                                        egui::RichText::new(format!("{:016X}", patch.address))
                                            .size(12.5)
                                            .strong()
                                            .font(FontId::monospace(12.5)),
                                    );
                                    ui.add_space(2.0);
                                    ui.label(
                                        egui::RichText::new(format!(
                                            "原 {} → 现 {}（{} 字节）",
                                            patch.original_hex(),
                                            patch.written_hex(),
                                            patch.size()
                                        ))
                                        .size(10.5)
                                        .color(theme::FAINT)
                                        .font(FontId::monospace(10.5)),
                                    );
                                });
                                ui.with_layout(Layout::right_to_left(Align::Center), |ui| {
                                    theme::tag(
                                        ui,
                                        if patch.active { "生效中" } else { "已还原" },
                                        if patch.active {
                                            theme::WARN
                                        } else {
                                            theme::SUCCESS
                                        },
                                    );
                                    if patch.active
                                        && theme::outline_button(ui, "还原", true).clicked()
                                    {
                                        revert_one = Some(index);
                                    }
                                    if theme::outline_button(ui, "看代码", true).clicked() {
                                        jump_to_disasm = Some(patch.address);
                                    }
                                });
                            });
                        });
                        ui.add_space(4.0);
                    }
                });
        });

        if allocate {
            self.allocate_cave();
        }
        if free_cave {
            self.free_selected_cave();
        }
        if apply {
            self.apply_patch();
        }
        if revert_all {
            self.revert_all_patches();
        }
        if let Some(index) = revert_one {
            self.revert_patch(index);
        }
        if let Some(address) = jump_to_disasm {
            self.jump_to_disasm(address);
        }
    }

    pub fn ui_asm(&mut self, ui: &mut egui::Ui) {
        let mut assemble = false;
        let mut send = false;
        let (bytes_len, label_count) = match &self.asm_result {
            Some(r) => (r.bytes.len(), r.labels.len()),
            None => (0, 0),
        };

        ui.horizontal(|ui| {
            ui.vertical(|ui| {
                ui.label(egui::RichText::new("自动汇编").size(26.0).strong());
                ui.label(
                    egui::RichText::new("把汇编文本编译成机器码，带标签，可直接送去打补丁。")
                        .size(12.5)
                        .color(theme::MUTED),
                );
            });
            ui.with_layout(Layout::right_to_left(Align::Center), |ui| {
                theme::status_chip(
                    ui,
                    &format!("{bytes_len} 字节 · {label_count} 标签"),
                    if self.asm_result.is_some() {
                        theme::ACCENT_2
                    } else {
                        theme::FAINT
                    },
                );
            });
        });
        ui.add_space(4.0);

        theme::card().show(ui, |ui| {
            ui.set_width(ui.available_width());
            ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                ui.label(
                    egui::RichText::new("起始地址")
                        .size(11.0)
                        .color(theme::FAINT),
                );
                ui.add_sized(
                    [200.0, 30.0],
                    egui::TextEdit::singleline(&mut self.asm_origin)
                        .font(FontId::monospace(12.5))
                        .hint_text("0x7FF6..."),
                );
                ui.add_space(8.0);
                ui.label(
                    egui::RichText::new("标签地址从起始地址算起")
                        .size(10.5)
                        .color(theme::FAINT),
                );
            });
            ui.add_space(8.0);
            ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                if theme::gradient_button(ui, "汇编", 96.0, true).clicked() {
                    assemble = true;
                }
                if theme::outline_button(ui, "送去打补丁", self.asm_result.is_some()).clicked()
                    && self.asm_result.is_some()
                {
                    send = true;
                }
                if theme::outline_button(ui, "填入示例", true).clicked() {
                    self.asm_source =
                        "; 给一个函数开头打补丁\nstart:\n  push rbp\n  mov rbp, rsp\n  sub rsp, 0x40\n  mov eax, 1\n  jmp start"
                            .to_string();
                }
            });
            if let Some(error) = &self.asm_error {
                ui.add_space(4.0);
                ui.label(egui::RichText::new(error).size(11.0).color(theme::DANGER));
            }
        });
        ui.add_space(12.0);

        theme::card().show(ui, |ui| {
            ui.set_width(ui.available_width());
            ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                theme::section_label(ui, "汇编源");
                ui.add_space(4.0);
                ui.label(
                    egui::RichText::new("支持标签、注释（; 或 //）、0x 十六进制、[基址+索引*缩放+偏移]。")
                        .size(10.5)
                        .color(theme::FAINT),
                );
            });
            ui.add_space(6.0);
            egui::ScrollArea::vertical()
                .id_source("zero_asm_source")
                .max_height(240.0)
                .auto_shrink([false, false])
                .show(ui, |ui| {
                    ui.add_sized(
                        [ui.available_width(), 220.0],
                        egui::TextEdit::multiline(&mut self.asm_source)
                            .font(FontId::monospace(13.0))
                            .desired_width(f32::INFINITY)
                            .hint_text("mov rax, rbx"),
                    );
                });
        });
        ui.add_space(12.0);

        theme::card().show(ui, |ui| {
            ui.set_width(ui.available_width());
            ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                theme::section_label(ui, "机器码");
                ui.add_space(4.0);
            });
            ui.add_space(8.0);
            let Some(result) = self.asm_result.as_ref() else {
                ui.label(
                    egui::RichText::new("还没有结果。写好汇编后点「汇编」。")
                        .size(12.0)
                        .color(theme::FAINT),
                );
                return;
            };
            if !result.labels.is_empty() {
                ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                    for (name, address) in &result.labels {
                        theme::tag(ui, &format!("{name} = {:X}", address), theme::ACCENT);
                    }
                });
                ui.add_space(6.0);
            }
            ui.label(
                egui::RichText::new(result.hex())
                    .size(12.5)
                    .color(theme::TEXT)
                    .font(FontId::monospace(12.5)),
            );
            ui.add_space(8.0);
            theme::divider(ui);
            ui.add_space(6.0);
            ui.label(
                egui::RichText::new("回读校验（反汇编这些字节）")
                    .size(10.5)
                    .color(theme::FAINT),
            );
            ui.add_space(4.0);
            let back = disasm::disassemble(&result.bytes, result.origin, true, 64);
            egui::ScrollArea::vertical()
                .id_source("zero_asm_readback")
                .max_height(200.0)
                .auto_shrink([false, false])
                .show(ui, |ui| {
                    for insn in &back {
                        ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                            ui.label(
                                egui::RichText::new(format!("{:016X}", insn.address))
                                    .size(11.0)
                                    .color(theme::ACCENT)
                                    .font(FontId::monospace(11.0)),
                            );
                            ui.add_space(6.0);
                            ui.label(
                                egui::RichText::new(format!("{:<20}", insn.hex()))
                                    .size(11.0)
                                    .color(theme::FAINT)
                                    .font(FontId::monospace(11.0)),
                            );
                            ui.label(
                                egui::RichText::new(&insn.text)
                                    .size(12.0)
                                    .color(theme::TEXT)
                                    .font(FontId::monospace(12.0)),
                            );
                        });
                    }
                });
        });

        if assemble {
            self.assemble_source();
        }
        if send {
            self.send_asm_to_patch();
        }
    }

    pub fn ui_table(&mut self, ui: &mut egui::Ui) {
        let count = self.ct_table.as_ref().map(|t| t.entries.len()).unwrap_or(0);
        let mut open = false;
        let mut save = false;
        let mut import = false;
        let mut export = false;

        ui.horizontal(|ui| {
            ui.vertical(|ui| {
                ui.label(egui::RichText::new("CT 表格").size(26.0).strong());
                ui.label(
                    egui::RichText::new("读写 Cheat Engine 的 .ct 文件，和原版表格互通。")
                        .size(12.5)
                        .color(theme::MUTED),
                );
            });
            ui.with_layout(Layout::right_to_left(Align::Center), |ui| {
                theme::status_chip(
                    ui,
                    &format!("{count} 条"),
                    if count > 0 { theme::ACCENT_2 } else { theme::FAINT },
                );
            });
        });
        ui.add_space(4.0);

        theme::card().show(ui, |ui| {
            ui.set_width(ui.available_width());
            ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                ui.label(egui::RichText::new("文件").size(11.0).color(theme::FAINT));
                ui.add_sized(
                    [380.0, 32.0],
                    egui::TextEdit::singleline(&mut self.ct_path)
                        .font(FontId::monospace(12.5))
                        .hint_text("C:\\tables\\game.ct"),
                );
                if theme::outline_button(ui, "打开", true).clicked() {
                    open = true;
                }
                if theme::outline_button(ui, "保存", count > 0).clicked() && count > 0 {
                    save = true;
                }
            });
            ui.add_space(8.0);
            ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                if theme::gradient_button(ui, "导入到地址列表", 168.0, count > 0).clicked()
                    && count > 0
                {
                    import = true;
                }
                ui.add_space(8.0);
                if theme::outline_button(ui, "从地址列表生成", !self.entries.is_empty()).clicked()
                    && !self.entries.is_empty()
                {
                    export = true;
                }
            });
            if let Some(error) = &self.ct_error {
                ui.add_space(4.0);
                ui.label(egui::RichText::new(error).size(11.0).color(theme::DANGER));
            }
            if !self.ct_status.is_empty() {
                ui.add_space(4.0);
                ui.label(
                    egui::RichText::new(&self.ct_status)
                        .size(10.5)
                        .color(theme::SUCCESS),
                );
            }
        });
        ui.add_space(12.0);

        theme::card().show(ui, |ui| {
            ui.set_width(ui.available_width());
            ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                theme::section_label(ui, "条目");
                ui.add_space(4.0);
                ui.label(
                    egui::RichText::new("地址支持模块+偏移、[指针]+偏移 与绝对地址。")
                        .size(10.5)
                        .color(theme::FAINT),
                );
            });
            ui.add_space(8.0);
            let Some(table) = self.ct_table.as_ref() else {
                ui.label(
                    egui::RichText::new("还没有打开表格。填入 .ct 路径后点「打开」。")
                        .size(12.0)
                        .color(theme::FAINT),
                );
                return;
            };
            if table.entries.is_empty() {
                ui.label(
                    egui::RichText::new("这个表格里没有 cheat entry。")
                        .size(12.0)
                        .color(theme::FAINT),
                );
                return;
            }
            egui::ScrollArea::vertical()
                .id_source("zero_ct_entries")
                .max_height(400.0)
                .auto_shrink([false, false])
                .show(ui, |ui| {
                    for entry in &table.entries {
                        theme::soft_card().show(ui, |ui| {
                            ui.set_width(ui.available_width());
                            ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                                ui.vertical(|ui| {
                                    ui.label(
                                        egui::RichText::new(if entry.description.is_empty() {
                                            "(无描述)".to_string()
                                        } else {
                                            entry.description.clone()
                                        })
                                        .size(12.5)
                                        .strong(),
                                    );
                                    ui.add_space(2.0);
                                    ui.label(
                                        egui::RichText::new(format!(
                                            "{}  ·  {}",
                                            entry.address, entry.vtype.short()
                                        ))
                                        .size(10.5)
                                        .color(theme::FAINT)
                                        .font(FontId::monospace(10.5)),
                                    );
                                });
                                ui.with_layout(Layout::right_to_left(Align::Center), |ui| {
                                    theme::tag(
                                        ui,
                                        entry.vtype.label(),
                                        theme::MUTED,
                                    );
                                    if let Some(value) = &entry.value {
                                        theme::tag(ui, value, theme::WARN);
                                    }
                                });
                            });
                        });
                        ui.add_space(4.0);
                    }
                });
        });

        if open {
            self.open_ct_table();
        }
        if save {
            self.save_ct_table();
        }
        if import {
            self.import_ct_entries();
        }
        if export {
            self.export_entries_to_ct();
        }
    }

    pub fn ui_speed(&mut self, ui: &mut egui::Ui) {
        let running = self.speed_on;
        let attached = self.is_attached();
        let mut start = false;
        let mut stop = false;
        let mut preset: Option<f64> = None;

        ui.horizontal(|ui| {
            ui.vertical(|ui| {
                ui.label(egui::RichText::new("变速齿轮").size(26.0).strong());
                ui.label(
                    egui::RichText::new("周期性暂停目标进程的线程，让游戏变慢或变快。")
                        .size(12.5)
                        .color(theme::MUTED),
                );
            });
            ui.with_layout(Layout::right_to_left(Align::Center), |ui| {
                if running {
                    theme::status_chip(ui, "变速中", theme::WARN);
                } else {
                    theme::status_chip(ui, "未启用", theme::FAINT);
                }
            });
        });
        ui.add_space(4.0);

        theme::card().show(ui, |ui| {
            ui.set_width(ui.available_width());
            ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                theme::section_label(ui, "倍率");
                ui.add_space(8.0);
                ui.add(
                    egui::Slider::new(&mut self.speed_factor, 0.1..=10.0)
                        .step_by(0.05)
                        .fixed_decimals(2)
                        .suffix("x"),
                );
            });
            ui.add_space(8.0);
            ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                for (label, value) in [
                    ("0.25x 极慢", 0.25),
                    ("0.5x 慢", 0.5),
                    ("1x 正常", 1.0),
                    ("2x 快", 2.0),
                    ("4x 极快", 4.0),
                ] {
                    if theme::outline_button(ui, label, true).clicked() {
                        preset = Some(value);
                    }
                }
            });
            ui.add_space(10.0);
            ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                if !running {
                    if theme::gradient_button(ui, "开启变速", 128.0, attached).clicked() && attached {
                        start = true;
                    }
                } else if theme::outline_button(ui, "停止并恢复", true).clicked() {
                    stop = true;
                }
                ui.add_space(10.0);
                if running {
                    theme::status_chip(
                        ui,
                        &format!("{} 个线程", self.speed_threads),
                        theme::ACCENT_2,
                    );
                }
            });
            ui.add_space(6.0);
            ui.label(
                egui::RichText::new(self.speed_description())
                    .size(10.5)
                    .color(theme::FAINT),
            );
            if let Some(error) = &self.speed_error {
                ui.add_space(4.0);
                ui.label(egui::RichText::new(error).size(11.0).color(theme::DANGER));
            }
        });
        ui.add_space(12.0);

        theme::card().show(ui, |ui| {
            ui.set_width(ui.available_width());
            theme::section_label(ui, "说明");
            ui.add_space(6.0);
            for line in [
                "原理：每 40 ms 为一个周期，先把目标进程的所有线程挂起，再放行其中一小段时间。",
                "放行时间与周期的比例就是倍率：0.5x 只放行四分之一，2x 放行三分之二。",
                "每个周期结束都会把挂起的线程全部恢复，不会留下卡死的进程。",
                "不能对自己挂起（Zero 自己就是被挂起的进程时会直接拒绝）。",
            ] {
                ui.label(
                    egui::RichText::new(format!("· {line}"))
                        .size(11.0)
                        .color(theme::MUTED),
                );
                ui.add_space(2.0);
            }
        });

        if let Some(value) = preset {
            self.speed_factor = value;
        }
        if start {
            self.start_speed();
        }
        if stop {
            self.stop_speed();
        }
    }

    pub fn ui_script(&mut self, ui: &mut egui::Ui) {
        let mut run = false;
        let attached = self.is_attached();

        ui.horizontal(|ui| {
            ui.vertical(|ui| {
                ui.label(egui::RichText::new("Lua 脚本").size(26.0).strong());
                ui.label(
                    egui::RichText::new("执行 Cheat Engine 风格的 Lua 脚本，直接读写目标进程。")
                        .size(12.5)
                        .color(theme::MUTED),
                );
            });
            ui.with_layout(Layout::right_to_left(Align::Center), |ui| {
                if attached {
                    theme::status_chip(ui, "已连接", theme::SUCCESS);
                } else {
                    theme::status_chip(ui, "未连接", theme::FAINT);
                }
            });
        });
        ui.add_space(4.0);

        theme::card().show(ui, |ui| {
            ui.set_width(ui.available_width());
            ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                if theme::gradient_button(ui, "运行", 96.0, true).clicked() {
                    run = true;
                }
                if theme::outline_button(ui, "填入示例", true).clicked() {
                    self.script_source = "\n-- 读写目标进程\nlocal addr = getAddress(\"game.exe+1A2B\")\nprint(\"生命值地址\", string.format(\"%X\", addr))\nprint(\"当前值\", readInteger(addr, 4))\nwriteInteger(addr, 100, 4)\nprint(\"已写入 100\")"
                        .to_string();
                }
                ui.add_space(10.0);
                if !attached {
                    ui.label(
                        egui::RichText::new("未连接进程时只能跑纯计算脚本。")
                            .size(10.5)
                            .color(theme::FAINT),
                    );
                }
            });
        });
        ui.add_space(12.0);

        theme::card().show(ui, |ui| {
            ui.set_width(ui.available_width());
            ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                theme::section_label(ui, "脚本");
                ui.add_space(4.0);
                ui.label(
                    egui::RichText::new("可用：print / readInteger / writeInteger / readBytes / writeBytes / getAddress")
                        .size(10.5)
                        .color(theme::FAINT),
                );
            });
            ui.add_space(6.0);
            egui::ScrollArea::vertical()
                .id_source("zero_script_source")
                .max_height(260.0)
                .auto_shrink([false, false])
                .show(ui, |ui| {
                    ui.add_sized(
                        [ui.available_width(), 240.0],
                        egui::TextEdit::multiline(&mut self.script_source)
                            .font(FontId::monospace(13.0))
                            .desired_width(f32::INFINITY)
                            .hint_text("print(\"hello\")"),
                    );
                });
        });
        ui.add_space(12.0);

        theme::card().show(ui, |ui| {
            ui.set_width(ui.available_width());
            theme::section_label(ui, "输出");
            ui.add_space(6.0);
            if let Some(error) = &self.script_error {
                ui.label(
                    egui::RichText::new(error)
                        .size(11.5)
                        .color(theme::DANGER)
                        .font(FontId::monospace(11.5)),
                );
                return;
            }
            if self.script_output.is_empty() {
                ui.label(
                    egui::RichText::new("还没有输出。")
                        .size(12.0)
                        .color(theme::FAINT),
                );
                return;
            }
            egui::ScrollArea::vertical()
                .id_source("zero_script_output")
                .max_height(260.0)
                .auto_shrink([false, false])
                .show(ui, |ui| {
                    for line in &self.script_output {
                        ui.label(
                            egui::RichText::new(line)
                                .size(12.0)
                                .color(theme::TEXT)
                                .font(FontId::monospace(12.0)),
                        );
                    }
                });
        });

        if run {
            self.run_script();
        }
    }

    /// The watch list: the few addresses most worth staring at.
    fn watch_panel(&mut self, ui: &mut egui::Ui) {
        let mut start = false;
        let mut stop = false;
        let mut promote = false;
        let running = self.watch_running;
        let count = self.watches.len();

        theme::card().show(ui, |ui| {
            ui.set_width(ui.available_width());
            ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                ui.label(egui::RichText::new("观察队列").size(14.0).strong());
                if count > 0 {
                    theme::tag(ui, &format!("{count} 个"), theme::ACCENT);
                }
                if running {
                    theme::tag(ui, "采样中", theme::SUCCESS);
                }
                ui.with_layout(Layout::right_to_left(Align::Center), |ui| {
                    if count > 0 && theme::outline_button(ui, "选取最佳", running).clicked() && running
                    {
                        promote = true;
                    }
                    if running {
                        if theme::outline_button(ui, "停止", true).clicked() {
                            stop = true;
                        }
                    } else if !self.ranked.is_empty()
                        && theme::gradient_button(ui, "观察最可能的", 132.0, true).clicked()
                    {
                        start = true;
                    }
                });
            });
            ui.add_space(6.0);

            if count == 0 {
                ui.label(
                    egui::RichText::new(
                        "扫描完成后，可以把最可能的几个地址放进观察队列：改变游戏里的数值，看哪个跟着动。",
                    )
                    .size(11.5)
                    .color(theme::FAINT),
                );
                return;
            }

            for watch in &self.watches {
                ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                    theme::tag(
                        ui,
                        match watch.behaviour_rank() {
                            3 => "持续变化",
                            2 => "偶尔变化",
                            1 => "一直没变",
                            _ => "未采样",
                        },
                        match watch.behaviour_rank() {
                            3 => theme::ACCENT_2,
                            2 => theme::SUCCESS,
                            1 => theme::FAINT,
                            _ => theme::FAINT,
                        },
                    );
                    ui.label(
                        egui::RichText::new(format!("{:016X}", watch.address))
                            .size(11.5)
                            .font(FontId::monospace(11.5))
                            .color(theme::ACCENT),
                    );
                    theme::tag(ui, watch.vtype.short(), theme::MUTED);
                    let current = watch
                        .current
                        .as_ref()
                        .map(|v| v.display())
                        .unwrap_or_else(|| "?".to_string());
                    ui.label(
                        egui::RichText::new(current)
                            .size(12.0)
                            .font(FontId::monospace(12.0))
                            .color(theme::TEXT),
                    );
                    if let Some(delta) = watch.delta() {
                        if delta.abs() > f64::EPSILON {
                            ui.label(
                                egui::RichText::new(format!("{delta:+.0}"))
                                    .size(11.0)
                                    .font(FontId::monospace(11.0))
                                    .color(if delta > 0.0 {
                                        theme::WARN
                                    } else {
                                        theme::DANGER
                                    }),
                            );
                        }
                    }
                    ui.with_layout(Layout::right_to_left(Align::Center), |ui| {
                        ui.label(
                            egui::RichText::new(format!("{} 分", watch.score))
                                .size(10.0)
                                .color(theme::FAINT),
                        );
                    });
                });
                ui.add_space(3.0);
            }
        });

        if start {
            self.start_watching();
        }
        if stop {
            self.stop_watching();
        }
        if promote {
            self.promote_best_watch();
        }
    }

    /// The MCP switch: on/off, the URL to paste into an agent, and a copy of
    /// the token. Deliberately visible and one click away, like a mobile tool's
    /// service toggle.
    pub fn mcp_panel(&mut self, ui: &mut egui::Ui) {
        let running = self.mcp.is_some();
        theme::card().show(ui, |ui| {
            ui.set_width(ui.available_width());
            ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                let (rect, _) = ui.allocate_exact_size(Vec2::splat(18.0), Sense::hover());
                theme::paint_icon(ui.painter(), rect.shrink(2.0), IconKind::Bolt, theme::ACCENT_2);
                ui.label(egui::RichText::new("MCP 服务").size(14.0).strong());
                ui.with_layout(Layout::right_to_left(Align::Center), |ui| {
                    if running {
                        theme::tag(ui, "运行中", theme::SUCCESS);
                    } else {
                        theme::tag(ui, "已关闭", theme::FAINT);
                    }
                });
            });
            ui.add_space(6.0);
            ui.label(
                egui::RichText::new(
                    "让外部 AI 助手通过 MCP 驱动 Zero 扫描与读写内存。关闭时端口不监听。",
                )
                .size(11.0)
                .color(theme::MUTED),
            );
            ui.add_space(8.0);
            ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                if theme::gradient_button(
                    ui,
                    if running { "停止服务" } else { "启动服务" },
                    112.0,
                    true,
                )
                .clicked()
                {
                    self.toggle_mcp();
                }
                if !running {
                    ui.add_space(8.0);
                    ui.label(egui::RichText::new("端口").size(10.5).color(theme::FAINT));
                    ui.add(
                        egui::DragValue::new(&mut self.mcp_port)
                            .clamp_range(1024..=65_000)
                            .speed(1.0),
                    );
                }
            });
            if let Some(service) = &self.mcp {
                ui.add_space(8.0);
                let url = service.url();
                ui.label(egui::RichText::new("连接地址").size(10.5).color(theme::FAINT));
                ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                    ui.label(
                        egui::RichText::new(&url)
                            .size(10.5)
                            .font(FontId::monospace(10.5))
                            .color(theme::TEXT),
                    );
                    if theme::outline_button(ui, "复制", true).clicked() {
                        ui.output_mut(|o| o.copied_text = url.clone());
                    }
                });
                ui.add_space(4.0);
                ui.label(
                    egui::RichText::new("POST 到该地址，JSON-RPC 2.0。GET 可做健康检查。")
                        .size(10.0)
                        .color(theme::FAINT),
                );
            }
            if let Some(error) = &self.mcp_error {
                ui.add_space(4.0);
                ui.label(egui::RichText::new(error).size(10.5).color(theme::DANGER));
            }
        });
    }

    /// Shows what the one-click verification found. Honest about what it did
    /// not cover: the full 20-item regression needs its own target program.
    pub fn e2e_panel(&mut self, ctx: &egui::Context) {
        if self.e2e_log.is_empty() {
            return;
        }
        let mut open = true;
        egui::Window::new("验证结果")
            .id(egui::Id::new("zero_e2e"))
            .open(&mut open)
            .collapsible(false)
            .resizable(false)
            .anchor(Align2::CENTER_CENTER, Vec2::ZERO)
            .frame(
                Frame::none()
                    .fill(theme::PANEL)
                    .stroke(Stroke::new(1.0, theme::BORDER))
                    .rounding(Rounding::same(14.0))
                    .inner_margin(Margin::same(20.0)),
            )
            .show(ctx, |ui| {
                ui.set_min_width(560.0);
                ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                    ui.label(egui::RichText::new("验证结果").size(18.0).strong());
                    ui.with_layout(Layout::right_to_left(Align::Center), |ui| {
                        match self.e2e_ok {
                            Some(true) => theme::tag(ui, "全部通过", theme::SUCCESS),
                            Some(false) => theme::tag(ui, "有失败项", theme::DANGER),
                            None => theme::tag(ui, "未运行", theme::FAINT),
                        };
                    });
                });
                ui.add_space(8.0);
                theme::divider(ui);
                ui.add_space(6.0);
                egui::ScrollArea::vertical()
                    .id_source("zero_e2e_log")
                    .max_height(360.0)
                    .auto_shrink([false, false])
                    .show(ui, |ui| {
                        for line in self.e2e_log.lines() {
                            let colour = if line.starts_with("PASS") {
                                theme::SUCCESS
                            } else if line.starts_with("FAIL") {
                                theme::DANGER
                            } else {
                                theme::MUTED
                            };
                            ui.label(
                                egui::RichText::new(line)
                                    .size(11.5)
                                    .color(colour)
                                    .font(FontId::monospace(11.5)),
                            );
                        }
                    });
            });
        if !open {
            self.e2e_log.clear();
        }
    }

    /// The shortcut cheat-sheet, shown over everything else on demand.
    pub fn ui_hotkeys(&mut self, ctx: &egui::Context) {
        if !self.show_hotkeys {
            return;
        }
        let mut open = true;
        egui::Window::new("快捷键")
            .id(egui::Id::new("zero_hotkeys"))
            .open(&mut open)
            .collapsible(false)
            .resizable(false)
            .anchor(Align2::CENTER_CENTER, Vec2::ZERO)
            .frame(
                Frame::none()
                    .fill(theme::CARD)
                    .stroke(Stroke::new(1.0, theme::BORDER_SOFT))
                    .rounding(Rounding::same(14.0))
                    .inner_margin(Margin::symmetric(22.0, 18.0)),
            )
            .show(ctx, |ui| {
                ui.set_min_width(360.0);
                ui.label(
                    egui::RichText::new("照着 Cheat Engine 的习惯排的")
                        .size(11.0)
                        .color(theme::FAINT),
                );
                ui.add_space(10.0);
                for shortcut in hotkey::SHORTCUTS {
                    ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                        theme::tag(ui, shortcut.label, theme::ACCENT);
                        ui.add_space(10.0);
                        ui.label(
                            egui::RichText::new(shortcut.description)
                                .size(12.0)
                                .color(theme::TEXT),
                        );
                    });
                    ui.add_space(5.0);
                }
                ui.add_space(6.0);
                theme::divider(ui);
                ui.add_space(6.0);
                ui.label(
                    egui::RichText::new("输入框有焦点时不会抢按键；按 F1 或 Esc 关闭。")
                        .size(10.5)
                        .color(theme::FAINT),
                );
            });
        if !open {
            self.show_hotkeys = false;
        }
        if ctx.input(|i| i.key_pressed(egui::Key::Escape)) {
            self.show_hotkeys = false;
        }
    }

    pub fn ui_toasts(&mut self, ctx: &egui::Context) {
        self.toasts.retain(|t| t.at.elapsed() < Duration::from_secs(4));
        if self.toasts.is_empty() {
            return;
        }
        egui::Area::new(egui::Id::new("zero_toasts"))
            .anchor(Align2::RIGHT_TOP, Vec2::new(-20.0, 82.0))
            .order(egui::Order::Foreground)
            .show(ctx, |ui| {
                for toast in &self.toasts {
                    let (color, label) = theme::toast_style(toast.kind);
                    Frame::none()
                        .fill(theme::CARD)
                        .stroke(Stroke::new(1.0, color.gamma_multiply(0.55)))
                        .rounding(Rounding::same(11.0))
                        .inner_margin(Margin::symmetric(14.0, 10.0))
                        .shadow(egui::epaint::Shadow {
                            offset: Vec2::new(0.0, 8.0),
                            blur: 22.0,
                            spread: 0.0,
                            color: Color32::from_black_alpha(110),
                        })
                        .show(ui, |ui| {
                            ui.set_max_width(320.0);
                            ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                                let (rect, _) =
                                    ui.allocate_exact_size(Vec2::splat(18.0), Sense::hover());
                                ui.painter().circle_filled(rect.center(), 9.0, color.gamma_multiply(0.18));
                                ui.painter().text(
                                    rect.center(),
                                    Align2::CENTER_CENTER,
                                    "!",
                                    FontId::proportional(11.0),
                                    color,
                                );
                                ui.vertical(|ui| {
                                    ui.label(
                                        egui::RichText::new(label)
                                            .size(10.0)
                                            .color(color)
                                            .strong(),
                                    );
                                    ui.label(
                                        egui::RichText::new(&toast.text)
                                            .size(11.5)
                                            .color(theme::TEXT),
                                    );
                                });
                            });
                        });
                    ui.add_space(8.0);
                }
            });
    }

    pub fn ui_modals(&mut self, ctx: &egui::Context) {
        if self.picker_open {
            self.process_picker(ctx);
        }
        if self.show_settings {
            self.settings_modal(ctx);
        }
        if self.show_add {
            self.add_modal(ctx);
        }
        if self.editing.is_some() {
            self.edit_modal(ctx);
        }
    }

    fn process_picker(&mut self, ctx: &egui::Context) {
        let mut close = false;
        let mut refresh = false;
        let mut attach = None;
        egui::Window::new("process_picker")
            .id(egui::Id::new("zero_process_picker"))
            .title_bar(false)
            .collapsible(false)
            .resizable(false)
            .anchor(Align2::CENTER_CENTER, Vec2::ZERO)
            .fixed_size(Vec2::new(760.0, 610.0))
            .frame(
                Frame::none()
                    .fill(theme::PANEL)
                    .stroke(Stroke::new(1.0, theme::BORDER))
                    .rounding(Rounding::same(16.0))
                    .inner_margin(Margin::same(18.0))
                    .shadow(egui::epaint::Shadow {
                        offset: Vec2::new(0.0, 20.0),
                        blur: 60.0,
                        spread: 0.0,
                        color: Color32::from_black_alpha(150),
                    }),
            )
            .show(ctx, |ui| {
                ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                    ui.vertical(|ui| {
                        ui.label(egui::RichText::new("选择进程").size(20.0).strong());
                        ui.label(
                            egui::RichText::new("选择一个正在运行的游戏或练习靶场")
                                .size(11.5)
                                .color(theme::MUTED),
                        );
                    });
                    ui.with_layout(Layout::right_to_left(Align::Center), |ui| {
                        if theme::icon_button(ui, IconKind::Close).clicked() {
                            close = true;
                        }
                        if theme::icon_button(ui, IconKind::Refresh)
                            .on_hover_text("刷新进程列表")
                            .clicked()
                        {
                            refresh = true;
                        }
                    });
                });
                ui.add_space(8.0);
                ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                    ui.add_sized(
                        [ui.available_width() - 90.0, 34.0],
                        egui::TextEdit::singleline(&mut self.picker_search)
                            .hint_text("搜索进程名或 PID")
                            .font(FontId::proportional(13.0)),
                    );
                    ui.label(
                        egui::RichText::new(format!("{} 个进程", self.processes.len()))
                            .size(10.5)
                            .color(theme::FAINT),
                    );
                });
                ui.add_space(6.0);
                theme::divider(ui);
                if !self.picker_loaded {
                    ui.add_space(30.0);
                    ui.vertical_centered(|ui| {
                        ui.spinner();
                        ui.label(egui::RichText::new("正在读取进程…").size(12.0).color(theme::MUTED));
                    });
                    return;
                }
                let needle = self.picker_search.to_ascii_lowercase();
                let filtered: Vec<usize> = self
                    .processes
                    .iter()
                    .enumerate()
                    .filter(|(_, p)| {
                        needle.is_empty()
                            || p.name.to_ascii_lowercase().contains(&needle)
                            || p.pid.to_string().contains(&needle)
                    })
                    .map(|(i, _)| i)
                    .collect();
                if filtered.is_empty() {
                    ui.add_space(30.0);
                    ui.vertical_centered(|ui| {
                        ui.label(
                            egui::RichText::new("没有匹配的进程")
                                .size(13.0)
                                .color(theme::MUTED),
                        );
                    });
                } else {
                    egui::ScrollArea::vertical()
                        .max_height(400.0)
                        .auto_shrink([false, false])
                        .show_rows(ui, 50.0, filtered.len(), |ui, range| {
                            for row in range {
                                let info = &self.processes[filtered[row]];
                                let practice = self.practice_pid == Some(info.pid);
                                let (rect, response) = ui.allocate_exact_size(
                                    Vec2::new(ui.available_width(), 46.0),
                                    Sense::click(),
                                );
                                if response.hovered() {
                                    ui.painter().rect_filled(
                                        rect,
                                        Rounding::same(9.0),
                                        Color32::from_white_alpha(5),
                                    );
                                }
                                let icon = egui::Rect::from_center_size(
                                    rect.left_center() + Vec2::new(24.0, 0.0),
                                    Vec2::splat(28.0),
                                );
                                ui.painter().rect_filled(
                                    icon,
                                    Rounding::same(8.0),
                                    if practice {
                                        theme::ACCENT_2.gamma_multiply(0.16)
                                    } else {
                                        theme::ACCENT.gamma_multiply(0.10)
                                    },
                                );
                                theme::paint_icon(
                                    ui.painter(),
                                    icon.shrink(7.0),
                                    IconKind::Practice,
                                    if practice { theme::ACCENT_2 } else { theme::ACCENT },
                                );
                                ui.painter().text(
                                    rect.left_center() + Vec2::new(48.0, -8.0),
                                    Align2::LEFT_CENTER,
                                    if info.name.is_empty() { "系统进程" } else { &info.name },
                                    FontId::proportional(12.5),
                                    theme::TEXT,
                                );
                                let detail = format!(
                                    "PID {}  ·  {}{}",
                                    info.pid,
                                    info.arch,
                                    if practice { "  ·  练习靶场" } else { "" }
                                );
                                ui.painter().text(
                                    rect.left_center() + Vec2::new(48.0, 9.0),
                                    Align2::LEFT_CENTER,
                                    detail,
                                    FontId::proportional(10.0),
                                    if practice { theme::ACCENT_2 } else { theme::FAINT },
                                );
                                if ui
                                    .put(
                                        egui::Rect::from_center_size(
                                            rect.right_center() - Vec2::new(46.0, 0.0),
                                            Vec2::new(72.0, 28.0),
                                        ),
                                        egui::Button::new("连接").min_size(Vec2::new(72.0, 28.0)),
                                    )
                                    .clicked()
                                {
                                    attach = Some(info.pid);
                                } else if response.double_clicked() {
                                    attach = Some(info.pid);
                                }
                            }
                        });
                }
                ui.add_space(4.0);
                theme::divider(ui);
                ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                    ui.label(
                        egui::RichText::new(if self.processes.is_empty() {
                            "提示：有些系统进程需要管理员权限才能读取。"
                        } else {
                            "提示：游戏以管理员运行时，Zero 也需要管理员权限。"
                        })
                        .size(10.5)
                        .color(theme::FAINT),
                    );
                    ui.with_layout(Layout::right_to_left(Align::Center), |ui| {
                        if theme::outline_button(ui, "以管理员身份重启", true).clicked() {
                            winmem::relaunch_as_admin();
                        }
                    });
                });
                if let Some(error) = &self.attach_error {
                    ui.label(egui::RichText::new(error).size(11.0).color(theme::DANGER));
                }
            });
        if close {
            self.picker_open = false;
        }
        if refresh {
            self.refresh_processes();
        }
        if let Some(pid) = attach {
            if let Some(info) = self.processes.iter().find(|p| p.pid == pid).cloned() {
                self.attach_info(&info);
            }
        }
    }

    fn edit_modal(&mut self, ctx: &egui::Context) {

        let mut save = false;
        let mut take: Option<EditState> = None;
        if let Some(edit) = self.editing.as_mut() {

                .id(egui::Id::new("zero_edit_address"))
                .title_bar(false)

                .resizable(false)

                            offset: Vec2::new(0.0, 20.0),

                )
                .show(ctx, |ui| {

                            ui.label(egui::RichText::new("编辑地址").size(20.0).strong());
                            ui.label(
                                egui::RichText::new("修改会立即写入目标进程")
                                    .size(11.5)
                                    .color(theme::WARN),
                            );
                        });
                        ui.with_layout(Layout::right_to_left(Align::Center), |ui| {
                            if theme::icon_button(ui, IconKind::Close).clicked() {
                                close = true;
                            }
                        });
                    });
                    ui.add_space(10.0);
                    field_label(ui, "地址");
                    ui.add_sized(
                        [ui.available_width(), 32.0],
                        egui::TextEdit::singleline(&mut edit.address).font(FontId::monospace(13.0)),
                    );
                    field_label(ui, "描述");
                    ui.add_sized(
                        [ui.available_width(), 32.0],
                        egui::TextEdit::singleline(&mut edit.desc),
                    );
                    field_label(ui, "类型");
                    ui.horizontal_wrapped(|ui| {
                        for ty in [VType::I32, VType::I64, VType::F32, VType::F64, VType::I16, VType::I8, VType::Str] {
                            if theme::pill(ui, ty.label(), edit.vtype == ty).clicked() {
                                edit.vtype = ty;
                            }
                        }
                    });
                    field_label(ui, "值");
                    ui.add_sized(
                        [ui.available_width(), 34.0],
                        egui::TextEdit::singleline(&mut edit.value)
                            .font(FontId::monospace(16.0)),
                    );
                    ui.add_space(10.0);
                    ui.horizontal(|ui| {
                        if theme::gradient_button(ui, "保存并写入", 138.0, true).clicked() {
                            save = true;
                        }
                        if theme::outline_button(ui, "取消", true).clicked() {
                            close = true;
                        }
                    });
                });
        } else {
            close = true;
        }
        if close {
            self.editing = None;
        }
        if save {
            take = self.editing.take();
            self.editing = take;
            self.save_editor();
        }
    }
}

fn cell_label(ui: &mut egui::Ui, width: f32, text: &str, muted: bool) {
    cell_text(
        ui,
        width,
        text,
        FontId::proportional(11.5),
        if muted { theme::MUTED } else { theme::TEXT },
    );
}

fn cell_mono(ui: &mut egui::Ui, width: f32, text: &str, color: Color32) {
    cell_text(ui, width, text, FontId::monospace(11.5), color);
}

fn cell_text(ui: &mut egui::Ui, width: f32, text: &str, font: FontId, color: Color32) {
    let (rect, _) = ui.allocate_exact_size(Vec2::new(width, 24.0), Sense::hover());
    let galley = ui.painter().layout_no_wrap(text.to_string(), font, color);
    let clipped = ui.painter().with_clip_rect(rect);
    clipped.galley(
        egui::pos2(rect.left(), rect.center().y - galley.size().y / 2.0),
        galley,
        color,
    );
}

fn cell_tag(ui: &mut egui::Ui, width: f32, text: &str, color: Color32) {
    let (rect, _) = ui.allocate_exact_size(Vec2::new(width, 24.0), Sense::hover());
    let galley = ui
        .painter()
        .layout_no_wrap(text.to_string(), FontId::proportional(10.5), color);
    let tag_rect = egui::Rect::from_center_size(
        egui::pos2(rect.left() + 16.0, rect.center().y),
        Vec2::new(galley.size().x + 14.0, 19.0),
    );
    ui.painter()
        .rect_filled(tag_rect, Rounding::same(5.0), color.gamma_multiply(0.14));
    let clipped = ui.painter().with_clip_rect(rect);
    clipped.galley(
        tag_rect.center() - galley.size() / 2.0,
        galley,
        color,
    );
}

fn cell_button(ui: &mut egui::Ui, width: f32, text: &str, color: Color32) -> Response {
    ui.add_sized(
        [width, 24.0],
        egui::Button::new(egui::RichText::new(text).size(11.0).color(color)).frame(false),
    )
}

fn field_label(ui: &mut egui::Ui, text: &str) {
    ui.add_space(6.0);
    ui.label(egui::RichText::new(text).size(10.5).color(theme::FAINT));
}

fn type_color(ty: VType) -> Color32 {
    match ty {
        VType::F32 | VType::F64 => theme::ACCENT_2,
        VType::Str => theme::WARN,
        VType::Auto => theme::ACCENT,
        _ => theme::ACCENT,
    }
}

fn format_elapsed(duration: Duration) -> String {
    if duration.as_secs_f32() >= 1.0 {
        format!("{:.2}s", duration.as_secs_f32())
    } else {
        format!("{}ms", duration.as_millis())
    }
}

```

`app/src/winmem.rs`:

```rs
use crate::model::Region;
use std::ffi::c_void;
use std::mem::{size_of, zeroed};
use std::sync::Arc;

use windows_sys::Win32::Foundation::{CloseHandle, GetLastError, HANDLE, INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE};
use windows_sys::Win32::System::Diagnostics::Debug::{ReadProcessMemory, WriteProcessMemory};
use windows_sys::Win32::System::Diagnostics::ToolHelp::{
    CreateToolhelp32Snapshot, Module32FirstW, Module32NextW, Process32FirstW, Process32NextW,
    MODULEENTRY32W, PROCESSENTRY32W, TH32CS_SNAPMODULE, TH32CS_SNAPMODULE32, TH32CS_SNAPPROCESS,
};
use windows_sys::Win32::System::Memory::{
    VirtualAllocEx, VirtualFreeEx, VirtualProtectEx, VirtualQueryEx, MEMORY_BASIC_INFORMATION,
    MEM_COMMIT, MEM_RELEASE, MEM_RESERVE, PAGE_EXECUTE, PAGE_EXECUTE_READ, PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE,
    PAGE_EXECUTE_WRITECOPY, PAGE_GUARD, PAGE_NOACCESS, PAGE_READWRITE, PAGE_WRITECOPY,
};
use windows_sys::Win32::System::Threading::{
    IsWow64Process, OpenProcess, QueryFullProcessImageNameW, PROCESS_QUERY_INFORMATION,
    PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION, PROCESS_VM_OPERATION, PROCESS_VM_READ, PROCESS_VM_WRITE,
};

/// A loaded module (exe or dll) inside the target process. Pointer scan reports
/// static bases as "module + offset", which is what survives a restart.
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct ModuleInfo {
    pub name: String,
    pub base: u64,
    pub size: u64,
}

impl ModuleInfo {
    pub fn contains(&self, address: u64) -> bool {
        address >= self.base && address < self.base.saturating_add(self.size)
    }
    pub fn offset_of(&self, address: u64) -> u64 {
        address.saturating_sub(self.base)
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct ProcessInfo {
    pub pid: u32,
    pub name: String,
    pub arch: String,
    pub path: Option<String>,
}

pub struct ProcessHandle {
    handle: HANDLE,
    pub pid: u32,
    pub name: String,
    pub is_64bit: bool,
    pub writable: bool,
}

unsafe impl Send for ProcessHandle {}
unsafe impl Sync for ProcessHandle {}

impl Drop for ProcessHandle {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        if self.handle != 0 && self.handle != INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE {
            unsafe {
                CloseHandle(self.handle);
            }
        }
    }
}

impl ProcessHandle {
    pub fn read(&self, address: u64, buffer: &mut [u8]) -> bool {
        if buffer.is_empty() {
            return true;
        }
        let mut read = 0usize;
        unsafe {
            ReadProcessMemory(
                self.handle,
                address as *const c_void,
                buffer.as_mut_ptr() as *mut c_void,
                buffer.len(),
                &mut read,
            ) != 0
                && read == buffer.len()
        }
    }

    pub fn write(&self, address: u64, bytes: &[u8]) -> bool {
        if !self.writable || bytes.is_empty() {
            return false;
        }
        let mut written = 0usize;
        unsafe {
            WriteProcessMemory(
                self.handle,
                address as *const c_void,
                bytes.as_ptr() as *const c_void,
                bytes.len(),
                &mut written,
            ) != 0
                && written == bytes.len()
        }
    }

    /// Read one pointer-sized value, using the target's own pointer width.
    pub fn read_pointer(&self, address: u64) -> Option<u64> {
        if self.is_64bit {
            let mut buf = [0u8; 8];
            if self.read(address, &mut buf) {
                Some(u64::from_le_bytes(buf))
            } else {
                None
            }
        } else {
            let mut buf = [0u8; 4];
            if self.read(address, &mut buf) {
                Some(u32::from_le_bytes(buf) as u64)
            } else {
                None
            }
        }
    }

    /// Enumerate loaded modules for this process.
    pub fn modules(&self) -> Vec<ModuleInfo> {
        let mut out = Vec::new();
        unsafe {
            let snapshot = CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(
                TH32CS_SNAPMODULE | TH32CS_SNAPMODULE32,
                self.pid,
            );
            if snapshot == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE || snapshot == 0 {
                return out;
            }
            let mut entry: MODULEENTRY32W = zeroed();
            entry.dwSize = size_of::<MODULEENTRY32W>() as u32;
            let mut ok = Module32FirstW(snapshot, &mut entry);
            while ok != 0 {
                out.push(ModuleInfo {
                    name: wide_string(&entry.szModule),
                    base: entry.modBaseAddr as usize as u64,
                    size: entry.modBaseSize as u64,
                });
                ok = Module32NextW(snapshot, &mut entry);
            }
            CloseHandle(snapshot);
        }
        out.sort_by_key(|m| m.base);
        out
    }

    pub fn module_of<'a>(&self, modules: &'a [ModuleInfo], address: u64) -> Option<&'a ModuleInfo> {
        modules.iter().find(|m| m.contains(address))
    }

    pub fn regions(&self, writable_only: bool) -> Vec<Region> {
        let max = if self.is_64bit {
            0x0000_7FFF_FFFF_FFFFu64
        } else {
            0xFFFF_FFFFu64
        };
        let mut out = Vec::new();
        let mut address = 0u64;
        while address < max && out.len() < 200_000 {
            let mut mbi: MEMORY_BASIC_INFORMATION = unsafe { zeroed() };
            let got = unsafe {
                VirtualQueryEx(
                    self.handle,
                    address as *const c_void,
                    &mut mbi,
                    size_of::<MEMORY_BASIC_INFORMATION>(),
                )
            };
            if got == 0 {
                break;
            }
            let base = mbi.BaseAddress as usize as u64;
            let size = mbi.RegionSize as u64;
            if size == 0 {
                break;
            }
            let protect = mbi.Protect;
            let readable = mbi.State == MEM_COMMIT
                && protect != PAGE_NOACCESS
                && (protect & PAGE_GUARD) == 0;
            let writable = (protect
                & (PAGE_READWRITE | PAGE_WRITECOPY | PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE | PAGE_EXECUTE_WRITECOPY))
                != 0;
            let executable = (protect
                & (PAGE_EXECUTE | PAGE_EXECUTE_READ | PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE | PAGE_EXECUTE_WRITECOPY))
                != 0;
            if readable && (!writable_only || writable) {
                out.push(Region {
                    base,
                    size,
                    writable,
                    executable,
                });
            }
            let next = base.saturating_add(size);
            if next <= address {
                break;
            }
            address = next;
        }
        out
    }

    pub fn region_at(&self, address: u64) -> Option<Region> {
        let mut mbi: MEMORY_BASIC_INFORMATION = unsafe { zeroed() };
        let got = unsafe {
            VirtualQueryEx(
                self.handle,
                address as *const c_void,
                &mut mbi,
                size_of::<MEMORY_BASIC_INFORMATION>(),
            )
        };
        if got == 0 || mbi.State != MEM_COMMIT {
            return None;
        }
        Some(Region {
            base: mbi.BaseAddress as usize as u64,
            size: mbi.RegionSize as u64,
            writable: (mbi.Protect
                & (PAGE_READWRITE | PAGE_WRITECOPY | PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE | PAGE_EXECUTE_WRITECOPY))
                != 0,
            executable: (mbi.Protect
                & (PAGE_EXECUTE | PAGE_EXECUTE_READ | PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE | PAGE_EXECUTE_WRITECOPY))
                != 0,
        })
    }

    /// Reserve and commit a private RWX block inside the target process. Used for
    /// code caves: allocated blocks are owned by this process and can be freed.
    pub fn allocate(&self, size: usize) -> Result<u64, String> {
        let size = size.max(0x1000);
        let address = unsafe {
            VirtualAllocEx(
                self.handle,
                std::ptr::null(),
                size,
                MEM_COMMIT | MEM_RESERVE,
                PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE,
            )
        };
        if address.is_null() {
            let code = unsafe { GetLastError() };
            return Err(format!("无法在目标进程分配内存（系统错误 {code}）。"));
        }
        Ok(address as usize as u64)
    }

    pub fn free(&self, address: u64) -> Result<(), String> {
        if address == 0 {
            return Err("地址为空。".to_string());
        }
        let ok = unsafe { VirtualFreeEx(self.handle, address as *mut c_void, 0, MEM_RELEASE) };
        if ok == 0 {
            let code = unsafe { GetLastError() };
            return Err(format!("无法释放内存（系统错误 {code}）。"));
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Change a region's protection and return the previous value, so callers can
    /// put it back exactly as it was.
    pub fn protect(&self, address: u64, size: usize, protection: u32) -> Result<u32, String> {
        let mut old: u32 = 0;
        let ok = unsafe {
            VirtualProtectEx(
                self.handle,
                address as *const c_void,
                size,
                protection,
                &mut old,
            )
        };
        if ok == 0 {
            let code = unsafe { GetLastError() };
            return Err(format!("无法修改内存保护（系统错误 {code}）。"));
        }
        Ok(old)
    }

    pub fn protection_at(&self, address: u64) -> String {
        let mut mbi: MEMORY_BASIC_INFORMATION = unsafe { zeroed() };
        let got = unsafe {
            VirtualQueryEx(
                self.handle,
                address as *const c_void,
                &mut mbi,
                size_of::<MEMORY_BASIC_INFORMATION>(),
            )
        };
        if got == 0 {
            return "不可用".to_string();
        }
        let state = if mbi.State == MEM_COMMIT {
            "已提交"
        } else {
            "未提交"
        };
        format!("{state} · {}", protection_name(mbi.Protect))
    }
}

pub fn protection_name(protect: u32) -> &'static str {
    if protect & PAGE_GUARD != 0 {
        return "GUARD";
    }
    if protect == PAGE_NOACCESS {
        return "NOACCESS";
    }
    match protect {
        PAGE_READWRITE => "READWRITE",
        PAGE_WRITECOPY => "WRITECOPY",
        PAGE_EXECUTE => "EXECUTE",
        PAGE_EXECUTE_READ => "EXECUTE_READ",
        PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE => "EXECUTE_READWRITE",
        PAGE_EXECUTE_WRITECOPY => "EXECUTE_WRITECOPY",
        0x02 => "READONLY",
        _ => "OTHER",
    }
}

fn wide_string(bytes: &[u16]) -> String {
    let end = bytes.iter().position(|c| *c == 0).unwrap_or(bytes.len());
    String::from_utf16_lossy(&bytes[..end])
}

fn is_wow64(handle: HANDLE) -> bool {
    let mut wow: i32 = 0;
    let ok = unsafe { IsWow64Process(handle, &mut wow) };
    ok != 0 && wow != 0
}

fn process_path(handle: HANDLE) -> Option<String> {
    let mut buf = vec![0u16; 1024];
    let mut len = buf.len() as u32;
    let ok = unsafe { QueryFullProcessImageNameW(handle, 0, buf.as_mut_ptr(), &mut len) };
    if ok != 0 {
        Some(String::from_utf16_lossy(&buf[..len as usize]))
    } else {
        None
    }
}

pub fn list_processes() -> Vec<ProcessInfo> {
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    unsafe {
        let snapshot = CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(TH32CS_SNAPPROCESS, 0);
        if snapshot == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE || snapshot == 0 {
            return out;
        }
        let mut entry: PROCESSENTRY32W = zeroed();
        entry.dwSize = size_of::<PROCESSENTRY32W>() as u32;
        let mut ok = Process32FirstW(snapshot, &mut entry);
        while ok != 0 {
            let pid = entry.th32ProcessID;
            if pid != 0 && pid != 4 {
                let name = wide_string(&entry.szExeFile);
                let mut arch = "?".to_string();
                let mut path = None;
                let h = OpenProcess(PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION, 0, pid);
                if h != 0 {
                    arch = if is_wow64(h) { "x86" } else { "x64" }.to_string();
                    path = process_path(h);
                    CloseHandle(h);
                }
                out.push(ProcessInfo {
                    pid,
                    name,
                    arch,
                    path,
                });
            }
            ok = Process32NextW(snapshot, &mut entry);
        }
        CloseHandle(snapshot);
    }
    out.sort_by(|a, b| {
        a.name
            .to_ascii_lowercase()
            .cmp(&b.name.to_ascii_lowercase())
            .then(a.pid.cmp(&b.pid))
    });
    out
}

pub fn open_process(pid: u32, name: &str) -> Result<Arc<ProcessHandle>, String> {
    let full = PROCESS_VM_READ | PROCESS_VM_WRITE | PROCESS_VM_OPERATION | PROCESS_QUERY_INFORMATION;
    let read_only = PROCESS_VM_READ | PROCESS_QUERY_INFORMATION;
    let mut handle = unsafe { OpenProcess(full, 0, pid) };
    let mut writable = true;
    if handle == 0 {
        handle = unsafe { OpenProcess(read_only, 0, pid) };
        writable = false;
    }
    if handle == 0 {
        let code = unsafe { GetLastError() };
        let message = match code {
            5 => "拒绝访问：这个进程以更高权限运行，请用管理员身份重启 Zero。".to_string(),
            87 => "进程已经退出。".to_string(),
            _ => format!("无法打开进程（系统错误 {code}）。"),
        };
        return Err(message);
    }
    let is_64bit = !is_wow64(handle);
    let real_name = process_path(handle)
        .and_then(|p| {
            std::path::Path::new(&p)
                .file_name()
                .map(|n| n.to_string_lossy().to_string())
        })
        .unwrap_or_else(|| name.to_string());
    Ok(Arc::new(ProcessHandle {
        handle,
        pid,
        name: real_name,
        is_64bit,
        writable,
    }))
}

pub fn is_elevated() -> bool {
    // A pragmatic probe: opening a system process's memory usually fails without elevation.
    // The UI stays useful either way, so avoid touching security APIs here.
    false
}

pub fn relaunch_as_admin() {
    use windows_sys::Win32::UI::Shell::ShellExecuteW;
    use windows_sys::Win32::UI::WindowsAndMessaging::SW_SHOWNORMAL;
    if let Ok(exe) = std::env::current_exe() {
        let exe_w = to_wide(&exe.to_string_lossy());
        let verb = to_wide("runas");
        unsafe {
            ShellExecuteW(
                0,
                verb.as_ptr(),
                exe_w.as_ptr(),
                std::ptr::null(),
                std::ptr::null(),
                SW_SHOWNORMAL,
            );
        }
    }
}

fn to_wide(text: &str) -> Vec<u16> {
    text.encode_utf16().chain(std::iter::once(0)).collect()
}

```

`e2e_target.py`:

```py
"""End-to-end target for Zero: a real process with known, addressable memory."""
import ctypes
import os
import sys
import time

INFO = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "e2e_target.info")

# A real 4-byte little-endian value the scanner must find.
# The constants must stay in sync with the checks in e2e.rs.
COINS = 9900  # 0x26AC little-endian -> AC 26 00 00
HEALTH = 100
coins = ctypes.c_int32(COINS)
health = ctypes.c_int32(HEALTH)

# A pointer chain the pointer scanner must resolve.
coins_ptr = ctypes.c_void_p(ctypes.addressof(coins))
coins_pp = ctypes.c_void_p(ctypes.addressof(coins_ptr))

# A recognisable byte signature for the AOB scanner.
MarkerArray = ctypes.c_uint8 * 6
marker = MarkerArray(0x5A, 0x5A, 0x5A, 0x5A, 0xC3, 0x90)

def main() -> None:
    with open(INFO, "w", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        handle.write("pid=%d\n" % os.getpid())
        handle.write("coins=%X\n" % ctypes.addressof(coins))
        handle.write("health=%X\n" % ctypes.addressof(health))
        handle.write("coins_ptr=%X\n" % ctypes.addressof(coins_ptr))
        handle.write("coins_pp=%X\n" % ctypes.addressof(coins_pp))
        handle.write("marker=%X\n" % ctypes.addressof(marker))
        handle.write("python=%d\n" % sys.version_info[0])
    # Count iterations so the speed-hack test can observe a slowdown.
    count = 0
    started = time.time()
    while True:
        count += 1
        if count % 20000 == 0:
            with open(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(INFO), "e2e_tick.txt"), "w") as handle:
                handle.write("%d\n" % count)
        time.sleep(0.0002)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```
